+MIRROR V2 HANDOFF
+=================
+
+Context
+-------
+
+This folder is a proposal/snapshot only:
+
+    android-apps/app_realizer/2/
+
+The original snapshot remains intentionally untouched:
+
+    android-apps/app_realizer/1/main.kt
+
+The branch/PR contains `/2` as the suggested next version. The user may manually
+copy/paste files instead of merging the PR.
+
+PR:
+
+    https://github.com/HalitZiyaKARTAL/test19/pull/1
+
+Primary files in /2
+-------------------
+
+    main.kt
+    manifest1.xml
+    accessibilityconfig.xml
+    accessibility_config.xml
+    strings.xml
+    MIRROR_JUMPSTART.txt
+    MIRROR_PARTS_CHEATSHEET.txt
+    HANDOFF.txt
+
+Main architectural intent
+-------------------------
+
+The app should be an install-once Android runtime/capability kernel. The real app
+logic lives in runtime HTML/JS/AI. Kotlin/native should expose stable phone powers
+through the JavaScript bridge object:
+
+    mirror
+
+Do not use old `K`; no legacy compatibility is desired.
+
+The desired mental model:
+
+    Kotlin/native = capability kernel / syscall layer
+    WebView HTML/JS = app/userland/AI logic
+    Android/Chrome/root/ADB/shell/USB/media/sensors = optional power backends
+
+The user specifically wants logical condensation/unification, not minified one-letter
+commands. Prefer semantic unified transactions:
+
+    mirror.exec(JSON.stringify({
+      observe: ['screen', 'events', 'notifications'],
+      act: [ { tap:[100,200] }, { say:'done' } ],
+      remember: { file:'profile.json.gz', value:'...', compress:true },
+      schedule: { in:3600000, payload:'drink water' },
+      returnEvents: true
+    }))
+
+Policy belongs to HTML, not Kotlin. Native should provide powers and paths, including
+powerful/unsafe paths if the user intentionally grants them. Examples:
+
+- custom headers including Cookie/Authorization
+- root/su
+- adb shell
+- USB manager and future USB bulk primitives
+- dynamic dex/native library loading
+- reflection
+- MediaProjection
+- accessibility automation
+
+Current v2 capabilities
+-----------------------
+
+The current `/2/main.kt` proposal includes broad raw connections:
+
+1. Primary bridge
+   - mirror.exec(json)
+   - mirror.execAsync(json, cbId)
+   - mirror.caps()
+   - mirror.events(json)
+
+2. Boot document
+   - simple fallback shape:
+       var editable: String? = null
+       var html = editable ?: """<!DOCTYPE html>..."""
+   - mirror.boot(html, reload)
+   - mirror.bootGet()
+   - mirror.bootReset(reload)
+
+3. WebView/pages
+   - multiple WebViews/pages/tabs
+   - pageOpen/pageEval/pageShow/pageClose/pageList
+   - WebView camera/mic/geolocation permission grant
+   - file/content access, media playback, mixed content, debugging, multiple windows
+
+4. Reflection/extension
+   - Java reflection with declared/private/inherited member scanning where allowed
+   - field/method/constructor caches
+   - Kotlin KClass handles/info when kotlin-reflect/full metadata is available
+   - classloader handle
+   - DexClassLoader loading
+   - native library loading
+
+5. Accessibility/app playing
+   - screen tree JSON
+   - event queue
+   - tap/swipe/gestures
+   - click/longClick/focus/input by query
+   - waitFor/playClick/currentApp
+   - can play Chrome or other apps as a user surface
+
+6. Chrome/browser strategy
+   - internal WebView for controllable programmable pages
+   - real Chrome can be launched/opened/played when Google/session/site compatibility matters
+   - openIn('com.android.chrome', url)
+   - no attempt to steal Chrome session; use Chrome naturally through UI automation if desired
+
+7. Native fetch/network
+   - mirror.fetch(url, method, headersJson, body, timeout, cleanTracking, sensitiveUnused, maxBytes)
+   - native HTTP avoids normal browser CORS
+   - HTML controls all headers; Cookie/Authorization are not blocked
+   - optional tracking query cleanup
+
+8. Shell/process/root/ADB
+   - one-shot sh
+   - persistent process sessions through exec process start/write/read/kill
+   - sys(mode, cmd, input, timeout) supports sh, su/root, adb, or custom prefix
+   - app cannot grant root/ADB, but is open to use them when available
+
+9. USB direction
+   - usb() returns UsbManager handle
+   - usbList() lists devices
+   - full Bugjaeger-like HTML still needs USB permission/bulk endpoint/ADB protocol helpers or HTML/dex implementation
+
+10. Media/video/screen/frame data
+   - mp(cbId) asks MediaProjection permission
+   - mpStart(w,h,dpi)
+   - mpFrame/mpFrameEx crop/downscale frame
+   - formats: jpg, png, gray, rgba/raw, filejpg/filepng/filegray/fileraw
+   - mpStreamStart callback frame stream
+   - framePipeStart/framePipeInfo/framePipeStop ring-buffer data plane for real-time use
+   - frame pipe writes latest frames to rotating cache files plus meta.json
+   - intended for WebGPU/WASM/JS vision loops and screen/video sharing transports
+
+11. Video chat / screen share
+   - native does not implement WebRTC library
+   - WebView gets getUserMedia permissions
+   - HTML implements RTCPeerConnection/signaling/UI
+   - mirror provides native fetch for signaling if needed
+   - mirror provides MediaProjection frame pipe for screen share / remote help
+
+12. Sensors/speech/notifications
+   - sensor(type, delay, cbId), sensorStop
+   - heart rate path via sensor type 21 if hardware/permission support exists
+   - say via TTS
+   - listen via SpeechRecognizer
+   - notify
+   - notification listener queue
+
+13. Storage/profile
+   - save/load/rm/keys small SharedPreferences storage
+   - fw/fr file storage, optionally gzip/base64
+   - zip/unzip helpers
+   - intended for profiles, timers, action logs, learning memory
+
+14. Scheduling/lifetime
+   - alarm(ms, payload)
+   - foreground BridgeService fg(true/false)
+   - lastIntent payload
+   - MainReceiver route back into Main
+
+15. Self-modification / performance shedding
+   - boot HTML can be replaced/reset
+   - close pages
+   - stop frame pipes
+   - stop MediaProjection/streams
+   - stop sensors
+   - kill processes
+   - turn foreground service off
+
+Important user clarifications already resolved
+---------------------------------------------
+
+- `/1` must stay unchanged. Work/suggestions go under `/2`.
+- No old `K` bridge compatibility. Use `mirror` only.
+- Default HTML should stay minimal; do not replace it with a heavy console.
+- The default HTML should be an open fallback literal, roughly:
+      html = editable ?: """<!DOCTYPE html>...full fallback code..."""
+- Logical condensation means semantic unification, not one-letter minification.
+- “resolve intents” meant understand the user's intent, not Android Intent only.
+- HTML should decide policy and power usage.
+- Do not block sensitive headers; app/user/HTML decides.
+- WebRTC should mostly be HTML; native gives permissions and screen/frame/data powers.
+- Chrome should be playable as an external app surface, especially for Google account/session flows, not internally copied/stolen.
+- Root/ADB should be optional power backends: the app can use them if the user/device grants/provides them.
+
+Docs already added
+------------------
+
+MIRROR_JUMPSTART.txt is the long conceptual guide and examples.
+MIRROR_PARTS_CHEATSHEET.txt is the practical method index.
+This HANDOFF.txt is the continuity summary.
+
+Read these before continuing:
+
+    android-apps/app_realizer/2/MIRROR_JUMPSTART.txt
+    android-apps/app_realizer/2/MIRROR_PARTS_CHEATSHEET.txt
+    android-apps/app_realizer/2/HANDOFF.txt
+
+Known rough areas / next best improvements
+------------------------------------------
+
+The current v2 is powerful but still rough. The next useful work is unification and
+ergonomics, not just adding random wrappers.
+
+Highest-value next tasks:
+
+1. Runtime manager
+   Add something like:
+
+       mirror.exec({ runtime:{ stats:true } })
+       mirror.exec({ runtime:{ stop:['framePipes','sensors','processes'], closePages:['vision'] } })
+
+   It should report pages, processes, sensors, frame pipes, projection state,
+   foreground state, handles, memory-ish stats, event queue sizes.
+
+2. Backend auto-routing
+   Actions should optionally choose backend:
+
+       { tap:[100,200], backend:'auto' }
+
+   Auto should try configured root/adb/accessibility routes depending available powers.
+   This turns root/ADB from raw shell access into a practical input backend.
+
+3. Unified event bus
+   Normalize all events into one event stream with ids/cursors:
+
+       { id, time, source:'sensor|speech|notification|accessibility|process|frame|intent', type, data }
+
+   Then mirror.events({ since, sources, max }) can be canonical.
+
+4. Permission/power negotiation
+   Add:
+
+       mirror.exec({ require:['accessibility','mediaProjection','microphone','notifications','bodySensors','root?','adb?'] })
+
+   Return missing capabilities and fix paths/settings actions/calls.
+
+5. File/binary primitives
+   Add binary-safe fileRead/fileWrite/fileList/fileInfo/fileDelete.
+   Needed for models, WASM, frame slots, ADB payloads, resources.
+
+6. USB bulk primitives
+   For Bugjaeger-like HTML:
+       usbRequest, usbOpen, usbBulkRead, usbBulkWrite, usbClose
+
+7. Frame pipe robustness
+   Atomic meta writes, dropped frame count, average FPS, latest seq, cleanup old files,
+   stride/format details for raw/gray, optional fixed names.
+
+8. Better structured exec return values
+   `exec` should normalize old OK/E/V/P results into structured JSON objects.
+
+9. Optional JS SDK string
+   Do not bloat default UI, but consider an editable/stored SDK string retrievable by:
+       mirror.sdkGet(), mirror.sdkSet(...)
+   HTML can opt into it.
+
+10. Page roles and message bus
+   Pages should have roles: main, worker, vision, remote, tool.
+   Add page metadata and page-to-page message/event support.
+
+Readiness assessment
+--------------------
+
+As of this handoff:
+
+- Raw capability coverage: strong (~8.5/10)
+- Ease/comfort for HTML authors: moderate (~6/10)
+- Real-time ML/game loops: improved with frame pipe, but needs robustness (~7/10)
+- Universal platform polish: not done (~6/10)
+
+The code gives HTML paths to almost all requested domains, but needs better unified
+runtime management and structured event/permission systems to feel truly comfortable.
+
+Potential risk/compile notes
+----------------------------
+
+This repository sandbox did not have an Android SDK/Gradle/emulator available, so the
+v2 source was not compiled here. Treat it as a source-level proposal that should be
+checked inside the real Android project.
+
+Likely things to verify in a real build:
+
+- compileSdk high enough for newer permissions/foreground service types
+- MediaProjection APIs and foreground-service requirements on target Android version
+- Accessibility screenshot API requires Android 11+
+- WebView WebGPU support depends on device/System WebView
+- POST_NOTIFICATIONS runtime permission on Android 13+
+- BODY_SENSORS runtime permission for heart rate
+- exact alarm policy/settings on newer Android
+- notification listener service requires user enablement
+- accessibility service requires user enablement
+- USB host permissions need additional flow for opening devices
+
+Branch/PR status
+----------------
+
+Work was committed and pushed to branch:
+
+    arena/019fbe59-test19
+
+PR opened:
+
+    https://github.com/HalitZiyaKARTAL/test19/pull/1
+
+The user may ignore the PR and manually copy files from `/2` if preferred.
+
+Closing note
+------------
+
+If another agent continues later, remember the core design principle:
+
+    Do not turn Kotlin into the app.
+    Keep Kotlin as mirror: a unified Android power kernel.
+    Let runtime HTML/JS/AI be the self-modifying app brain.

+MIRROR APP REALIZER V2 — JUMPSTART DESIGN NOTES
+=================================================
+
+This document describes the proposed `android-apps/app_realizer/2` snapshot.
+`/1` is intentionally left unchanged. `/2` is a suggestion/prototype for a
+more universal Android runtime kernel where HTML/JS is the app layer and the
+native Kotlin side is only the phone capability bridge.
+
+Core idea
+---------
+
+Install the Android app once. After that, pasted or loaded HTML can become many
+kinds of apps without changing the APK:
+
+- browser-like runtime
+- AI automation tool
+- app tester
+- video chat / remote help surface
+- phone controlled from PC
+- local WebGPU/WASM model runner
+- game/app vision/action loop
+- reminder/profile/scheduler
+- speech assistant
+- Termux-like shell/process bridge
+- root/ADB powered tool if those powers are granted
+- USB/ADB/Bugjaeger-like tool if HTML or loaded dex implements the protocol
+
+The bridge object exposed to JavaScript is:
+
+    mirror
+
+The intended primary interface is unified/logically-condensed:
+
+    mirror.exec(JSON.stringify({...}))
+    mirror.execAsync(JSON.stringify({...}), callbackId)
+    mirror.caps()
+    mirror.events(JSON.stringify({...}))
+
+Older/direct helper methods exist too, but future HTML should prefer `mirror.exec`
+plus a small JS SDK wrapper.
+
+Mental model
+------------
+
+Think of the app as:
+
+    Kotlin/native = capability kernel / syscall surface
+    WebView HTML/JS = userland app logic
+    optional AI/PC = planner/controller
+    Android/Chrome/root/ADB/shell/USB/sensors/media = power backends
+
+Do not put policy in Kotlin. Let HTML decide policy:
+
+- whether to use WebView, Chrome, shell, root, adb, accessibility, or USB
+- whether to send cookies/authorization/custom headers
+- whether to strip tracking parameters
+- whether to use MediaProjection frames or accessibility tree
+- whether to stop expensive frame loops
+- whether to run a hidden page, WebGPU worker, WASM worker, or process
+
+Kotlin provides broad, stable paths. HTML composes behavior.
+
+Default boot document
+---------------------
+
+The default HTML remains a minimal editor/realizer. In `/2/main.kt` it is shaped
+like this:
+
+    var editable: String? = null
+    var html = editable ?: """<!DOCTYPE html>...full fallback editor..."""
+    fun bootHtml() = html
+
+On startup, local saved override is loaded into `editable`, then `html` becomes:
+
+    html = editable ?: """fallback document literal"""
+
+So the fallback is open in the declaration. Runtime HTML/reflection can set the
+string if desired. The helper methods are:
+
+    mirror.boot(html, reload)
+    mirror.bootGet()
+    mirror.bootReset(reload)
+
+Minimal JS SDK suggestion
+-------------------------
+
+Paste this into any runtime HTML as a convenience layer:
+
+    const phone = {
+      raw: mirror,
+      exec(o) {
+        return JSON.parse(mirror.exec(JSON.stringify(o)));
+      },
+      execAsync(o, cbId) {
+        return mirror.execAsync(JSON.stringify(o), cbId);
+      },
+      caps() {
+        const r = mirror.caps();
+        return r.startsWith('V') ? JSON.parse(r.slice(1)) : { error: r };
+      },
+      events(max = 100) {
+        const r = mirror.events(JSON.stringify({ max }));
+        return r.startsWith('V') ? JSON.parse(r.slice(1)) : { error: r };
+      },
+      observe(...what) {
+        return this.exec({ observe: what.flat(), returnEvents: true });
+      },
+      act(...actions) {
+        return this.exec({ act: actions.flat() });
+      },
+      remember(file, value, compress = true) {
+        return this.exec({ remember: { file, value, compress } });
+      },
+      schedule(ms, payload) {
+        return this.exec({ schedule: { in: ms, payload } });
+      },
+      page(id, src, kind = 'html', visible = true) {
+        return this.exec({ page: { id, src, kind, visible } });
+      },
+      framePipe(config, cb = 0) {
+        const r = mirror.framePipeStart(JSON.stringify(config), cb);
+        return r.startsWith('V') ? JSON.parse(r.slice(1)) : { error: r };
+      },
+      mpFrame(config) {
+        const r = mirror.mpFrameEx(
+          config.l ?? -1, config.t ?? -1, config.r ?? -1, config.b ?? -1,
+          config.maxW ?? 320, config.quality ?? 60, config.format ?? 'jpg'
+        );
+        return r.startsWith('V') ? JSON.parse(r.slice(1)) : { error: r };
+      },
+      shell(cmd, timeout = 10000) {
+        const r = mirror.sh(cmd, '', timeout);
+        return r.startsWith('V') ? JSON.parse(r.slice(1)) : { error: r };
+      },
+      sys(mode, cmd, timeout = 10000) {
+        const r = mirror.sys(mode, cmd, '', timeout);
+        return r.startsWith('V') ? JSON.parse(r.slice(1)) : { error: r };
+      }
+    };
+
+Unified exec examples
+---------------------
+
+Observe screen/events/notifications/capabilities:
+
+    const state = phone.exec({
+      capabilities: true,
+      observe: ['screen', 'events', 'notifications', 'intent'],
+      returnEvents: true
+    });
+
+Act on the phone:
+
+    phone.exec({
+      act: [
+        { tap: [540, 1200] },
+        { click: 'OK' },
+        { input: ['Search', 'hello world'] },
+        { say: 'Done' },
+        { notify: [1, 'Mirror', 'Action complete'] }
+      ]
+    });
+
+Remember profile:
+
+    phone.exec({
+      remember: {
+        file: 'profile.json.gz',
+        value: JSON.stringify({ goals: ['hydrate', 'sleep earlier'] }),
+        compress: true
+      }
+    });
+
+Schedule reminder:
+
+    phone.exec({
+      schedule: {
+        in: 3600000,
+        payload: 'drink water'
+      }
+    });
+
+Run a shell command:
+
+    phone.exec({
+      act: [
+        { shell: 'id; uname -a', timeout: 5000 }
+      ]
+    });
+
+Use optional root or adb power if available/granted:
+
+    phone.exec({
+      act: [
+        { sys: { mode: 'su', cmd: 'id', timeout: 5000 } },
+        { sys: { mode: 'adb', cmd: 'settings list global', timeout: 5000 } }
+      ]
+    });
+
+Multiple pages / tabs / workers
+-------------------------------
+
+`/2` adds multiple WebView/page support. This allows:
+
+- visible UI page
+- hidden vision/model worker page
+- remote bridge page
+- tool page
+- experimentation pages
+
+Open a visible page:
+
+    mirror.pageOpen('ui', '<!doctype html><h1>Hello</h1>', 'html', true);
+
+Open a hidden worker/model page:
+
+    mirror.pageOpen('vision', '<!doctype html><script>function run(){return 1}</script>', 'html', false);
+
+Evaluate JS inside a page:
+
+    window.onC = (id, msg) => console.log('callback', id, msg);
+    mirror.pageEval('vision', 'run()', 7);
+
+Switch visible page:
+
+    mirror.pageShow('ui');
+
+Close a page:
+
+    mirror.pageClose('vision');
+
+List pages:
+
+    const pages = JSON.parse(mirror.pageList().slice(1));
+
+Video chat shape
+----------------
+
+Native does not implement WebRTC itself. That should be HTML's job. Native gives
+HTML the powers it needs:
+
+- WebView camera/mic permission grant
+- geolocation permission grant
+- media playback without gesture
+- multiple windows
+- foreground service path
+- MediaProjection consent and frame pipe for screen sharing
+- native fetch for signaling if browser fetch/CORS is a problem
+
+Simple video chat HTML skeleton:
+
+    <!doctype html>
+    <video id="local" autoplay playsinline muted></video>
+    <video id="remote" autoplay playsinline></video>
+    <textarea id="signal"></textarea>
+    <button onclick="start()">start</button>
+    <script>
+    let pc, dc;
+    async function start() {
+      const stream = await navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({video:true, audio:true});
+      local.srcObject = stream;
+      pc = new RTCPeerConnection({iceServers:[{urls:'stun:stun.l.google.com:19302'}]});
+      stream.getTracks().forEach(t => pc.addTrack(t, stream));
+      pc.ontrack = e => remote.srcObject = e.streams[0];
+      dc = pc.createDataChannel('mirror');
+      dc.onmessage = e => {
+        // Optional: receive remote command packet and execute on phone.
+        try { dc.send(mirror.exec(e.data)); } catch (err) { dc.send(JSON.stringify({ok:false,error:String(err)})); }
+      };
+      pc.onicecandidate = () => signal.value = JSON.stringify(pc.localDescription);
+      const offer = await pc.createOffer();
+      await pc.setLocalDescription(offer);
+    }
+    async function accept(answerJson) {
+      await pc.setRemoteDescription(JSON.parse(answerJson));
+    }
+    </script>
+
+Screen-share / remote-help shape
+--------------------------------
+
+Use HTML WebRTC/DataChannel/WebSocket/etc. for transport. Use `mirror` only as
+power provider.
+
+1. Ask for screen-capture permission:
+
+    mirror.mp(10);
+
+2. After callback/user consent:
+
+    mirror.mpStart(0, 0, 0);
+
+3. Start efficient frame pipe:
+
+    const pipe = JSON.parse(mirror.framePipeStart(JSON.stringify({
+      l: 0, t: 0, r: 720, b: 1280,
+      maxW: 360,
+      quality: 55,
+      format: 'jpg',
+      fps: 15,
+      slots: 3
+    }), 0).slice(1));
+
+4. HTML sends latest frame through chosen transport:
+
+    async function sendLoop() {
+      const metaRes = await fetch(pipe.meta);
+      const meta = await metaRes.json();
+      const frameBuf = await fetch(meta.url).then(r => r.arrayBuffer());
+      // send frameBuf through WebRTC DataChannel, WebSocket, WebTransport, etc.
+      requestAnimationFrame(sendLoop);
+    }
+
+The app gives power to use WebRTC; HTML implements the networking/product logic.
+
+AI/game vision loop shape
+-------------------------
+
+For a local WebGPU/WASM model that watches a small window and acts:
+
+    mirror.mp(10);       // user grants once
+    mirror.mpStart(0,0,0);
+
+    const pipe = phone.framePipe({
+      l: 100, t: 200, r: 500, b: 600,
+      maxW: 128,
+      format: 'gray',
+      fps: 30,
+      slots: 3
+    });
+
+    async function loop() {
+      const meta = await fetch(pipe.meta).then(r => r.json());
+      const gray = await fetch(meta.url).then(r => r.arrayBuffer());
+
+      // Feed gray bytes to JS/WASM/WebGPU model.
+      const action = await model.decide(gray, meta.w, meta.h);
+
+      // Execute selected action.
+      phone.act(action);
+
+      // Learn from feedback/events.
+      const feedback = phone.observe('events', 'screen');
+      model.learn(feedback);
+
+      requestAnimationFrame(loop);
+    }
+
+If the game/content is inside the same WebView, do not use mirror for visual data.
+Read the canvas/video directly in JS for maximum speed. Use mirror only for phone
+I/O, memory, fetch, shell, etc.
+
+Chrome/app playing shape
+------------------------
+
+If a site works better in Chrome or has an existing login/session, use real Chrome
+as a surface and let mirror be eyes/hands:
+
+    phone.exec({ act: [
+      { openIn: { pkg: 'com.android.chrome', uri: 'https://accounts.google.com' } },
+      { waitFor: { q: 'Google', ms: 5000 } }
+    ]});
+
+Then use:
+
+- accessibility tree (`observe: ['screen']`)
+- frame pipe (`mpFrame`/`framePipe`)
+- click/type/swipe actions
+- learning logs
+
+This does not steal Chrome data. It drives Chrome as the user would, using whatever
+state Chrome already has.
+
+Native fetch / CORS / headers
+-----------------------------
+
+`mirror.fetch` performs native HTTP outside browser CORS. HTML controls headers.
+No special filtering is imposed by native policy.
+
+    const r = mirror.fetch(
+      'https://example.com/api?utm_source=x',
+      'POST',
+      JSON.stringify({
+        'User-Agent': 'mirror-runtime',
+        'Authorization': 'Bearer ...',
+        'Cookie': '...',
+        'Origin': 'https://desired-origin.example',
+        'Referer': 'https://desired-referer.example'
+      }),
+      JSON.stringify({hello:'world'}),
+      15000,
+      true,   // clean tracking params if desired
+      true,   // retained arg; native does not block sensitive headers
+      10_000_000
+    );
+
+Use this for signaling, model/resource downloads, API access, proxy-like behavior,
+or pages where browser CORS is a blocker.
+
+Shell/process/root/ADB shape
+----------------------------
+
+One-shot shell:
+
+    const r = phone.shell('id; ls -la /sdcard', 5000);
+
+Persistent process:
+
+    let start = phone.exec({ process: { start: 'sh', cwd: '/sdcard' } });
+    // Suppose it returns process id 1.
+    phone.exec({ process: { id: 1, write: 'echo hi\n' } });
+    let out = phone.exec({ process: { read: 1, clear: true } });
+    phone.exec({ process: { kill: 1 } });
+
+Optional root/ADB power:
+
+    phone.sys('su', 'id; input tap 100 200', 5000);
+    phone.sys('adb', 'shell input tap 100 200', 5000);
+
+The app cannot grant root/ADB. It is open to using those backends when available.
+
+USB / Bugjaeger-like direction
+------------------------------
+
+Current proposal includes:
+
+    mirror.usb()
+    mirror.usbList()
+
+`mirror.usb()` returns a UsbManager handle for reflection/dex use. `usbList()` gives
+a JSON summary of connected USB devices. A full Bugjaeger-like tool would still need
+HTML/WASM/dex code for USB permission, endpoint claiming, and ADB protocol. The
+kernel is open to that direction but not a full ADB client by itself.
+
+Self-modification / removing lag
+--------------------------------
+
+HTML can dynamically reduce load:
+
+- stop frame pipe: `mirror.framePipeStop(id)` or `mirror.framePipeStop(0)`
+- stop stream: `mirror.mpStreamStop()`
+- stop projection: `mirror.mpStop()`
+- stop sensor: `mirror.sensorStop(id)`
+- close page: `mirror.pageClose(id)`
+- kill process: `mirror.exec({process:{kill:id}})`
+- change boot document: `mirror.boot(html, true)`
+- reset boot document: `mirror.bootReset(true)`
+
+A future cleanup pass should add a single runtime manager:
+
+    mirror.exec({ runtime: { stats: true } })
+    mirror.exec({ runtime: { stop: ['framePipes','sensors','processes'] } })
+
+Important current gaps
+----------------------
+
+The `/2` proposal is powerful but still rough. Remaining high-value improvements:
+
+1. Unified runtime manager for stats/stop/profile.
+2. Unified event bus with cursors for all events.
+3. Backend auto-routing for actions: accessibility/root/adb.
+4. Runtime permission/fix negotiation.
+5. Binary file primitives for models/frame data/USB/ADB.
+6. USB bulk read/write primitives for true Bugjaeger-like HTML.
+7. Better structured JSON return format for `exec` results.
+8. Better page metadata/roles for worker/UI/remote/model pages.
+9. Frame pipe robustness: atomic metadata, cleanup, dropped frame counts, FPS stats.
+10. Optional JS SDK string stored/editable like boot document.
+
+Recommended HTML strategy
+-------------------------
+
+- Put intelligence/policy in HTML.
+- Use `mirror.exec` for small command/control transactions.
+- Use frame pipes or files for high-rate data.
+- Use WebView-native canvas/WebGPU directly when content is inside WebView.
+- Use Chrome/app automation when external app/browser compatibility is needed.
+- Use root/ADB/shell only when user/device grants that power.
+- Keep model/profile/action logs compressed in files.
+- Close pages, sensors, processes, and frame pipes when not needed.
+
+The intended final feeling is:
+
+    mirror = phone power kernel
+    HTML = self-modifying app/AI/runtime
+    Android/Chrome/root/ADB/shell/USB/media/sensors = optional backends

new file mode 100644

+MIRROR PARTS CHEATSHEET
+=======================
+
+This is a practical index for the `/2` proposal. Use `mirror.*` from JavaScript.
+Most methods return old compact strings:
+
+    OK          success/no value
+    E:...       error
+    V...        primitive/string JSON value follows after V
+    P<number>   native object handle
+
+The newer preferred API `mirror.exec(...)` returns JSON directly.
+
+Primary unified API
+-------------------
+
+mirror.exec(jsonString)
+    Runs a unified transaction.
+
+    Example:
+        mirror.exec(JSON.stringify({
+          capabilities: true,
+          observe: ['screen','events','notifications'],
+          act: [ { tap:[100,200] }, { say:'done' } ],
+          returnEvents: true
+        }))
+
+mirror.execAsync(jsonString, cbId)
+    Runs exec on a background thread and calls window.onC(cbId, result).
+
+mirror.caps()
+    Returns V + capability JSON.
+
+mirror.events(jsonString)
+    Returns V + event state JSON.
+
+mirror.run(text), mirror.e(text), mirror.r(text), mirror.flow(text)
+    Human-readable flow commands. Example:
+        mirror.run(`
+        launch com.android.chrome
+        waitFor Google
+        playClick Search
+        `)
+
+Boot document
+-------------
+
+mirror.boot(html, reload)
+    Sets editable boot HTML. Persists in local preferences. If reload=true, loads it.
+
+mirror.bootGet()
+    Returns V + current boot HTML.
+
+mirror.bootReset(reload)
+    Clears local override and resets to fallback literal.
+
+Java/Kotlin reflection
+----------------------
+
+mirror.c(className)
+    Class.forName / primitive/array aware. Returns P handle.
+
+mirror.cl()
+    Returns app classloader handle.
+
+mirror.c2(loaderHandle, className)
+    Load class through a classloader handle.
+
+mirror.n(className, jsonArgs)
+    Construct object.
+
+mirror.x(handle, methodName, jsonArgs)
+    Invoke method.
+
+mirror.u(handle, methodName, jsonArgs, cbId)
+    Invoke and callback.
+
+mirror.g(handle, field)
+    Get field.
+
+mirror.s(handle, field, jsonValue)
+    Set field.
+
+mirror.a(typeName, length)
+    New array.
+
+mirror.p(interfaceName, callbackId)
+    Create Java proxy for interface; callbacks go to window.onL.
+
+mirror.kc(className), mirror.kco(handle), mirror.kinfo(handle), mirror.jinfo(handle)
+    Kotlin/Java class handles and info.
+
+mirror.type(handle), mirror.get(handle), mirror.str(value), mirror.del(handle)
+    Handle helpers.
+
+Pages / tabs / workers
+----------------------
+
+mirror.pageOpen(id, src, kind, visible)
+    kind: html, url, base64.
+
+mirror.pageEval(id, js, cbId)
+    Evaluate JS in page, callback result.
+
+mirror.pageShow(id)
+    Show a page and hide others.
+
+mirror.pageClose(id)
+    Destroy page.
+
+mirror.pageList()
+    Returns V + JSON array of page ids.
+
+Example:
+    mirror.pageOpen('worker', '<script>function f(){return 5}</script>', 'html', false)
+    mirror.pageEval('worker', 'f()', 8)
+
+Accessibility / app playing
+---------------------------
+
+mirror.svc()
+    Returns AccessibilityService handle if enabled.
+
+mirror.currentApp()
+    Returns current active package from accessibility root.
+
+mirror.waitFor(query, ms)
+    Wait until a screen node contains query.
+
+mirror.playClick(query, ms)
+    Wait then click node containing query.
+
+Via service reflection or exec actions:
+    tap [x,y]
+    swipe [x1,y1,x2,y2,ms]
+    click query
+    input [query,text]
+    back/home/recents/notifications
+
+Example:
+    mirror.exec(JSON.stringify({act:[
+      {launch:'com.android.chrome'},
+      {waitFor:{q:'Google',ms:5000}},
+      {playClick:{q:'Search',ms:5000}}
+    ]}))
+
+Intents / browser / Chrome
+--------------------------
+
+mirror.open(uri)
+    ACTION_VIEW uri.
+
+mirror.openIn(pkg, uri)
+    Open URI targeted to package, e.g. Chrome.
+
+mirror.launch(packageName)
+    Launch app package.
+
+mirror.share(text, mime)
+    Android share sheet.
+
+mirror.choose(action, uri, mime, extrasJson)
+    Android chooser.
+
+mirror.resolveIntent(action, uri, mime, pkg, cls, extras, cats, flags)
+    Returns matching activities.
+
+mirror.canIntent(...)
+    Returns whether intent can resolve.
+
+mirror.startEx(action, uri, mime, pkg, cls, extras, cats, flags)
+    Full intent start.
+
+Native fetch / CORS helper
+--------------------------
+
+mirror.fetch(url, method, headersJson, body, timeoutMs, cleanTracking, sensitiveUnused, maxBytes)
+    Returns V + JSON:
+        { url, code, type, headers, text, base64 }
+
+Headers are controlled by HTML. Cookie/Authorization are not blocked by native.
+
+Example:
+    mirror.fetch('https://api.example.com','GET','{"Authorization":"Bearer x"}','',15000,true,true,2000000)
+
+Shell / process / root / adb
+----------------------------
+
+mirror.sh(cmd, input, timeoutMs)
+    One-shot shell as app UID.
+
+mirror.sys(mode, cmd, input, timeoutMs)
+    mode: sh, su/root, adb, or custom prefix.
+
+Unified process sessions through exec:
+    mirror.exec({process:{start:'sh',cwd:'/sdcard'}})
+    mirror.exec({process:{id:1,write:'ls\n'}})
+    mirror.exec({process:{read:1,clear:true}})
+    mirror.exec({process:{kill:1}})
+
+MediaProjection / frames
+------------------------
+
+mirror.mp(cbId)
+    Request screen-capture consent. Callback gets result/data handle.
+
+mirror.mpStart(w,h,dpi)
+    Start MediaProjection after consent. Use 0,0,0 for display defaults.
+
+mirror.mpFrame(l,t,r,b,maxW,quality)
+    Old JPEG crop base64.
+
+mirror.mpFrameEx(l,t,r,b,maxW,quality,format)
+    Formats:
+        jpg, png, gray, rgba, raw, filejpg, filepng, filegray, fileraw
+
+mirror.mpStreamStart(configJson, cbId)
+    Push frame payloads to window.onC callback.
+
+mirror.mpStreamStop()
+    Stop callback stream.
+
+mirror.framePipeStart(configJson, cbId)
+    Start ring-buffer frame data plane. Config:
+        {l,t,r,b,maxW,quality,format,fps,slots}
+
+mirror.framePipeInfo(id)
+    Returns V + latest meta JSON.
+
+mirror.framePipeStop(id)
+    Stop one pipe. id=0 stops all.
+
+mirror.mpStop()
+    Stop stream/projection/resources.
+
+Recommended for ML:
+    format='gray', maxW small, fps 15-30, slots 3.
+
+Recommended for remote help/video share:
+    format='jpg' or 'filejpg', maxW 360-720, fps 10-20.
+
+Accessibility screenshot
+------------------------
+
+mirror.screenshot(l,t,r,b,maxW,quality)
+    Uses AccessibilityService screenshot API on Android 11+.
+    Useful for occasional screenshots if MediaProjection is not active.
+
+Sensors / speech / notifications
+--------------------------------
+
+mirror.sensor(type, delay, cbId)
+    Register Android sensor. Callback receives JSON values.
+    Heart rate is usually type 21 if supported and permission granted.
+
+mirror.sensorStop(id)
+    Stop sensor.
+
+mirror.say(text)
+    TextToSpeech.
+
+mirror.listen(cbId, lang, prompt)
+    SpeechRecognizer one-shot listen.
+
+mirror.stopListen()
+    Stop speech recognition.
+
+mirror.notify(id, title, text)
+    Post notification.
+
+mirror.notis(max)
+    Notification listener event queue.
+
+Storage / profile
+-----------------
+
+mirror.save(key, value, zip)
+    SharedPreferences small storage.
+
+mirror.load(key, zip)
+    Load small storage.
+
+mirror.rm(key)
+    Remove.
+
+mirror.keys(prefix)
+    List keys.
+
+mirror.zip(text), mirror.unzip(base64Gzip)
+    GZIP/Base64 helpers.
+
+mirror.fw(name, value, zip)
+    Write app file text, optionally gzipped.
+
+mirror.fr(name, zip)
+    Read app file text, optionally gunzipped.
+
+Clipboard / vibration / time
+----------------------------
+
+mirror.clip(text)
+mirror.getClip()
+mirror.vibe(ms)
+mirror.toast(text)
+mirror.now()
+mirror.later(ms, cbId, value)
+
+Foreground / lifetime
+---------------------
+
+mirror.fg(true)
+    Start foreground BridgeService.
+
+mirror.fg(false)
+    Stop foreground service.
+
+mirror.alarm(ms, value)
+    Schedule alarm to reopen app with payload.
+
+mirror.lastIntent()
+    Last intent payload JSON.
+
+USB
+---
+
+mirror.usb()
+    Returns UsbManager handle.
+
+mirror.usbList()
+    Returns V + connected USB device summary.
+
+Dynamic extension
+-----------------
+
+mirror.dex(path, optDir, libDir)
+    Load external dex/jar/apk through DexClassLoader. Returns loader handle.
+
+mirror.lib(pathOrName, abs)
+    System.load or System.loadLibrary.
+
+Common patterns
+---------------
+
+1. App tester:
+    mirror.exec({observe:['screen'],act:[{click:'OK'},{input:['Name','Alice']}],returnEvents:true})
+
+2. Chrome player:
+    mirror.exec({act:[{openIn:{pkg:'com.android.chrome',uri:'https://google.com'}},{waitFor:{q:'Google',ms:5000}}]})
+
+3. Vision AI:
+    mirror.mp(1); mirror.mpStart(0,0,0);
+    const pipe = mirror.framePipeStart(JSON.stringify({l:0,t:0,r:720,b:1280,maxW:128,format:'gray',fps:30,slots:3}),0)
+
+4. Video chat:
+    HTML uses getUserMedia + RTCPeerConnection. mirror provides camera/mic permission and optional screen frame pipe.
+
+5. PC bridge:
+    HTML transport receives JSON command packets, runs mirror.exec(packet), returns result.
+
+6. Termux-like:
+    Use process sessions for persistent shell, sys('su') for root if available, sys('adb') for adb if available.
+
+7. Self-modification:
+    mirror.boot(newHtml,true), pageOpen/pageClose, framePipeStop, sensorStop, procKill.

+    fun mkWeb(id: String): WebView = WebView(this).apply {

+                resultMsg?.sendToTarget()

+            override fun onPageStarted(view: WebView?, url: String?, favicon: Bitmap?) { A = false }
+            override fun shouldInterceptRequest(view: WebView?, request: WebResourceRequest?): WebResourceResponse? {
+                val url = request?.url; val h = url?.host?.lowercase()
+                val allowed = !NET || url?.scheme in listOf("file", "data", "about", "blob") || (h != null && D.any { it.isNotEmpty() && (h == it || h.endsWith(".$it")) })
+                return if (allowed) null else WebResourceResponse("text/plain", "utf-8", ByteArrayInputStream(ByteArray(0)))
+            }
+        }
+    }

+    fun pageMake(id: String, visible: Boolean): WebView {

+    }

+    override fun onDestroy() {
+        super.onDestroy()
+        runCatching { sr?.destroy() }; sr = null
+        runCatching { tts?.shutdown() }; tts = null
+        runCatching { val sm = getSystemService(Context.SENSOR_SERVICE) as android.hardware.SensorManager; SL.values.forEach { sm.unregisterListener(it) }; SL.clear() }
+        runCatching { framePipeStop(0) }; runCatching { mpStreamStop() }; runCatching { mpStop() }
+        runCatching { synchronized(PR) { PR.values.forEach { it.p.destroyForcibly() }; PR.clear() } }
+        runCatching { pages.values.toList().forEach { it.destroy() }; pages.clear() }
+        H.clear()
+    }

+    override fun onKeyDown(keyCode: Int, event: KeyEvent?): Boolean {

+            b.visibility = View.VISIBLE
+            Handler(Looper.getMainLooper()).postDelayed({ v(m) }, 3000)
+            return false // Don't change volume
+        }
+        return super.onKeyDown(keyCode, event)
+    }

+        }

+        U("Sandbox: ${if (s) "ON" else "OFF"}", if (s) Color.GREEN else Color.RED, "Password", PW, {
+            s = !s; S("S", s)
+            it.text = "Sandbox: ${if (s) "ON" else "OFF"}"
+            it.setTextColor(if (s) Color.GREEN else Color.RED)

+            text = SpannableStringBuilder().apply {

+                "unhide/hide settings:till next focus/open/perma".split(Regex("(?<=[a-z])(?=[/:])|(?<=[/:])(?=[a-z])")).forEach { w ->
+                    if (map.containsKey(w)) {
+                        val start = length
+                        append(" $w ")

+                            }

+            }
+        }
+        l.addView(tv)
+
+        U("Cut Internet: ${if (NET) "ON" else "OFF"}", if (NET) Color.RED else Color.GREEN, "Exceptions", D.joinToString(","), {
+            NET = !NET; S("N", NET)
+            it.text = "Cut Internet: ${if (NET) "ON" else "OFF"}"
+            it.setTextColor(if (NET) Color.RED else Color.GREEN)
+        }) { D.clear(); D.addAll(it.split(",").map { i -> i.trim().lowercase() }.filter { i -> i.isNotEmpty() }); S("D", D.joinToString(",")) }
+
+        val n = arrayOf("Blacklist", "Graylist", "Whitelist")
+        (0..2).forEach { i ->
+            l.addView(TextView(this).apply { text = n[i]; setTextColor(Color.WHITE) })
+            val e = EditText(this).apply {
+                setText(L[i].joinToString(","))
+                setTextColor(Color.WHITE)
+                setBackgroundColor(Color.DKGRAY)
+            }
+            T(e) { L[i].clear(); L[i].addAll(it.split(",").map { x -> x.trim().lowercase() }.filter { x -> x.isNotEmpty() }); S("$i", L[i].joinToString(",")) }
+            l.addView(e)
+        }
+        AlertDialog.Builder(this).setView(sv).show()
+    }
+
+    fun K(i: Int, s: String) = L[i].any { it.equals("ALL", true) || (it.isNotBlank() && s.contains(it.trim().lowercase())) }
+
+    fun C(n: String): Class<*>? = try {
+        val x = n.trim()
+        val prim = mapOf(
+            "boolean" to java.lang.Boolean.TYPE, "byte" to java.lang.Byte.TYPE,
+            "short" to java.lang.Short.TYPE, "char" to java.lang.Character.TYPE,
+            "int" to java.lang.Integer.TYPE, "long" to java.lang.Long.TYPE,
+            "float" to java.lang.Float.TYPE, "double" to java.lang.Double.TYPE,
+            "void" to java.lang.Void.TYPE
+        )
+        when {
+            prim.containsKey(x) -> prim[x]
+            x.endsWith("[]") -> C(x.removeSuffix("[]"))?.let { java.lang.reflect.Array.newInstance(it, 0).javaClass }
+            else -> Class.forName(x)
+        }
+    } catch (e: Exception) { null }
+
+    fun hp(p: Int) = synchronized(H) { H.getOrNull(p) }
+    fun keep(o: Any?): String = synchronized(H) { H.add(o); "P${H.size - 1}" }
+    fun mainThread() = Looper.myLooper() == Looper.getMainLooper()
+
+    fun exUi(t: Any?, n: String, a: JSONArray, y: Int): String {
+        if (mainThread()) return Ex(t, n, a, y)
+        var r = "E:TIME"
+        val latch = CountDownLatch(1)
+        runOnUiThread {
+            r = try { Ex(t, n, a, y) } catch (e: Exception) { "E:$e" }
+            latch.countDown()
+        }
+        return if (latch.await(30, TimeUnit.SECONDS)) r else "E:TIME"
+    }
+
+    fun R(t: Any?, n: String, j: String, y: Int, cI: Int): String {
+        if (!ok()) return "E:SEC"
+        try {
+            val c = if (t is Class<*>) t else t?.javaClass ?: return "E:NUL"
+            val a = JSONArray(j)
+            val sig = "$n ${c.name}".lowercase()
+            if (y < 4 && K(0, sig)) return "E:BL"
+
+            // Ask once for unknown public reflection calls. Async calls receive W:first, then final via cb.
+            // Sync JS calls run on WebView bridge thread, so they can wait for the dialog result.
+            val g = if (y > 3 || K(1, sig)) null else if (K(2, sig)) "ALL" else L[2].find { it.isNotBlank() && sig.contains(it.trim().lowercase()) } ?: if (L[0].isEmpty() && L[1].isEmpty()) "ALL" else null
+            if (g != null) {
+                val waitId = (System.currentTimeMillis() % 999).toInt()
+                if (cI <= 0 && !mainThread()) {
+                    var r = "E:CANCEL"
+                    val latch = CountDownLatch(1)
+                    runOnUiThread {
+                        val d = AlertDialog.Builder(this).setTitle("REQ:$sig")
+                            .setPositiveButton("1") { _, _ -> r = exUi(t, n, a, y); latch.countDown() }
+                            .setNeutralButton("OK") { _, _ -> L[1].add(g); S("1", L[1].joinToString(",")); r = exUi(t, n, a, y); latch.countDown() }
+                            .setNegativeButton("NO") { _, _ -> L[0].add(g); S("0", L[0].joinToString(",")); r = "E:BL"; latch.countDown() }
+                            .create()
+                        d.setOnCancelListener { r = "E:CANCEL"; latch.countDown() }
+                        d.show()
+                    }
+                    return if (latch.await(300, TimeUnit.SECONDS)) r else "E:TIME"
+                }
+                runOnUiThread {
+                    val d = AlertDialog.Builder(this).setTitle("REQ:$sig")
+                        .setPositiveButton("1") { _, _ -> cb(cI, exUi(t, n, a, y)) }
+                        .setNeutralButton("OK") { _, _ -> L[1].add(g); S("1", L[1].joinToString(",")); cb(cI, exUi(t, n, a, y)) }
+                        .setNegativeButton("NO") { _, _ -> L[0].add(g); S("0", L[0].joinToString(",")); cb(cI, "E:BL") }
+                        .create()
+                    d.setOnCancelListener { cb(cI, "E:CANCEL") }
+                    d.show()
+                }
+                return "W:$waitId"
+            }
+            return exUi(t, n, a, y)
+        } catch (e: Exception) { return "E:$e" }
+    }
+
+    fun acc(x: java.lang.reflect.AccessibleObject) = runCatching { x.isAccessible = true }.isSuccess
+
+    fun fieldsOf(c: Class<*>): List<Field> = synchronized(FC) { FC.getOrPut(c.name) {
+        val r = ArrayList<Field>(); val seen = HashSet<String>(); var k: Class<*>? = c
+        while (k != null) { for (f in k.declaredFields) if (seen.add(f.name)) { acc(f); r.add(f) }; k = k.superclass }
+        for (f in c.fields) if (seen.add(f.name)) { acc(f); r.add(f) }
+        r
+    } }
+
+    fun fieldOf(c: Class<*>, n: String): Field? = fieldsOf(c).firstOrNull { it.name == n }
+
+    fun methodsOf(c: Class<*>): List<Method> = synchronized(MC) { MC.getOrPut(c.name) {
+        val r = ArrayList<Method>(); val seen = HashSet<String>(); var k: Class<*>? = c
+        fun key(m: Method) = m.name + "(" + m.parameterTypes.joinToString(",") { it.name } + ")"
+        while (k != null) { for (m in k.declaredMethods) if (seen.add(key(m))) { acc(m); r.add(m) }; k = k.superclass }
+        for (m in c.methods) if (seen.add(key(m))) { acc(m); r.add(m) }
+        r
+    } }
+
+    fun consOf(c: Class<*>): List<Constructor<*>> = synchronized(CC) { CC.getOrPut(c.name) { c.declaredConstructors.toList().onEach { acc(it) } } }
+
+    fun Ex(t: Any?, n: String, a: JSONArray, y: Int): String {
+        try {
+            val c = if (t is Class<*>) t else t!!.javaClass
+            val l = a.length()
+            if (y == 2) return ret((fieldOf(c, n) ?: return "E:FIELD").get(if (t is Class<*>) null else t)) // Get field, including private/inherited where allowed
+            if (y == 3) { val f = fieldOf(c, n) ?: return "E:FIELD"; f.set(if (t is Class<*>) null else t, cv(a.get(0), f.type)); return "OK" } // Set field, including private/inherited where allowed
+            if (y == 4) return ret(java.lang.reflect.Array.newInstance(c, a.getInt(0))) // Array new
+
+            // Proxy for Java interface callbacks into JS: window.onL(id, methodName, encodedArgsJsonString)
+            val proxyId = if (y == 5) a.getInt(0) else 0
+            if (y == 5) return ret(Proxy.newProxyInstance(c.classLoader, arrayOf(c)) { _, m, r ->
+                if (m.declaringClass == Any::class.java) {
+                    return@newProxyInstance when (m.name) {
+                        "toString" -> "Proxy#$proxyId:${c.name}"
+                        "hashCode" -> proxyId
+                        "equals" -> false
+                        else -> null
+                    }
+                }
+                val g = r?.map { org.json.JSONObject.quote(ret(it)) }?.joinToString(",") ?: ""
+                runOnUiThread { w.evaluateJavascript("window.onL($proxyId,${org.json.JSONObject.quote(m.name)},${org.json.JSONObject.quote("[$g]")})", null) }
+                def(m.returnType)
+            })
+
+            var firstErr: Exception? = null
+            if (y == 1) {
+                for (con in consOf(c)) {
+                    if (con.parameterTypes.size != l) continue
+                    try {
+                        val v = Array(l) { i -> cv(a.get(i), con.parameterTypes[i]) }
+                        return ret(con.newInstance(*v))
+                    } catch (e: Exception) { if (firstErr == null) firstErr = e }
+                }
+            } else {
+                for (m in methodsOf(c)) {
+                    if (m.name != n || m.parameterTypes.size != l) continue
+                    try {
+                        val v = Array(l) { i -> cv(a.get(i), m.parameterTypes[i]) }
+                        return ret(m.invoke(if (t is Class<*>) null else t, *v))
+                    } catch (e: Exception) { if (firstErr == null) firstErr = e }
+                }
+            }
+            return if (firstErr != null) "E:SIG:${firstErr}" else "E:SIG"
+        } catch (e: Exception) {
+            return if (e is InvocationTargetException) "E:${e.targetException}" else "E:$e"
+        }
+    }
+
+    fun boxed(t: Class<*>) = when (t) {
+        java.lang.Boolean.TYPE -> java.lang.Boolean::class.java
+        java.lang.Byte.TYPE -> java.lang.Byte::class.java
+        java.lang.Short.TYPE -> java.lang.Short::class.java
+        java.lang.Character.TYPE -> java.lang.Character::class.java
+        java.lang.Integer.TYPE -> java.lang.Integer::class.java
+        java.lang.Long.TYPE -> java.lang.Long::class.java
+        java.lang.Float.TYPE -> java.lang.Float::class.java
+        java.lang.Double.TYPE -> java.lang.Double::class.java
+        java.lang.Void.TYPE -> java.lang.Void::class.java
+        else -> t
+    }
+
+    fun def(t: Class<*>): Any? = when (t) {
+        java.lang.Boolean.TYPE -> false
+        java.lang.Byte.TYPE -> 0.toByte()
+        java.lang.Short.TYPE -> 0.toShort()
+        java.lang.Character.TYPE -> 0.toChar()
+        java.lang.Integer.TYPE -> 0
+        java.lang.Long.TYPE -> 0L
+        java.lang.Float.TYPE -> 0f
+        java.lang.Double.TYPE -> 0.0
+        java.lang.Void.TYPE -> null
+        else -> null
+    }
+
+    @Suppress("UNCHECKED_CAST")
+    fun cv(o: Any?, t: Class<*>): Any? {
+        if (o === org.json.JSONObject.NULL) return null
+        if (t.isArray && o is JSONArray) {
+            val comp = t.componentType
+            val arr = java.lang.reflect.Array.newInstance(comp, o.length())
+            for (i in 0 until o.length()) java.lang.reflect.Array.set(arr, i, cv(o.get(i), comp))
+            return arr
+        }
+        if (o is String && o.startsWith("P")) return hp(o.substring(1).toIntOrNull() ?: -1)
+        val b = boxed(t)
+        if (o == null) return null
+        if (b.isInstance(o)) return o
+        if (b == String::class.java || b == CharSequence::class.java) return o.toString()
+        if (b == java.lang.Boolean::class.java) return if (o is Boolean) o else o.toString().toBoolean()
+        if (b == java.lang.Character::class.java) return o.toString().firstOrNull() ?: 0.toChar()
+        if (o is Number) return when (b) {
+            java.lang.Byte::class.java -> o.toByte()
+            java.lang.Short::class.java -> o.toShort()
+            java.lang.Integer::class.java -> o.toInt()
+            java.lang.Long::class.java -> o.toLong()
+            java.lang.Float::class.java -> o.toFloat()
+            java.lang.Double::class.java -> o.toDouble()
+            else -> o
+        }
+        if (o is String) {
+            when (b) {
+                java.lang.Byte::class.java -> return o.toByte()
+                java.lang.Short::class.java -> return o.toShort()
+                java.lang.Integer::class.java -> return o.toInt()
+                java.lang.Long::class.java -> return o.toLong()
+                java.lang.Float::class.java -> return o.toFloat()
+                java.lang.Double::class.java -> return o.toDouble()
+                Class::class.java -> return C(o)
+            }
+            if (b.isEnum) return b.enumConstants.firstOrNull { (it as Enum<*>).name == o }
+        }
+        return o
+    }
+
+    fun ret(o: Any?) = if (o == null || o is Unit) "V" else if (o is Number || o is String || o is Boolean || o is Char) "V$o" else keep(o)
+
+    fun cb(i: Int, r: String) = if (i > 0) runOnUiThread { w.evaluateJavascript("window.onC($i,${org.json.JSONObject.quote(r)})", null) } else Unit
+
+    fun gz(v: String): String {
+        val out = ByteArrayOutputStream()
+        GZIPOutputStream(out).use { it.write(v.toByteArray(Charsets.UTF_8)) }
+        return Base64.encodeToString(out.toByteArray(), Base64.NO_WRAP)
+    }
+
+    fun ungz(v: String): String {
+        val b = Base64.decode(v, Base64.NO_WRAP)
+        return GZIPInputStream(ByteArrayInputStream(b)).bufferedReader(Charsets.UTF_8).use { it.readText() }
+    }
+
+    fun jput(a: JSONArray, v: Any?) { a.put(if (v == null) JSONObject.NULL else v) }
+
+    fun cmd(o: JSONObject): String {
+        val op = o.optString("op")
+        return when (op) {
+            "c" -> c(o.optString("n"))
+            "cl" -> cl()
+            "c2" -> c2(o.optInt("loader"), o.optString("n"))
+            "new", "n" -> n(o.optString("c"), o.optString("j", "[]"))
+            "x" -> x(o.optInt("p"), o.optString("n"), o.optString("j", "[]"))
+            "u" -> { u(o.optInt("p"), o.optString("n"), o.optString("j", "[]"), o.optInt("cb")); "OK" }
+            "g" -> g(o.optInt("p"), o.optString("f"))
+            "s" -> s(o.optInt("p"), o.optString("f"), o.optString("v", "null"))
+            "a" -> a(o.optString("t"), o.optInt("l"))
+            "p" -> p(o.optString("t"), o.optInt("id"))
+            "svc" -> svc()
+            "str" -> str(o.optString("v"))
+            "get" -> get(o.optInt("p"))
+            "type" -> "V" + type(o.optInt("p"))
+            "del" -> { del(o.optInt("p")); "OK" }
+            "sleep" -> { Thread.sleep(o.optLong("ms", 0)); "OK" }
+            "toast" -> toast(o.optString("v"))
+            "vibe" -> vibe(o.optLong("ms", 80))
+            "clip" -> clip(o.optString("v"))
+            "getClip" -> getClip()
+            "save" -> save(o.optString("k"), o.optString("v"), o.optBoolean("zip", false))
+            "load" -> load(o.optString("k"), o.optBoolean("zip", false))
+            "rm" -> rm(o.optString("k"))
+            "keys" -> keys(o.optString("prefix", ""))
+            "zip" -> "V" + gz(o.optString("v"))
+            "unzip" -> "V" + ungz(o.optString("v"))
+            "events" -> "V" + (GlobalService.instance?.events(o.optInt("max", 100)) ?: "[]")
+            "now" -> "V" + System.currentTimeMillis()
+            "later" -> later(o.optLong("ms"), o.optInt("cb"), o.optString("v", "OK"))
+            "sh" -> sh(o.optString("cmd"), o.optString("in", ""), o.optLong("ms", 10000))
+            "sys" -> "V" + sysRun(o.optString("mode", "sh"), o.optString("cmd"), o.optString("in", ""), o.optLong("ms", 10000))
+            "page" -> pageOpen(o.optString("id"), o.optString("src"), o.optString("kind", "html"), o.optBoolean("visible", true))
+            "pageEval" -> pageEval(o.optString("id"), o.optString("js"), o.optInt("cb"))
+            "pageShow" -> pageShow(o.optString("id"))
+            "pageClose" -> pageClose(o.optString("id"))
+            "usb" -> usb()
+            "usbList" -> usbList()
+            "kc" -> kc(o.optString("n"))
+            "kco" -> kco(o.optInt("p"))
+            "kinfo" -> kinfo(o.optInt("p"))
+            "jinfo" -> jinfo(o.optInt("p"))
+            "q" -> q(o.optString("v"))
+            "dex" -> dex(o.optString("path"), o.optString("opt", ""), o.optString("lib", ""))
+            "lib" -> lib(o.optString("v"), o.optBoolean("abs", true))
+            "sensor" -> sensor(o.optInt("type"), o.optInt("delay", 1), o.optInt("cb"))
+            "sensorStop" -> sensorStop(o.optInt("id"))
+            "mp" -> mediaProjection(o.optInt("cb"))
+            "mpStart" -> mpStart(o.optInt("w", 0), o.optInt("h", 0), o.optInt("dpi", 0))
+            "mpFrame" -> mpFrameEx(o.optInt("l", -1), o.optInt("t", -1), o.optInt("r", -1), o.optInt("b", -1), o.optInt("maxW", 640), o.optInt("quality", 70), o.optString("format", "jpg"))
+            "mpStream" -> mpStreamStart(o.toString(), o.optInt("cb"))
+            "mpStreamStop" -> mpStreamStop()
+            "framePipe" -> framePipeStart(JSONObject().put("l", a.optInt(1, -1)).put("t", a.optInt(2, -1)).put("r", a.optInt(3, -1)).put("b", a.optInt(4, -1)).put("maxW", a.optInt(5, 320)).put("quality", a.optInt(6, 60)).put("format", a.optString(7, "jpg")).put("fps", a.optInt(8, 10)).put("slots", a.optInt(9, 3)).toString(), a.optInt(10))
+            "framePipeStop" -> framePipeStop(a.optInt(1, 0))
+            "framePipeInfo" -> framePipeInfo(a.optInt(1))
+            "mpStop" -> mpStop()
+            "mpData" -> if (mpData == null) "E:NUL" else ret(mpData)
+            "intent" -> startEx(o.optString("action"), o.optString("uri", ""), o.optString("mime", ""), o.optString("pkg", ""), o.optString("cls", ""), o.optString("extras", "{}"), o.optString("cats", ""), o.optInt("flags", 0))
+            "resolve" -> resolveIntent(o.optString("action"), o.optString("uri", ""), o.optString("mime", ""), o.optString("pkg", ""), o.optString("cls", ""), o.optString("extras", "{}"), o.optString("cats", ""), o.optInt("flags", 0))
+            "canIntent" -> canIntent(o.optString("action"), o.optString("uri", ""), o.optString("mime", ""), o.optString("pkg", ""), o.optString("cls", ""), o.optString("extras", "{}"), o.optString("cats", ""), o.optInt("flags", 0))
+            "open" -> open(o.optString("uri"))
+            "openIn" -> openIn(o.optString("pkg"), o.optString("uri"))
+            "launch" -> launch(o.optString("pkg"))
+            "waitFor" -> waitFor(o.optString("q"), o.optLong("ms", 5000))
+            "playClick" -> playClick(o.optString("q"), o.optLong("ms", 5000))
+            "share" -> share(o.optString("text"), o.optString("mime", "text/plain"))
+            "choose" -> choose(o.optString("action"), o.optString("uri", ""), o.optString("mime", ""), o.optString("extras", "{}"))
+            "alarm" -> alarm(o.optLong("ms"), o.optString("v", ""))
+            "notify" -> notify(o.optInt("id", 1), o.optString("title"), o.optString("text"))
+            "fr" -> fr(o.optString("name"), o.optBoolean("zip", false))
+            "fw" -> fw(o.optString("name"), o.optString("v"), o.optBoolean("zip", false))
+            "fg" -> fg(o.optBoolean("on", true))
+            "notis" -> "V" + NotifService.events(o.optInt("max", 100))
+            "boot" -> boot(o.optString("html", ""), o.optBoolean("reload", false))
+            "fetch" -> fetch(o.optString("url"), o.optString("method", "GET"), o.optString("headers", "{}"), o.optString("body", ""), o.optInt("timeout", 15000), o.optBoolean("clean", true), o.optBoolean("sensitive", false), o.optInt("max", 2097152))
+            "screenshot" -> screenshot(o.optInt("l", -1), o.optInt("t", -1), o.optInt("r", -1), o.optInt("b", -1), o.optInt("maxW", 640), o.optInt("quality", 70))
+            else -> "E:OP"
+        }
+    }

+    fun argJson(a: JSONArray, i: Int): String = if (i >= a.length() || a.isNull(i)) "[]" else when (val v = a.get(i)) {
+        is JSONArray -> v.toString()
+        is JSONObject -> v.toString()
+        else -> v.toString()
+    }

+    // Condensed op form for high-frequency loops: ["x",ptr,"method",[args...]], ["tap",x,y], ["save",k,v,zip]
+    fun cmdA(a: JSONArray): String {
+        val op = a.optString(0)
+        return when (op) {
+            "c" -> c(a.optString(1))
+            "cl" -> cl()
+            "c2" -> c2(a.optInt(1), a.optString(2))
+            "n", "new" -> n(a.optString(1), argJson(a, 2))
+            "x" -> x(a.optInt(1), a.optString(2), argJson(a, 3))
+            "u" -> { u(a.optInt(1), a.optString(2), argJson(a, 3), a.optInt(4)); "OK" }
+            "g" -> g(a.optInt(1), a.optString(2))
+            "s" -> s(a.optInt(1), a.optString(2), argJson(a, 3).removeSurrounding("[", "]"))
+            "a" -> this.a(a.optString(1), a.optInt(2))
+            "p" -> p(a.optString(1), a.optInt(2))
+            "svc" -> svc()
+            "tap" -> GlobalService.instance?.tap(a.optDouble(1).toFloat(), a.optDouble(2).toFloat())?.let { "V$it" } ?: "E:SVC"
+            "swipe" -> GlobalService.instance?.swipe(a.optDouble(1).toFloat(), a.optDouble(2).toFloat(), a.optDouble(3).toFloat(), a.optDouble(4).toFloat(), a.optLong(5, 120))?.let { "V$it" } ?: "E:SVC"
+            "read" -> "V" + (GlobalService.instance?.readScreen() ?: "[]")
+            "events" -> "V" + (GlobalService.instance?.events(a.optInt(1, 100)) ?: "[]")
+            "clip" -> clip(a.optString(1))
+            "getClip" -> getClip()
+            "save" -> save(a.optString(1), a.optString(2), a.optBoolean(3, false))
+            "load" -> load(a.optString(1), a.optBoolean(2, false))
+            "sh" -> sh(a.optString(1), a.optString(2, ""), a.optLong(3, 10000))
+            "sys" -> "V" + sysRun(a.optString(1, "sh"), a.optString(2), a.optString(3, ""), a.optLong(4, 10000))
+            "page" -> pageOpen(a.optString(1), a.optString(2), a.optString(3, "html"), a.optBoolean(4, true))
+            "pageEval" -> pageEval(a.optString(1), a.optString(2), a.optInt(3))
+            "pageShow" -> pageShow(a.optString(1))
+            "pageClose" -> pageClose(a.optString(1))
+            "usb" -> usb()
+            "usbList" -> usbList()
+            "kc" -> kc(a.optString(1))
+            "kco" -> kco(a.optInt(1))
+            "kinfo" -> kinfo(a.optInt(1))
+            "jinfo" -> jinfo(a.optInt(1))
+            "q" -> q(a.optString(1))
+            "dex" -> dex(a.optString(1), a.optString(2, ""), a.optString(3, ""))
+            "lib" -> lib(a.optString(1), a.optBoolean(2, true))
+            "sensor" -> sensor(a.optInt(1), a.optInt(2, 1), a.optInt(3))
+            "sensorStop" -> sensorStop(a.optInt(1))
+            "mp" -> mediaProjection(a.optInt(1))
+            "mpStart" -> mpStart(a.optInt(1, 0), a.optInt(2, 0), a.optInt(3, 0))
+            "mpFrame" -> mpFrameEx(a.optInt(1, -1), a.optInt(2, -1), a.optInt(3, -1), a.optInt(4, -1), a.optInt(5, 640), a.optInt(6, 70), a.optString(7, "jpg"))
+            "mpStream" -> mpStreamStart(JSONObject().put("l", a.optInt(1, -1)).put("t", a.optInt(2, -1)).put("r", a.optInt(3, -1)).put("b", a.optInt(4, -1)).put("maxW", a.optInt(5, 320)).put("quality", a.optInt(6, 60)).put("format", a.optString(7, "jpg")).put("fps", a.optInt(8, 10)).toString(), a.optInt(9))
+            "mpStreamStop" -> mpStreamStop()
+            "framePipe" -> framePipeStart(JSONObject().put("l", a.optInt(1, -1)).put("t", a.optInt(2, -1)).put("r", a.optInt(3, -1)).put("b", a.optInt(4, -1)).put("maxW", a.optInt(5, 320)).put("quality", a.optInt(6, 60)).put("format", a.optString(7, "jpg")).put("fps", a.optInt(8, 10)).put("slots", a.optInt(9, 3)).toString(), a.optInt(10))
+            "framePipeStop" -> framePipeStop(a.optInt(1, 0))
+            "framePipeInfo" -> framePipeInfo(a.optInt(1))
+            "mpStop" -> mpStop()
+            "intent" -> startEx(a.optString(1), a.optString(2, ""), a.optString(3, ""), a.optString(4, ""), a.optString(5, ""), a.optString(6, "{}"), a.optString(7, ""), a.optInt(8, 0))
+            "resolve" -> resolveIntent(a.optString(1), a.optString(2, ""), a.optString(3, ""), a.optString(4, ""), a.optString(5, ""), a.optString(6, "{}"), a.optString(7, ""), a.optInt(8, 0))
+            "open" -> open(a.optString(1))
+            "openIn" -> openIn(a.optString(1), a.optString(2))
+            "launch" -> launch(a.optString(1))
+            "waitFor" -> waitFor(a.optString(1), a.optLong(2, 5000))
+            "playClick" -> playClick(a.optString(1), a.optLong(2, 5000))
+            "share" -> share(a.optString(1), a.optString(2, "text/plain"))
+            "choose" -> choose(a.optString(1), a.optString(2, ""), a.optString(3, ""), a.optString(4, "{}"))
+            "alarm" -> alarm(a.optLong(1), a.optString(2, ""))
+            "notify" -> notify(a.optInt(1, 1), a.optString(2), a.optString(3))
+            "fr" -> fr(a.optString(1), a.optBoolean(2, false))
+            "fw" -> fw(a.optString(1), a.optString(2), a.optBoolean(3, false))
+            "fg" -> fg(a.optBoolean(1, true))
+            "notis" -> "V" + NotifService.events(a.optInt(1, 100))
+            "fetch" -> fetch(a.optString(1), a.optString(2, "GET"), a.optString(3, "{}"), a.optString(4, ""), a.optInt(5, 15000), a.optBoolean(6, true), a.optBoolean(7, false), a.optInt(8, 2097152))
+            "screenshot" -> screenshot(a.optInt(1, -1), a.optInt(2, -1), a.optInt(3, -1), a.optInt(4, -1), a.optInt(5, 640), a.optInt(6, 70))
+            else -> cmd(JSONObject().put("op", op))
+        }
+    }

+    fun urlAllowed(u: String): Boolean = try {
+        val uri = android.net.Uri.parse(u); val h = uri.host?.lowercase()
+        !NET || uri.scheme in listOf("file", "data", "about", "blob") || (h != null && D.any { it.isNotEmpty() && (h == it || h.endsWith(".$it")) })
+    } catch (_: Exception) { false }
+
+    fun cleanUrl(u: String): String = try {
+        val uri = android.net.Uri.parse(u); val drop = setOf("fbclid", "gclid", "dclid", "mc_cid", "mc_eid", "igshid", "si")
+        val b = uri.buildUpon().clearQuery(); for (k in uri.queryParameterNames) if (!k.startsWith("utm_") && !drop.contains(k)) uri.getQueryParameters(k).forEach { b.appendQueryParameter(k, it) }
+        b.build().toString()
+    } catch (_: Exception) { u }
+
+    fun netFetch(url0: String, method: String, headers: String, body: String, timeout: Int, clean: Boolean, sensitive: Boolean, max: Int): String {
+        return try {
+        if (!ok()) return "E:SEC"
+        val url = if (clean) cleanUrl(url0) else url0
+        if (!urlAllowed(url)) return "E:NET"
+        val c = (URL(url).openConnection() as HttpURLConnection).apply {
+            requestMethod = method.ifBlank { "GET" }.uppercase(); connectTimeout = timeout; readTimeout = timeout; instanceFollowRedirects = true
+            val hs = if (headers.isBlank()) JSONObject() else JSONObject(headers)
+            for (k in hs.keys()) setRequestProperty(k, hs.get(k).toString())
+            if (body.isNotEmpty()) { doOutput = true; outputStream.use { it.write(body.toByteArray()) } }
+        }
+        val code = c.responseCode; val bytes = (if (code >= 400) c.errorStream else c.inputStream)?.readBytes()?.let { if (it.size > max) it.copyOf(max) else it } ?: ByteArray(0)
+        val hs = JSONObject(); c.headerFields?.forEach { (k, v) -> if (k != null) hs.put(k, JSONArray(v)) }
+        JSONObject().put("url", url).put("code", code).put("type", c.contentType ?: "").put("headers", hs)
+            .put("text", runCatching { String(bytes, Charsets.UTF_8) }.getOrDefault(""))
+            .put("base64", Base64.encodeToString(bytes, Base64.NO_WRAP)).toString()
+    } catch (e: Exception) { "E:$e" }
+    }
+
+    fun shell(cmd: String, input: String, timeoutMs: Long): String {
+        val p = ProcessBuilder("sh", "-c", cmd).redirectErrorStream(false).start()
+        if (input.isNotEmpty()) p.outputStream.use { it.write(input.toByteArray()) } else p.outputStream.close()
+        val out = ByteArrayOutputStream(); val err = ByteArrayOutputStream()
+        val t1 = Thread { runCatching { p.inputStream.copyTo(out) } }
+        val t2 = Thread { runCatching { p.errorStream.copyTo(err) } }
+        t1.start(); t2.start()
+        val deadline = System.currentTimeMillis() + maxOf(1L, timeoutMs)
+        var ok = false; var code = -1
+        while (System.currentTimeMillis() < deadline) {
+            try { code = p.exitValue(); ok = true; break } catch (_: IllegalThreadStateException) { Thread.sleep(10) }
+        }
+        if (!ok) p.destroyForcibly() else code = p.exitValue()
+        t1.join(200); t2.join(200)
+        return JSONObject().put("code", code).put("timeout", !ok)
+            .put("out", out.toString("UTF-8")).put("err", err.toString("UTF-8")).toString()
+    }
+
+    fun procStart(cmd: String, cwd: String, env: JSONObject?, input: String): String = try {
+        if (!ok()) "E:SEC" else {
+            val pb = ProcessBuilder("sh", "-c", cmd)
+            if (cwd.isNotBlank()) pb.directory(java.io.File(cwd))
+            env?.let { for (k in it.keys()) pb.environment()[k] = it.get(k).toString() }
+            val pr = Proc(pb.start()); val id = synchronized(PR) { val i = prid++; PR[i] = pr; i }
+            Thread { runCatching { pr.p.inputStream.copyTo(pr.out) } }.start()
+            Thread { runCatching { pr.p.errorStream.copyTo(pr.err) } }.start()
+            if (input.isNotEmpty()) runCatching { pr.p.outputStream.write(input.toByteArray()); pr.p.outputStream.flush() }
+            "V" + JSONObject().put("id", id).toString()
+        }
+    } catch (e: Exception) { "E:$e" }
+
+    fun procWrite(id: Int, data: String, close: Boolean): String = synchronized(PR) { PR[id] }?.let {
+        runCatching { it.p.outputStream.write(data.toByteArray()); it.p.outputStream.flush(); if (close) it.p.outputStream.close() }.fold({ "OK" }, { e -> "E:$e" })
+    } ?: "E:NOPROC"
+
+    fun procRead(id: Int, clear: Boolean): String = synchronized(PR) { PR[id] }?.let { pr ->
+        val out: String; val err: String
+        synchronized(pr.out) { out = pr.out.toString("UTF-8"); if (clear) pr.out.reset() }
+        synchronized(pr.err) { err = pr.err.toString("UTF-8"); if (clear) pr.err.reset() }
+        val alive = runCatching { pr.p.exitValue(); false }.getOrDefault(true)
+        val code = if (alive) JSONObject.NULL else runCatching { pr.p.exitValue() }.getOrDefault(-1)
+        "V" + JSONObject().put("alive", alive).put("code", code).put("out", out).put("err", err).toString()
+    } ?: "E:NOPROC"
+
+    fun sysRun(mode: String, cmd: String, input: String, timeoutMs: Long): String = when (mode.lowercase()) {
+        "root", "su" -> shell("su -c ${org.json.JSONObject.quote(cmd)}", input, timeoutMs)
+        "adb" -> shell("adb shell ${org.json.JSONObject.quote(cmd)}", input, timeoutMs)
+        "sh", "shell", "" -> shell(cmd, input, timeoutMs)
+        else -> shell(mode + " " + cmd, input, timeoutMs)
+    }
+
+    @JavascriptInterface fun pageOpen(id: String, src: String, kind: String, visible: Boolean): String = try {
+        if (!ok()) "E:SEC" else {
+            runOnUiThread {
+                val v = pageMake(id.ifBlank { "page" + System.currentTimeMillis() }, visible)
+                when (kind.lowercase()) { "url" -> v.loadUrl(src); "base64" -> v.loadData(String(Base64.decode(src, Base64.NO_WRAP)), "text/html", "utf-8"); else -> v.loadDataWithBaseURL(null, src, "text/html", "utf-8", null) }
+            }
+            "OK"
+        }
+    } catch (e: Exception) { "E:$e" }
+
+    @JavascriptInterface fun pageEval(id: String, js: String, cbId: Int): String { if (!ok()) return "E:SEC"; val v = pages[id] ?: return "E:PAGE"; runOnUiThread { v.evaluateJavascript(js) { r -> cb(cbId, r ?: "") } }; return "OK" }
+    @JavascriptInterface fun pageShow(id: String): String { if (!ok()) return "E:SEC"; val v = pages[id] ?: return "E:PAGE"; runOnUiThread { pages.values.forEach { it.visibility = View.GONE }; v.visibility = View.VISIBLE; w = v; b.bringToFront() }; return "OK" }
+    @JavascriptInterface fun pageClose(id: String): String { if (!ok()) return "E:SEC"; val v = pages.remove(id) ?: return "E:PAGE"; runOnUiThread { root.removeView(v); v.destroy(); if (w == v) pages["main"]?.let { w = it; it.visibility = View.VISIBLE } }; return "OK" }
+    @JavascriptInterface fun pageList(): String { if (!ok()) return "E:SEC"; val a = JSONArray(); pages.keys.forEach { a.put(it) }; return "V" + a.toString() }
+    @JavascriptInterface fun usb() = if (ok()) ret(getSystemService(Context.USB_SERVICE)) else "E:SEC"
+    @JavascriptInterface fun usbList(): String { if (!ok()) return "E:SEC"; val m = getSystemService(Context.USB_SERVICE) as android.hardware.usb.UsbManager; val a = JSONArray(); m.deviceList.values.forEach { d -> a.put(JSONObject().put("name", d.deviceName).put("vendor", d.vendorId).put("product", d.productId).put("class", d.deviceClass).put("subclass", d.deviceSubclass).put("interfaces", d.interfaceCount)) }; return "V" + a.toString() }

+    fun procKill(id: Int): String = synchronized(PR) { PR.remove(id) }?.let { it.p.destroyForcibly(); "OK" } ?: "E:NOPROC"
+
+    fun klassOf(p: Int): Class<*>? = when (val o = hp(p)) { is Class<*> -> o; else -> o?.javaClass }
+
+    // Batch cuts JS<->Java overhead for fast game/control loops. Accepts object ops or compact array ops.
+    fun batchExec(cmds: String): String {
+        if (!ok()) return "[\"E:SEC\"]"
+        val input = JSONArray(cmds)
+        val out = JSONArray()
+        for (i in 0 until input.length()) {
+            try {
+                val v = input.get(i)
+                jput(out, if (v is JSONArray) cmdA(v) else cmd(v as JSONObject))
+            } catch (e: Exception) { jput(out, "E:$e") }
+        }
+        return out.toString()
+    }
+
+    fun info(c: Class<*>, kotlinExtra: Boolean): String {
+        val mods = java.lang.reflect.Modifier.toString(c.modifiers)
+        return JSONObject().apply {
+            put("name", c.name); put("simple", c.simpleName ?: ""); put("pkg", c.`package`?.name ?: ""); put("mods", mods)
+            put("super", c.superclass?.name ?: ""); put("interfaces", JSONArray(c.interfaces.map { it.name }))
+            put("kotlin", c.getAnnotation(kotlin.Metadata::class.java) != null)
+            put("fields", JSONArray(fieldsOf(c).map { java.lang.reflect.Modifier.toString(it.modifiers) + " " + it.name + ":" + it.type.name }))
+            put("constructors", JSONArray(consOf(c).map { "(" + it.parameterTypes.joinToString(",") { t -> t.name } + ")" }))
+            put("methods", JSONArray(methodsOf(c).map { java.lang.reflect.Modifier.toString(it.modifiers) + " " + it.name + "(" + it.parameterTypes.joinToString(",") { t -> t.name } + "):" + it.returnType.name }.distinct().take(1200)))
+            put("inners", JSONArray(c.declaredClasses.map { it.name }))
+            if (kotlinExtra) runCatching {
+                val kc = c.kotlin
+                put("kQualified", kc.qualifiedName ?: ""); put("kSimple", kc.simpleName ?: "")
+                val kclasses = Class.forName("kotlin.reflect.full.KClasses")
+                fun names(fn: String): JSONArray = runCatching {
+                    val m = kclasses.methods.firstOrNull { it.name == fn && it.parameterTypes.size == 1 } ?: return@runCatching JSONArray()
+                    val col = m.invoke(null, kc) as? Collection<*> ?: return@runCatching JSONArray()
+                    JSONArray(col.map { it.toString() })
+                }.getOrDefault(JSONArray())
+                put("kMembers", names("getMembers")); put("kFunctions", names("getFunctions")); put("kProperties", names("getMemberProperties"))
+            }
+        }.toString()
+    }
