+function renderState(){
+  log(77,1);ui.mode.textContent=state.overlayMode;ui.count.textContent=String(state.circles.length);ui.selected.textContent=state.selectedId==null?'none':'#'+state.selectedId;ui.action.textContent=state.busy?(ui.action.textContent==='idle'?'busy':ui.action.textContent):(state.captureOwner||'idle');ui.liveState.textContent=state.live.enabled?'ON':'OFF';ui.feedbackState.textContent=state.live.feedback?(state.overlayMode==='HIDDEN'?'ON · paused':'ON'):'OFF';ui.frameState.textContent=String(state.live.frames);ui.foundState.textContent=state.live.found+' / '+state.circles.length;const disabled=state.busy;[ui.start,ui.add,ui.remove,ui.accessibility,ui.calibrate,ui.place,ui.visual,ui.read,ui.click,ui.both].forEach(button=>button.disabled=disabled);ui.destroy.disabled=false;ui.hidden.disabled=false;ui.remove.disabled=disabled||!state.circles.length;ui.calibrate.disabled=disabled||!state.circles.length;ui.read.disabled=disabled||!state.circles.length;ui.click.disabled=disabled||!state.circles.length;ui.both.disabled=disabled||!state.circles.length;ui.liveOn.disabled=disabled||state.live.enabled;ui.liveOff.disabled=!state.live.enabled;ui.feedbackOn.disabled=disabled||state.live.feedback;ui.feedbackOff.disabled=!state.live.feedback;updateControllerText();scheduleDebug();log(77,2);

+async function openAccessibilitySettings(){

Enable HTML Realizer Service, then return.')}finally{dropToken(intent)}log(78,2);

+MIRROR /4 FRACTAL OPS EXAMPLES
+==============================
+
+/4 adds the first practical step toward the manifest-first/fractal design:
+manifest/resource introspection plus a generic op dispatcher.
+
+New direct bridge calls
+-----------------------
+
+mirror.manifest()
+    Returns V + manifest/package declaration info:
+    permissions, features, activities, services, receivers, providers.
+
+mirror.resources()
+    Returns V + currently running/open resource info:
+    pages, processes, framePipes, sensors, handles, MediaProjection state, speech, foreground, accessibility.
+
+mirror.root(name)
+    Generic root/system-service handle getter.
+
+    Examples:
+      mirror.root('activity')
+      mirror.root('package')
+      mirror.root('content')
+      mirror.root('sensor')
+      mirror.root('usb')
+      mirror.root('mediaProjection')
+      mirror.root('accessibility')
+      mirror.root('page:main')
+
+mirror.permissionRequest('["android.permission.CAMERA"]', cbId)
+    Generic runtime permission request. Result goes to window.onC(cbId,...).
+
+mirror.requireCaps('["accessibility","mediaProjection","android.permission.CAMERA"]')
+    Returns V + status/fix info for requested powers.
+
+mirror.stop(target)
+    Generic cleanup by resource target.
+
+    Examples:
+      mirror.stop('frame:*')
+      mirror.stop('sensor:*')
+      mirror.stop('process:*')
+      mirror.stop('page:*')
+      mirror.stop('mediaProjection')
+      mirror.stop('foreground')
+      mirror.stop('all')
+
+New exec integrations
+---------------------
+
+mirror.exec(JSON.stringify({
+  capabilities: true,
+  manifest: true,
+  resources: true,
+  require: ['accessibility', 'mediaProjection', 'android.permission.CAMERA']
+}))
+
+Generic ops are available through:
+
+mirror.exec(JSON.stringify({
+  ops: [
+    { op: 'read', target: 'mirror:caps' },
+    { op: 'read', target: 'mirror:manifest' },
+    { op: 'read', target: 'mirror:resources' },
+    { op: 'read', target: 'mirror:events', max: 50 }
+  ]
+}))
+
+Examples by resource type
+-------------------------
+
+Open a page:
+
+mirror.exec(JSON.stringify({
+  ops: [
+    { op: 'open', target: 'page:worker', data: { html: '<script>1+1</script>', kind: 'html', visible: false } }
+  ]
+}))
+
+Evaluate/write to a page:
+
+mirror.exec(JSON.stringify({
+  ops: [
+    { op: 'write', target: 'page:worker', data: { eval: 'document.title', cb: 7 } }
+  ]
+}))
+
+Start a process:
+
+mirror.exec(JSON.stringify({
+  ops: [
+    { op: 'open', target: 'process:sh1', data: { cmd: 'sh', cwd: '/sdcard' } }
+  ]
+}))
+
+Write/read process:
+
+mirror.exec(JSON.stringify({
+  ops: [
+    { op: 'write', target: 'process:1', data: 'ls\n' },
+    { op: 'read', target: 'process:1', clear: true }
+  ]
+}))
+
+Start frame pipe:
+
+mirror.exec(JSON.stringify({
+  ops: [
+    { op: 'open', target: 'frame:vision', data: { l: 0, t: 0, r: 500, b: 500, maxW: 160, format: 'gray', fps: 30, slots: 3 } }
+  ]
+}))
+
+Read frame pipe info:
+
+mirror.exec(JSON.stringify({
+  ops: [
+    { op: 'read', target: 'frame:1' }
+  ]
+}))
+
+Send gesture through generic input target:
+
+mirror.exec(JSON.stringify({
+  ops: [
+    { op: 'write', target: 'input:auto', data: { gesture: [ { points: [[100,200],[200,300]], start: 0, duration: 250 } ] } }
+  ]
+}))
+
+Write/read file:
+
+mirror.exec(JSON.stringify({
+  ops: [
+    { op: 'write', target: 'file:profile.json', data: '{"ok":true}' },
+    { op: 'read', target: 'file:profile.json' }
+  ]
+}))
+
+Stop heavy resources:
+
+mirror.exec(JSON.stringify({
+  stop: ['frame:*', 'sensor:*', 'process:*']
+}))
+
+Design meaning
+--------------
+
+This is not the final full graph system, but it starts the intended pattern:
+
+    op + target + data
+
+Targets are address-like:
+
+    mirror:caps
+    mirror:resources
+    page:worker
+    process:1
+    frame:1
+    file:profile.json
+    input:auto
+    handle:42
+
+Future adapters should plug into this shape instead of adding one-off product methods.

+MIRROR V2 HANDOFF
+=================
+
+Context
+-------
+
+This folder is a proposal/snapshot only:
+
+    android-apps/app_realizer/2/
+
+The original snapshot remains intentionally untouched:
+
+    android-apps/app_realizer/1/main.kt
+
+The branch/PR contains `/2` as the suggested next version. The user may manually
+copy/paste files instead of merging the PR.
+
+PR:
+
+    https://github.com/HalitZiyaKARTAL/test19/pull/1
+
+Primary files in /2
+-------------------
+
+    main.kt
+    manifest1.xml
+    accessibilityconfig.xml
+    accessibility_config.xml
+    strings.xml
+    MIRROR_JUMPSTART.txt
+    MIRROR_PARTS_CHEATSHEET.txt
+    HANDOFF.txt
+
+Main architectural intent
+-------------------------
+
+The app should be an install-once Android runtime/capability kernel. The real app
+logic lives in runtime HTML/JS/AI. Kotlin/native should expose stable phone powers
+through the JavaScript bridge object:
+
+    mirror
+
+Do not use old `K`; no legacy compatibility is desired.
+
+The desired mental model:
+
+    Kotlin/native = capability kernel / syscall layer
+    WebView HTML/JS = app/userland/AI logic
+    Android/Chrome/root/ADB/shell/USB/media/sensors = optional power backends
+
+The user specifically wants logical condensation/unification, not minified one-letter
+commands. Prefer semantic unified transactions:
+
+    mirror.exec(JSON.stringify({
+      observe: ['screen', 'events', 'notifications'],
+      act: [ { tap:[100,200] }, { say:'done' } ],
+      remember: { file:'profile.json.gz', value:'...', compress:true },
+      schedule: { in:3600000, payload:'drink water' },
+      returnEvents: true
+    }))
+
+Policy belongs to HTML, not Kotlin. Native should provide powers and paths, including
+powerful/unsafe paths if the user intentionally grants them. Examples:
+
+- custom headers including Cookie/Authorization
+- root/su
+- adb shell
+- USB manager and future USB bulk primitives
+- dynamic dex/native library loading
+- reflection
+- MediaProjection
+- accessibility automation
+
+Current v2 capabilities
+-----------------------
+
+The current `/2/main.kt` proposal includes broad raw connections:
+
+1. Primary bridge
+   - mirror.exec(json)
+   - mirror.execAsync(json, cbId)
+   - mirror.caps()
+   - mirror.events(json)
+
+2. Boot document
+   - simple fallback shape:
+       var editable: String? = null
+       var html = editable ?: """<!DOCTYPE html>..."""
+   - mirror.boot(html, reload)
+   - mirror.bootGet()
+   - mirror.bootReset(reload)
+
+3. WebView/pages
+   - multiple WebViews/pages/tabs
+   - pageOpen/pageEval/pageShow/pageClose/pageList
+   - WebView camera/mic/geolocation permission grant
+   - file/content access, media playback, mixed content, debugging, multiple windows
+
+4. Reflection/extension
+   - Java reflection with declared/private/inherited member scanning where allowed
+   - field/method/constructor caches
+   - Kotlin KClass handles/info when kotlin-reflect/full metadata is available
+   - classloader handle
+   - DexClassLoader loading
+   - native library loading
+
+5. Accessibility/app playing
+   - screen tree JSON
+   - event queue
+   - tap/swipe/gestures
+   - click/longClick/focus/input by query
+   - waitFor/playClick/currentApp
+   - can play Chrome or other apps as a user surface
+
+6. Chrome/browser strategy
+   - internal WebView for controllable programmable pages
+   - real Chrome can be launched/opened/played when Google/session/site compatibility matters
+   - openIn('com.android.chrome', url)
+   - no attempt to steal Chrome session; use Chrome naturally through UI automation if desired
+
+7. Native fetch/network
+   - mirror.fetch(url, method, headersJson, body, timeout, cleanTracking, sensitiveUnused, maxBytes)
+   - native HTTP avoids normal browser CORS
+   - HTML controls all headers; Cookie/Authorization are not blocked
+   - optional tracking query cleanup
+
+8. Shell/process/root/ADB
+   - one-shot sh
+   - persistent process sessions through exec process start/write/read/kill
+   - sys(mode, cmd, input, timeout) supports sh, su/root, adb, or custom prefix
+   - app cannot grant root/ADB, but is open to use them when available
+
+9. USB direction
+   - usb() returns UsbManager handle
+   - usbList() lists devices
+   - full Bugjaeger-like HTML still needs USB permission/bulk endpoint/ADB protocol helpers or HTML/dex implementation
+
+10. Media/video/screen/frame data
+   - mp(cbId) asks MediaProjection permission
+   - mpStart(w,h,dpi)
+   - mpFrame/mpFrameEx crop/downscale frame
+   - formats: jpg, png, gray, rgba/raw, filejpg/filepng/filegray/fileraw
+   - mpStreamStart callback frame stream
+   - framePipeStart/framePipeInfo/framePipeStop ring-buffer data plane for real-time use
+   - frame pipe writes latest frames to rotating cache files plus meta.json
+   - intended for WebGPU/WASM/JS vision loops and screen/video sharing transports
+
+11. Video chat / screen share
+   - native does not implement WebRTC library
+   - WebView gets getUserMedia permissions
+   - HTML implements RTCPeerConnection/signaling/UI
+   - mirror provides native fetch for signaling if needed
+   - mirror provides MediaProjection frame pipe for screen share / remote help
+
+12. Sensors/speech/notifications
+   - sensor(type, delay, cbId), sensorStop
+   - heart rate path via sensor type 21 if hardware/permission support exists
+   - say via TTS
+   - listen via SpeechRecognizer
+   - notify
+   - notification listener queue
+
+13. Storage/profile
+   - save/load/rm/keys small SharedPreferences storage
+   - fw/fr file storage, optionally gzip/base64
+   - zip/unzip helpers
+   - intended for profiles, timers, action logs, learning memory
+
+14. Scheduling/lifetime
+   - alarm(ms, payload)
+   - foreground BridgeService fg(true/false)
+   - lastIntent payload
+   - MainReceiver route back into Main
+
+15. Self-modification / performance shedding
+   - boot HTML can be replaced/reset
+   - close pages
+   - stop frame pipes
+   - stop MediaProjection/streams
+   - stop sensors
+   - kill processes
+   - turn foreground service off
+
+Important user clarifications already resolved
+---------------------------------------------
+
+- `/1` must stay unchanged. Work/suggestions go under `/2`.
+- No old `K` bridge compatibility. Use `mirror` only.
+- Default HTML should stay minimal; do not replace it with a heavy console.
+- The default HTML should be an open fallback literal, roughly:
+      html = editable ?: """<!DOCTYPE html>...full fallback code..."""
+- Logical condensation means semantic unification, not one-letter minification.
+- “resolve intents” meant understand the user's intent, not Android Intent only.
+- HTML should decide policy and power usage.
+- Do not block sensitive headers; app/user/HTML decides.
+- WebRTC should mostly be HTML; native gives permissions and screen/frame/data powers.
+- Chrome should be playable as an external app surface, especially for Google account/session flows, not internally copied/stolen.
+- Root/ADB should be optional power backends: the app can use them if the user/device grants/provides them.
+
+Docs already added
+------------------
+
+MIRROR_JUMPSTART.txt is the long conceptual guide and examples.
+MIRROR_PARTS_CHEATSHEET.txt is the practical method index.
+This HANDOFF.txt is the continuity summary.
+
+Read these before continuing:
+
+    android-apps/app_realizer/2/MIRROR_JUMPSTART.txt
+    android-apps/app_realizer/2/MIRROR_PARTS_CHEATSHEET.txt
+    android-apps/app_realizer/2/HANDOFF.txt
+
+Known rough areas / next best improvements
+------------------------------------------
+
+The current v2 is powerful but still rough. The next useful work is unification and
+ergonomics, not just adding random wrappers.
+
+Highest-value next tasks:
+
+1. Runtime manager
+   Add something like:
+
+       mirror.exec({ runtime:{ stats:true } })
+       mirror.exec({ runtime:{ stop:['framePipes','sensors','processes'], closePages:['vision'] } })
+
+   It should report pages, processes, sensors, frame pipes, projection state,
+   foreground state, handles, memory-ish stats, event queue sizes.
+
+2. Backend auto-routing
+   Actions should optionally choose backend:
+
+       { tap:[100,200], backend:'auto' }
+
+   Auto should try configured root/adb/accessibility routes depending available powers.
+   This turns root/ADB from raw shell access into a practical input backend.
+
+3. Unified event bus
+   Normalize all events into one event stream with ids/cursors:
+
+       { id, time, source:'sensor|speech|notification|accessibility|process|frame|intent', type, data }
+
+   Then mirror.events({ since, sources, max }) can be canonical.
+
+4. Permission/power negotiation
+   Add:
+
+       mirror.exec({ require:['accessibility','mediaProjection','microphone','notifications','bodySensors','root?','adb?'] })
+
+   Return missing capabilities and fix paths/settings actions/calls.
+
+5. File/binary primitives
+   Add binary-safe fileRead/fileWrite/fileList/fileInfo/fileDelete.
+   Needed for models, WASM, frame slots, ADB payloads, resources.
+
+6. USB bulk primitives
+   For Bugjaeger-like HTML:
+       usbRequest, usbOpen, usbBulkRead, usbBulkWrite, usbClose
+
+7. Frame pipe robustness
+   Atomic meta writes, dropped frame count, average FPS, latest seq, cleanup old files,
+   stride/format details for raw/gray, optional fixed names.
+
+8. Better structured exec return values
+   `exec` should normalize old OK/E/V/P results into structured JSON objects.
+
+9. Optional JS SDK string
+   Do not bloat default UI, but consider an editable/stored SDK string retrievable by:
+       mirror.sdkGet(), mirror.sdkSet(...)
+   HTML can opt into it.
+
+10. Page roles and message bus
+   Pages should have roles: main, worker, vision, remote, tool.
+   Add page metadata and page-to-page message/event support.
+
+Readiness assessment
+--------------------
+
+As of this handoff:
+
+- Raw capability coverage: strong (~8.5/10)
+- Ease/comfort for HTML authors: moderate (~6/10)
+- Real-time ML/game loops: improved with frame pipe, but needs robustness (~7/10)
+- Universal platform polish: not done (~6/10)
+
+The code gives HTML paths to almost all requested domains, but needs better unified
+runtime management and structured event/permission systems to feel truly comfortable.
+
+Potential risk/compile notes
+----------------------------
+
+This repository sandbox did not have an Android SDK/Gradle/emulator available, so the
+v2 source was not compiled here. Treat it as a source-level proposal that should be
+checked inside the real Android project.
+
+Likely things to verify in a real build:
+
+- compileSdk high enough for newer permissions/foreground service types
+- MediaProjection APIs and foreground-service requirements on target Android version
+- Accessibility screenshot API requires Android 11+
+- WebView WebGPU support depends on device/System WebView
+- POST_NOTIFICATIONS runtime permission on Android 13+
+- BODY_SENSORS runtime permission for heart rate
+- exact alarm policy/settings on newer Android
+- notification listener service requires user enablement
+- accessibility service requires user enablement
+- USB host permissions need additional flow for opening devices
+
+Branch/PR status
+----------------
+
+Work was committed and pushed to branch:
+
+    arena/019fbe59-test19
+
+PR opened:
+
+    https://github.com/HalitZiyaKARTAL/test19/pull/1
+
+The user may ignore the PR and manually copy files from `/2` if preferred.
+
+Closing note
+------------
+
+If another agent continues later, remember the core design principle:
+
+    Do not turn Kotlin into the app.
+    Keep Kotlin as mirror: a unified Android power kernel.
+    Let runtime HTML/JS/AI be the self-modifying app brain.

+MIRROR /0 KERNEL MAP
+====================
+
+This file explains the intended shape of /0 after the cleanup pass.
+
+First principles
+----------------
+
+mirror should be a small Android capability kernel, not the app itself.
+HTML/JS/AI is the app brain. Kotlin exposes primitive phone powers.
+
+The public bridge should stay conceptually small:
+
+  mirror.exec(json)        semantic transaction bus
+  mirror.execAsync(json, cb)
+  mirror.caps()            capability map
+  mirror.events(json)      event/state drain
+  mirror.run(text)         optional readable flow helper
+  mirror.gesture(json)     low-level multi-touch primitive
+  reflection handles       c/n/x/g/s/etc for escape hatch
+
+Everything else should be a thin wrapper or an implementation detail.
+
+Layer map
+---------
+
+1. Handle table
+   - Stores native objects as P<number> handles.
+   - Used by reflection and dynamic extension.
+
+2. Reflection
+   - Java/Kotlin class loading, method calls, fields, constructors, proxies.
+   - Escape hatch for things not covered by semantic exec.
+
+3. Exec transaction bus
+   - observe: screen/events/notifications/capabilities/intent
+   - act: tap/hold/swipe/gesture/open/launch/say/fetch/etc.
+   - remember: profile/file storage
+   - schedule: alarms
+   - process: shell sessions
+   - page: WebView pages/workers
+
+4. Gesture primitive
+   - Canonical macro building block.
+   - Kotlin should not own macro policy.
+   - HTML builds record/replay/learning on top.
+   - Stroke schema:
+       [
+         {"points":[[x,y],[x,y]], "start":0, "duration":300},
+         {"points":[[x,y]], "start":0, "duration":300}
+       ]
+   - Strokes with same start run together for multi-finger gestures.
+
+5. Observation
+   - Accessibility tree/events.
+   - Notification events.
+   - Intent payload.
+   - MediaProjection/frame pipe for pixels.
+
+6. Data plane
+   - Large/high-rate data should not go through exec JSON.
+   - Use framePipe/cache files for frames/video-ish data.
+   - Use process pipes/files for shell/binary data.
+
+7. Page manager
+   - Multiple WebViews for UI, hidden workers, model pages, remote-control pages.
+   - Main page should remain safe.
+
+8. Power backends
+   - Normal app APIs.
+   - Accessibility service.
+   - Chrome/external app playing.
+   - Shell/process.
+   - Optional su/root.
+   - Optional adb.
+   - USB manager and future USB bulk/ADB protocol helpers.
+
+9. Runtime safety
+   - /1 remains original.
+   - /0 is latest working state.
+   - /2 and /3 are frozen milestones.
+   - Accessibility kill sequence remains exact down/up/down/up.
+   - Avoid loose volume-count panic because normal media usage could trigger it.
+
+Cleanup decisions in this pass
+------------------------------
+
+Removed/de-emphasized from /0:
+
+- one-letter q mini-eval
+- one-letter e/r aliases
+- B alias for batch
+- macro wrapper logic
+
+Kept:
+
+- flow/run for readable human scripts
+- batch for compact machine scripts if needed
+- gesture as minimal kernel primitive for all macro systems
+- direct low-level reflection helpers as escape hatch
+
+Why macro wrapper was removed
+-----------------------------
+
+Macro recording/replay/learning belongs in HTML. Native only needs to execute
+generic gestures accurately. This keeps mirror from becoming a bloated macro app.
+
+HTML can implement:
+
+  tap(x,y,ms) -> [{points:[[x,y]], start:0, duration:ms}]
+  hold(x,y,ms) -> same with longer duration
+  swipe(...) -> path points
+  replay(recording) -> schedule mirror.gesture(...) calls
+
+Next recommended simplifications
+--------------------------------
+
+1. Normalize exec return values into structured JSON.
+2. Runtime manager: stats/stop/profile for pages/processes/sensors/frame pipes.
+3. Unified event bus with cursors and source/type/data events.
+4. Backend auto-routing for gestures: accessibility/root/adb.
+5. Binary file primitives and USB bulk primitives.
+6. Consolidate direct wrappers so most are thin calls into exec modules.

+MIRROR /0 MANIFEST-FIRST FRACTAL DESIGN
+=======================================
+
+User intent
+-----------
+
+The desired design is close to:
+
+    adding/changing manifest capability should usually be enough
+
+Meaning Kotlin should not need a new hand-written feature for every Android power.
+The manifest declares what the app is allowed/able to do. mirror exposes generic
+ways for HTML to discover, request, route, and use those declared powers.
+
+This is the stronger version of the kernel idea:
+
+    manifest = capability declaration
+    mirror = capability resolver / adapter graph
+    HTML = policy and app logic
+
+Goal
+----
+
+If later the app adds a permission, feature, service, receiver, provider, or intent
+filter in the manifest, HTML should usually be able to:
+
+1. see it in mirror.caps/resources
+2. request/enable it if needed
+3. get a generic root/handle/resource for it
+4. use it through reflection/generic ops/events
+
+without Kotlin adding a special method like mirror.doNewFeature().
+
+Fractal rule
+------------
+
+Every Android capability should map to the same shape:
+
+    declared -> discovered -> requested/enabled -> opened/rooted -> read/write/call -> events -> closed
+
+Examples:
+
+    CAMERA permission
+      declared in manifest
+      caps shows permission state
+      request permission generically
+      HTML uses WebView getUserMedia or CameraManager via root/reflection
+
+    BODY_SENSORS permission
+      declared in manifest
+      caps shows permission state and sensor list
+      request permission generically
+      HTML opens sensor resource by type
+      events deliver values
+
+    NotificationListener service
+      declared in manifest
+      caps says settings-required/enabled
+      user opens settings
+      service events flow into event bus
+
+    Accessibility service
+      declared in manifest/xml
+      caps says enabled/disabled/fix settings action
+      service root exposed
+      gestures/screen/events available
+
+    USB host feature
+      declared in manifest
+      caps shows UsbManager/connected devices
+      generic USB permission/open/read/write should be adapter-level
+
+    New future permission
+      declared in manifest
+      caps lists it automatically from PackageInfo.requestedPermissions
+      requestPermissions can ask for it by string
+      HTML uses reflection/system roots to reach APIs
+
+Manifest-first primitives needed
+--------------------------------
+
+1. Manifest introspection
+
+mirror should be able to report:
+
+    requested permissions
+    permission grant states
+    declared features
+    activities/services/receivers/providers
+    intent filters when practical
+    foreground service types if practical
+    package name/version
+
+HTML can then see what the native APK declares.
+
+2. Generic runtime permission requester
+
+Instead of adding requestCamera(), requestAudio(), requestSensors(), etc.:
+
+    mirror.permissionRequest(JSON.stringify([
+      "android.permission.CAMERA",
+      "android.permission.RECORD_AUDIO",
+      "android.permission.BODY_SENSORS"
+    ]), cbId)
+
+Results go to event bus/callback.
+
+3. Generic settings/fix actions
+
+For powers that are not normal runtime permissions:
+
+    accessibility service
+    notification listener
+    exact alarms
+    overlay permission
+    battery optimization
+    usage stats
+    manage external storage
+
+caps should return fix paths:
+
+    { type:"settings", action:"android.settings.ACCESSIBILITY_SETTINGS" }
+
+HTML decides when to open them.
+
+4. Generic system service root
+
+Instead of one helper per service:
+
+    mirror.root("window")
+    mirror.root("sensor")
+    mirror.root("usb")
+    mirror.root("media_projection")
+    mirror.root("notification")
+    mirror.root("package")
+    mirror.root("content")
+
+This can call getSystemService generically and return a handle.
+
+5. Generic activity result / intent result bridge
+
+Many Android powers require starting an intent and receiving a result:
+
+    MediaProjection consent
+    file picker
+    document tree picker
+    account/login flows
+    settings screens
+
+Use one generic mechanism:
+
+    mirror.activityStartForResult(intentSpec, requestId)
+
+and route results into the event bus:
+
+    { source:"activityResult", requestId, resultCode, dataHandle }
+
+6. Generic broadcast receiver path
+
+Manifest receivers or dynamic receivers should route broadcasts to the event bus.
+A generic receiver can report:
+
+    action
+    extras
+    data URI
+    package
+    timestamp
+
+Then adding a receiver action in manifest becomes useful to HTML without a custom method.
+
+7. Generic content/file URI path
+
+ContentResolver should be reachable as a root and have binary-safe primitives:
+
+    openInputStream(uri)
+    openOutputStream(uri)
+    query(uri,...)
+
+This supports document providers, media, downloads, shared files, etc.
+
+8. Generic resource registry
+
+Every opened service/path/session becomes a resource:
+
+    permission:CAMERA
+    service:accessibility
+    service:notificationListener
+    system:usb
+    system:sensor
+    activityResult:mediaProjection
+    page:main
+    process:sh1
+    frame:vision
+
+Same lifecycle:
+
+    info/read/write/events/close
+
+What still needs native adapters
+--------------------------------
+
+Manifest-first does not mean Kotlin never changes.
+Some Android powers need native lifecycle/callback classes that HTML cannot create
+from pure JS easily.
+
+These deserve generic adapters, not product-specific wrappers:
+
+1. AccessibilityService adapter
+   Needed for gestures, screen tree/events, overlay windows, key kill switch.
+
+2. NotificationListenerService adapter
+   Needed for notification events.
+
+3. MediaProjection adapter
+   Needed for screen capture and frame pipes.
+
+4. Foreground Service adapter
+   Needed for long-running operation.
+
+5. Generic BroadcastReceiver adapter
+   Needed for intent/broadcast events.
+
+6. USB adapter
+   Needed for permission/open/bulk endpoints if Bugjaeger-like HTML is desired.
+
+7. Runtime permission adapter
+   Needed because requestPermissions returns asynchronously.
+
+But once these generic adapters exist, adding a new use case should mostly be
+manifest/config/HTML, not new Kotlin feature methods.
+
+Desired caps() shape
+--------------------
+
+caps should become a manifest/capability graph, not only booleans.
+
+Example:
+
+{
+  "package": "a.htmlapprealizer",
+  "permissions": {
+    "android.permission.CAMERA": {"declared":true,"granted":true,"requestable":true},
+    "android.permission.BODY_SENSORS": {"declared":true,"granted":false,"requestable":true},
+    "android.permission.PACKAGE_USAGE_STATS": {"declared":true,"granted":false,"fix":{"settings":"android.settings.USAGE_ACCESS_SETTINGS"}}
+  },
+  "services": {
+    "accessibility": {"declared":true,"enabled":false,"fix":{"settings":"android.settings.ACCESSIBILITY_SETTINGS"}},
+    "notificationListener": {"declared":true,"enabled":false,"fix":{"settings":"android.settings.ACTION_NOTIFICATION_LISTENER_SETTINGS"}}
+  },
+  "roots": ["activity","application","package","content","sensor","usb","window","notification","mediaProjection"],
+  "routes": {
+    "screen": ["accessibilityTree","accessibilityScreenshot","mediaProjectionFramePipe","adbScreencap?","rootScreencap?"],
+    "input": ["accessibilityGesture","adbInput?","rootInput?"],
+    "compute": ["pageJS","wasm/html-test","webgpu/html-test","process","dex","nativeLib"]
+  }
+}
+
+This lets HTML choose routes instead of Kotlin deciding.
+
+Generic op target model
+-----------------------
+
+Manifest-first works best with addressable targets:
+
+    cap:android.permission.CAMERA
+    service:accessibility
+    service:notificationListener
+    system:usb
+    system:sensor
+    system:mediaProjection
+    content:content://...
+    intent:android.settings.ACCESSIBILITY_SETTINGS
+    page:main
+    process:sh1
+    frame:vision
+    input:auto
+    handle:42
+
+Uniform verbs:
+
+    get/read/open/write/call/close/set
+
+Examples:
+
+    {"op":"read", "target":"cap:*"}
+    {"op":"open", "target":"intent:android.settings.ACCESSIBILITY_SETTINGS"}
+    {"op":"open", "target":"system:usb"}
+    {"op":"call", "target":"handle:42", "data":{"method":"foo","args":[]}}
+    {"op":"write", "target":"input:auto", "data":{"gesture":[...]}}
+
+Adding a manifest permission then affects caps/root availability, not public API shape.
+
+Design smell checklist
+----------------------
+
+Bad signs:
+
+    adding a new permission requires a new mirror.somePermissionFeature() method
+    adding a new service requires a separate event queue
+    adding a new data source requires a separate callback convention
+    adding a new backend requires rewriting HTML actions
+    adding a new capability requires editing many switch statements
+
+Good signs:
+
+    new permission appears automatically in caps
+    new service emits into same event bus
+    new long-running thing appears in resources
+    new data source uses pipe/file pattern
+    new backend implements same input/action interface
+    HTML can discover and choose it
+
+Practical next code steps
+-------------------------
+
+1. Add manifest/capability introspection to caps().
+2. Add generic root(name) system-service getter.
+3. Add generic permissionRequest(permissionsJson, cbId).
+4. Add resource registry for pages/processes/framePipes/sensors/projection.
+5. Add event bus IDs/cursors and route all service callbacks there.
+6. Add generic op dispatcher with op+target+data.
+7. Keep old direct helpers as wrappers while the op system matures.
+
+This would move mirror closer to the desired state where manifest + HTML is often
+enough, and Kotlin only needs new code when a genuinely new Android adapter/lifecycle
+is required.

+App Realizer /4 milestone
+=========================
+
+This milestone was created to start moving /0 toward the manifest-first/fractal kernel design.
+
+Focus:
+- resource introspection
+- manifest/capability introspection
+- generic root handles
+- generic permission request path
+- generic stop/cleanup targets
+- exec integration for resources/manifest/require/stop
+
+/1 original unchanged.
+/2 earlier preserved proposal.
+/3 milestone with testable mirror overlay HTML.
+/0 remains continuous latest working state.
+/4 is this deeper manifest/resource-kernel milestone.

+MIRROR APP REALIZER V2 — JUMPSTART DESIGN NOTES
+=================================================
+
+This document describes the proposed `android-apps/app_realizer/2` snapshot.
+`/1` is intentionally left unchanged. `/2` is a suggestion/prototype for a
+more universal Android runtime kernel where HTML/JS is the app layer and the
+native Kotlin side is only the phone capability bridge.
+
+Core idea
+---------
+
+Install the Android app once. After that, pasted or loaded HTML can become many
+kinds of apps without changing the APK:
+
+- browser-like runtime
+- AI automation tool
+- app tester
+- video chat / remote help surface
+- phone controlled from PC
+- local WebGPU/WASM model runner
+- game/app vision/action loop
+- reminder/profile/scheduler
+- speech assistant
+- Termux-like shell/process bridge
+- root/ADB powered tool if those powers are granted
+- USB/ADB/Bugjaeger-like tool if HTML or loaded dex implements the protocol
+
+The bridge object exposed to JavaScript is:
+
+    mirror
+
+The intended primary interface is unified/logically-condensed:
+
+    mirror.exec(JSON.stringify({...}))
+    mirror.execAsync(JSON.stringify({...}), callbackId)
+    mirror.caps()
+    mirror.events(JSON.stringify({...}))
+
+Older/direct helper methods exist too, but future HTML should prefer `mirror.exec`
+plus a small JS SDK wrapper.
+
+Mental model
+------------
+
+Think of the app as:
+
+    Kotlin/native = capability kernel / syscall surface
+    WebView HTML/JS = userland app logic
+    optional AI/PC = planner/controller
+    Android/Chrome/root/ADB/shell/USB/sensors/media = power backends
+
+Do not put policy in Kotlin. Let HTML decide policy:
+
+- whether to use WebView, Chrome, shell, root, adb, accessibility, or USB
+- whether to send cookies/authorization/custom headers
+- whether to strip tracking parameters
+- whether to use MediaProjection frames or accessibility tree
+- whether to stop expensive frame loops
+- whether to run a hidden page, WebGPU worker, WASM worker, or process
+
+Kotlin provides broad, stable paths. HTML composes behavior.
+
+Default boot document
+---------------------
+
+The default HTML remains a minimal editor/realizer. In `/2/main.kt` it is shaped
+like this:
+
+    var editable: String? = null
+    var html = editable ?: """<!DOCTYPE html>...full fallback editor..."""
+    fun bootHtml() = html
+
+On startup, local saved override is loaded into `editable`, then `html` becomes:
+
+    html = editable ?: """fallback document literal"""
+
+So the fallback is open in the declaration. Runtime HTML/reflection can set the
+string if desired. The helper methods are:
+
+    mirror.boot(html, reload)
+    mirror.bootGet()
+    mirror.bootReset(reload)
+
+Minimal JS SDK suggestion
+-------------------------
+
+Paste this into any runtime HTML as a convenience layer:
+
+    const phone = {
+      raw: mirror,
+      exec(o) {
+        return JSON.parse(mirror.exec(JSON.stringify(o)));
+      },
+      execAsync(o, cbId) {
+        return mirror.execAsync(JSON.stringify(o), cbId);
+      },
+      caps() {
+        const r = mirror.caps();
+        return r.startsWith('V') ? JSON.parse(r.slice(1)) : { error: r };
+      },
+      events(max = 100) {
+        const r = mirror.events(JSON.stringify({ max }));
+        return r.startsWith('V') ? JSON.parse(r.slice(1)) : { error: r };
+      },
+      observe(...what) {
+        return this.exec({ observe: what.flat(), returnEvents: true });
+      },
+      act(...actions) {
+        return this.exec({ act: actions.flat() });
+      },
+      remember(file, value, compress = true) {
+        return this.exec({ remember: { file, value, compress } });
+      },
+      schedule(ms, payload) {
+        return this.exec({ schedule: { in: ms, payload } });
+      },
+      page(id, src, kind = 'html', visible = true) {
+        return this.exec({ page: { id, src, kind, visible } });
+      },
+      framePipe(config, cb = 0) {
+        const r = mirror.framePipeStart(JSON.stringify(config), cb);
+        return r.startsWith('V') ? JSON.parse(r.slice(1)) : { error: r };
+      },
+      mpFrame(config) {
+        const r = mirror.mpFrameEx(
+          config.l ?? -1, config.t ?? -1, config.r ?? -1, config.b ?? -1,
+          config.maxW ?? 320, config.quality ?? 60, config.format ?? 'jpg'
+        );
+        return r.startsWith('V') ? JSON.parse(r.slice(1)) : { error: r };
+      },
+      shell(cmd, timeout = 10000) {
+        const r = mirror.sh(cmd, '', timeout);
+        return r.startsWith('V') ? JSON.parse(r.slice(1)) : { error: r };
+      },
+      sys(mode, cmd, timeout = 10000) {
+        const r = mirror.sys(mode, cmd, '', timeout);
+        return r.startsWith('V') ? JSON.parse(r.slice(1)) : { error: r };
+      }
+    };
+
+Unified exec examples
+---------------------
+
+Observe screen/events/notifications/capabilities:
+
+    const state = phone.exec({
+      capabilities: true,
+      observe: ['screen', 'events', 'notifications', 'intent'],
+      returnEvents: true
+    });
+
+Act on the phone:
+
+    phone.exec({
+      act: [
+        { tap: [540, 1200] },
+        { click: 'OK' },
+        { input: ['Search', 'hello world'] },
+        { say: 'Done' },
+        { notify: [1, 'Mirror', 'Action complete'] }
+      ]
+    });
+
+Remember profile:
+
+    phone.exec({
+      remember: {
+        file: 'profile.json.gz',
+        value: JSON.stringify({ goals: ['hydrate', 'sleep earlier'] }),
+        compress: true
+      }
+    });
+
+Schedule reminder:
+
+    phone.exec({
+      schedule: {
+        in: 3600000,
+        payload: 'drink water'
+      }
+    });
+
+Run a shell command:
+
+    phone.exec({
+      act: [
+        { shell: 'id; uname -a', timeout: 5000 }
+      ]
+    });
+
+Use optional root or adb power if available/granted:
+
+    phone.exec({
+      act: [
+        { sys: { mode: 'su', cmd: 'id', timeout: 5000 } },
+        { sys: { mode: 'adb', cmd: 'settings list global', timeout: 5000 } }
+      ]
+    });
+
+Multiple pages / tabs / workers
+-------------------------------
+
+`/2` adds multiple WebView/page support. This allows:
+
+- visible UI page
+- hidden vision/model worker page
+- remote bridge page
+- tool page
+- experimentation pages
+
+Open a visible page:
+
+    mirror.pageOpen('ui', '<!doctype html><h1>Hello</h1>', 'html', true);
+
+Open a hidden worker/model page:
+
+    mirror.pageOpen('vision', '<!doctype html><script>function run(){return 1}</script>', 'html', false);
+
+Evaluate JS inside a page:
+
+    window.onC = (id, msg) => console.log('callback', id, msg);
+    mirror.pageEval('vision', 'run()', 7);
+
+Switch visible page:
+
+    mirror.pageShow('ui');
+
+Close a page:
+
+    mirror.pageClose('vision');
+
+List pages:
+
+    const pages = JSON.parse(mirror.pageList().slice(1));
+
+Video chat shape
+----------------
+
+Native does not implement WebRTC itself. That should be HTML's job. Native gives
+HTML the powers it needs:
+
+- WebView camera/mic permission grant
+- geolocation permission grant
+- media playback without gesture
+- multiple windows
+- foreground service path
+- MediaProjection consent and frame pipe for screen sharing
+- native fetch for signaling if browser fetch/CORS is a problem
+
+Simple video chat HTML skeleton:
+
+    <!doctype html>
+    <video id="local" autoplay playsinline muted></video>
+    <video id="remote" autoplay playsinline></video>
+    <textarea id="signal"></textarea>
+    <button onclick="start()">start</button>
+    <script>
+    let pc, dc;
+    async function start() {
+      const stream = await navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({video:true, audio:true});
+      local.srcObject = stream;
+      pc = new RTCPeerConnection({iceServers:[{urls:'stun:stun.l.google.com:19302'}]});
+      stream.getTracks().forEach(t => pc.addTrack(t, stream));
+      pc.ontrack = e => remote.srcObject = e.streams[0];
+      dc = pc.createDataChannel('mirror');
+      dc.onmessage = e => {
+        // Optional: receive remote command packet and execute on phone.
+        try { dc.send(mirror.exec(e.data)); } catch (err) { dc.send(JSON.stringify({ok:false,error:String(err)})); }
+      };
+      pc.onicecandidate = () => signal.value = JSON.stringify(pc.localDescription);
+      const offer = await pc.createOffer();
+      await pc.setLocalDescription(offer);
+    }
+    async function accept(answerJson) {
+      await pc.setRemoteDescription(JSON.parse(answerJson));
+    }
+    </script>
+
+Screen-share / remote-help shape
+--------------------------------
+
+Use HTML WebRTC/DataChannel/WebSocket/etc. for transport. Use `mirror` only as
+power provider.
+
+1. Ask for screen-capture permission:
+
+    mirror.mp(10);
+
+2. After callback/user consent:
+
+    mirror.mpStart(0, 0, 0);
+
+3. Start efficient frame pipe:
+
+    const pipe = JSON.parse(mirror.framePipeStart(JSON.stringify({
+      l: 0, t: 0, r: 720, b: 1280,
+      maxW: 360,
+      quality: 55,
+      format: 'jpg',
+      fps: 15,
+      slots: 3
+    }), 0).slice(1));
+
+4. HTML sends latest frame through chosen transport:
+
+    async function sendLoop() {
+      const metaRes = await fetch(pipe.meta);
+      const meta = await metaRes.json();
+      const frameBuf = await fetch(meta.url).then(r => r.arrayBuffer());
+      // send frameBuf through WebRTC DataChannel, WebSocket, WebTransport, etc.
+      requestAnimationFrame(sendLoop);
+    }
+
+The app gives power to use WebRTC; HTML implements the networking/product logic.
+
+AI/game vision loop shape
+-------------------------
+
+For a local WebGPU/WASM model that watches a small window and acts:
+
+    mirror.mp(10);       // user grants once
+    mirror.mpStart(0,0,0);
+
+    const pipe = phone.framePipe({
+      l: 100, t: 200, r: 500, b: 600,
+      maxW: 128,
+      format: 'gray',
+      fps: 30,
+      slots: 3
+    });
+
+    async function loop() {
+      const meta = await fetch(pipe.meta).then(r => r.json());
+      const gray = await fetch(meta.url).then(r => r.arrayBuffer());
+
+      // Feed gray bytes to JS/WASM/WebGPU model.
+      const action = await model.decide(gray, meta.w, meta.h);
+
+      // Execute selected action.
+      phone.act(action);
+
+      // Learn from feedback/events.
+      const feedback = phone.observe('events', 'screen');
+      model.learn(feedback);
+
+      requestAnimationFrame(loop);
+    }
+
+If the game/content is inside the same WebView, do not use mirror for visual data.
+Read the canvas/video directly in JS for maximum speed. Use mirror only for phone
+I/O, memory, fetch, shell, etc.
+
+Chrome/app playing shape
+------------------------
+
+If a site works better in Chrome or has an existing login/session, use real Chrome
+as a surface and let mirror be eyes/hands:
+
+    phone.exec({ act: [
+      { openIn: { pkg: 'com.android.chrome', uri: 'https://accounts.google.com' } },
+      { waitFor: { q: 'Google', ms: 5000 } }
+    ]});
+
+Then use:
+
+- accessibility tree (`observe: ['screen']`)
+- frame pipe (`mpFrame`/`framePipe`)
+- click/type/swipe actions
+- learning logs
+
+This does not steal Chrome data. It drives Chrome as the user would, using whatever
+state Chrome already has.
+
+Native fetch / CORS / headers
+-----------------------------
+
+`mirror.fetch` performs native HTTP outside browser CORS. HTML controls headers.
+No special filtering is imposed by native policy.
+
+    const r = mirror.fetch(
+      'https://example.com/api?utm_source=x',
+      'POST',
+      JSON.stringify({
+        'User-Agent': 'mirror-runtime',
+        'Authorization': 'Bearer ...',
+        'Cookie': '...',
+        'Origin': 'https://desired-origin.example',
+        'Referer': 'https://desired-referer.example'
+      }),
+      JSON.stringify({hello:'world'}),
+      15000,
+      true,   // clean tracking params if desired
+      true,   // retained arg; native does not block sensitive headers
+      10_000_000
+    );
+
+Use this for signaling, model/resource downloads, API access, proxy-like behavior,
+or pages where browser CORS is a blocker.
+
+Shell/process/root/ADB shape
+----------------------------
+
+One-shot shell:
+
+    const r = phone.shell('id; ls -la /sdcard', 5000);
+
+Persistent process:
+
+    let start = phone.exec({ process: { start: 'sh', cwd: '/sdcard' } });
+    // Suppose it returns process id 1.
+    phone.exec({ process: { id: 1, write: 'echo hi\n' } });
+    let out = phone.exec({ process: { read: 1, clear: true } });
+    phone.exec({ process: { kill: 1 } });
+
+Optional root/ADB power:
+
+    phone.sys('su', 'id; input tap 100 200', 5000);
+    phone.sys('adb', 'shell input tap 100 200', 5000);
+
+The app cannot grant root/ADB. It is open to using those backends when available.
+
+USB / Bugjaeger-like direction
+------------------------------
+
+Current proposal includes:
+
+    mirror.usb()
+    mirror.usbList()
+
+`mirror.usb()` returns a UsbManager handle for reflection/dex use. `usbList()` gives
+a JSON summary of connected USB devices. A full Bugjaeger-like tool would still need
+HTML/WASM/dex code for USB permission, endpoint claiming, and ADB protocol. The
+kernel is open to that direction but not a full ADB client by itself.
+
+Self-modification / removing lag
+--------------------------------
+
+HTML can dynamically reduce load:
+
+- stop frame pipe: `mirror.framePipeStop(id)` or `mirror.framePipeStop(0)`
+- stop stream: `mirror.mpStreamStop()`
+- stop projection: `mirror.mpStop()`
+- stop sensor: `mirror.sensorStop(id)`
+- close page: `mirror.pageClose(id)`
+- kill process: `mirror.exec({process:{kill:id}})`
+- change boot document: `mirror.boot(html, true)`
+- reset boot document: `mirror.bootReset(true)`
+
+A future cleanup pass should add a single runtime manager:
+
+    mirror.exec({ runtime: { stats: true } })
+    mirror.exec({ runtime: { stop: ['framePipes','sensors','processes'] } })
+
+Important current gaps
+----------------------
+
+The `/2` proposal is powerful but still rough. Remaining high-value improvements:
+
+1. Unified runtime manager for stats/stop/profile.
+2. Unified event bus with cursors for all events.
+3. Backend auto-routing for actions: accessibility/root/adb.
+4. Runtime permission/fix negotiation.
+5. Binary file primitives for models/frame data/USB/ADB.
+6. USB bulk read/write primitives for true Bugjaeger-like HTML.
+7. Better structured JSON return format for `exec` results.
+8. Better page metadata/roles for worker/UI/remote/model pages.
+9. Frame pipe robustness: atomic metadata, cleanup, dropped frame counts, FPS stats.
+10. Optional JS SDK string stored/editable like boot document.
+
+Recommended HTML strategy
+-------------------------
+
+- Put intelligence/policy in HTML.
+- Use `mirror.exec` for small command/control transactions.
+- Use frame pipes or files for high-rate data.
+- Use WebView-native canvas/WebGPU directly when content is inside WebView.
+- Use Chrome/app automation when external app/browser compatibility is needed.
+- Use root/ADB/shell only when user/device grants that power.
+- Keep model/profile/action logs compressed in files.
+- Close pages, sensors, processes, and frame pipes when not needed.
+
+The intended final feeling is:
+
+    mirror = phone power kernel
+    HTML = self-modifying app/AI/runtime
+    Android/Chrome/root/ADB/shell/USB/media/sensors = optional backends

new file mode 100644

+++ b/android-apps/app_realizer/4/MIRROR_PARTS_CHEATSHEET.txt

+MIRROR PARTS CHEATSHEET
+=======================
+
+This is a practical index for the `/2` proposal. Use `mirror.*` from JavaScript.
+Most methods return old compact strings:
+
+    OK          success/no value
+    E:...       error
+    V...        primitive/string JSON value follows after V
+    P<number>   native object handle
+
+The newer preferred API `mirror.exec(...)` returns JSON directly.
+
+Primary unified API
+-------------------
+
+mirror.exec(jsonString)
+    Runs a unified transaction.
+
+    Example:
+        mirror.exec(JSON.stringify({
+          capabilities: true,
+          observe: ['screen','events','notifications'],
+          act: [ { tap:[100,200] }, { say:'done' } ],
+          returnEvents: true
+        }))
+
+mirror.execAsync(jsonString, cbId)
+    Runs exec on a background thread and calls window.onC(cbId, result).
+
+mirror.caps()
+    Returns V + capability JSON.
+
+mirror.events(jsonString)
+    Returns V + event state JSON.
+
+mirror.run(text), mirror.e(text), mirror.r(text), mirror.flow(text)
+    Human-readable flow commands. Example:
+        mirror.run(`
+        launch com.android.chrome
+        waitFor Google
+        playClick Search
+        `)
+
+Boot document
+-------------
+
+mirror.boot(html, reload)
+    Sets editable boot HTML. Persists in local preferences. If reload=true, loads it.
+
+mirror.bootGet()
+    Returns V + current boot HTML.
+
+mirror.bootReset(reload)
+    Clears local override and resets to fallback literal.
+
+Java/Kotlin reflection
+----------------------
+
+mirror.c(className)
+    Class.forName / primitive/array aware. Returns P handle.
+
+mirror.cl()
+    Returns app classloader handle.
+
+mirror.c2(loaderHandle, className)
+    Load class through a classloader handle.
+
+mirror.n(className, jsonArgs)
+    Construct object.
+
+mirror.x(handle, methodName, jsonArgs)
+    Invoke method.
+
+mirror.u(handle, methodName, jsonArgs, cbId)
+    Invoke and callback.
+
+mirror.g(handle, field)
+    Get field.
+
+mirror.s(handle, field, jsonValue)
+    Set field.
+
+mirror.a(typeName, length)
+    New array.
+
+mirror.p(interfaceName, callbackId)
+    Create Java proxy for interface; callbacks go to window.onL.
+
+mirror.kc(className), mirror.kco(handle), mirror.kinfo(handle), mirror.jinfo(handle)
+    Kotlin/Java class handles and info.
+
+mirror.type(handle), mirror.get(handle), mirror.str(value), mirror.del(handle)
+    Handle helpers.
+
+Pages / tabs / workers
+----------------------
+
+mirror.pageOpen(id, src, kind, visible)
+    kind: html, url, base64.
+
+mirror.pageEval(id, js, cbId)
+    Evaluate JS in page, callback result.
+
+mirror.pageShow(id)
+    Show a page and hide others.
+
+mirror.pageClose(id)
+    Destroy page.
+
+mirror.pageList()
+    Returns V + JSON array of page ids.
+
+Example:
+    mirror.pageOpen('worker', '<script>function f(){return 5}</script>', 'html', false)
+    mirror.pageEval('worker', 'f()', 8)
+
+Accessibility / app playing
+---------------------------
+
+mirror.svc()
+    Returns AccessibilityService handle if enabled.
+
+mirror.currentApp()
+    Returns current active package from accessibility root.
+
+mirror.waitFor(query, ms)
+    Wait until a screen node contains query.
+
+mirror.playClick(query, ms)
+    Wait then click node containing query.
+
+Via service reflection or exec actions:
+    tap [x,y]
+    swipe [x1,y1,x2,y2,ms]
+    click query
+    input [query,text]
+    back/home/recents/notifications
+
+Example:
+    mirror.exec(JSON.stringify({act:[
+      {launch:'com.android.chrome'},
+      {waitFor:{q:'Google',ms:5000}},
+      {playClick:{q:'Search',ms:5000}}
+    ]}))
+
+Intents / browser / Chrome
+--------------------------
+
+mirror.open(uri)
+    ACTION_VIEW uri.
+
+mirror.openIn(pkg, uri)
+    Open URI targeted to package, e.g. Chrome.
+
+mirror.launch(packageName)
+    Launch app package.
+
+mirror.share(text, mime)
+    Android share sheet.
+
+mirror.choose(action, uri, mime, extrasJson)
+    Android chooser.
+
+mirror.resolveIntent(action, uri, mime, pkg, cls, extras, cats, flags)
+    Returns matching activities.
+
+mirror.canIntent(...)
+    Returns whether intent can resolve.
+
+mirror.startEx(action, uri, mime, pkg, cls, extras, cats, flags)
+    Full intent start.
+
+Native fetch / CORS helper
+--------------------------
+
+mirror.fetch(url, method, headersJson, body, timeoutMs, cleanTracking, sensitiveUnused, maxBytes)
+    Returns V + JSON:
+        { url, code, type, headers, text, base64 }
+
+Headers are controlled by HTML. Cookie/Authorization are not blocked by native.
+
+Example:
+    mirror.fetch('https://api.example.com','GET','{"Authorization":"Bearer x"}','',15000,true,true,2000000)
+
+Shell / process / root / adb
+----------------------------
+
+mirror.sh(cmd, input, timeoutMs)
+    One-shot shell as app UID.
+
+mirror.sys(mode, cmd, input, timeoutMs)
+    mode: sh, su/root, adb, or custom prefix.
+
+Unified process sessions through exec:
+    mirror.exec({process:{start:'sh',cwd:'/sdcard'}})
+    mirror.exec({process:{id:1,write:'ls\n'}})
+    mirror.exec({process:{read:1,clear:true}})
+    mirror.exec({process:{kill:1}})
+
+MediaProjection / frames
+------------------------
+
+mirror.mp(cbId)
+    Request screen-capture consent. Callback gets result/data handle.
+
+mirror.mpStart(w,h,dpi)
+    Start MediaProjection after consent. Use 0,0,0 for display defaults.
+
+mirror.mpFrame(l,t,r,b,maxW,quality)
+    Old JPEG crop base64.
+
+mirror.mpFrameEx(l,t,r,b,maxW,quality,format)
+    Formats:
+        jpg, png, gray, rgba, raw, filejpg, filepng, filegray, fileraw
+
+mirror.mpStreamStart(configJson, cbId)
+    Push frame payloads to window.onC callback.
+
+mirror.mpStreamStop()
+    Stop callback stream.
+
+mirror.framePipeStart(configJson, cbId)
+    Start ring-buffer frame data plane. Config:
+        {l,t,r,b,maxW,quality,format,fps,slots}
+
+mirror.framePipeInfo(id)
+    Returns V + latest meta JSON.
+
+mirror.framePipeStop(id)
+    Stop one pipe. id=0 stops all.
+
+mirror.mpStop()
+    Stop stream/projection/resources.
+
+Recommended for ML:
+    format='gray', maxW small, fps 15-30, slots 3.
+
+Recommended for remote help/video share:
+    format='jpg' or 'filejpg', maxW 360-720, fps 10-20.
+
+Accessibility screenshot
+------------------------
+
+mirror.screenshot(l,t,r,b,maxW,quality)
+    Uses AccessibilityService screenshot API on Android 11+.
+    Useful for occasional screenshots if MediaProjection is not active.
+
+Sensors / speech / notifications
+--------------------------------
+
+mirror.sensor(type, delay, cbId)
+    Register Android sensor. Callback receives JSON values.
+    Heart rate is usually type 21 if supported and permission granted.
+
+mirror.sensorStop(id)
+    Stop sensor.
+
+mirror.say(text)
+    TextToSpeech.
+
+mirror.listen(cbId, lang, prompt)
+    SpeechRecognizer one-shot listen.
+
+mirror.stopListen()
+    Stop speech recognition.
+
+mirror.notify(id, title, text)
+    Post notification.
+
+mirror.notis(max)
+    Notification listener event queue.
+
+Storage / profile
+-----------------
+
+mirror.save(key, value, zip)
+    SharedPreferences small storage.
+
+mirror.load(key, zip)
+    Load small storage.
+
+mirror.rm(key)
+    Remove.
+
+mirror.keys(prefix)
+    List keys.
+
+mirror.zip(text), mirror.unzip(base64Gzip)
+    GZIP/Base64 helpers.
+
+mirror.fw(name, value, zip)
+    Write app file text, optionally gzipped.
+
+mirror.fr(name, zip)
+    Read app file text, optionally gunzipped.
+
+Clipboard / vibration / time
+----------------------------
+
+mirror.clip(text)
+mirror.getClip()
+mirror.vibe(ms)
+mirror.toast(text)
+mirror.now()
+mirror.later(ms, cbId, value)
+
+Foreground / lifetime
+---------------------
+
+mirror.fg(true)
+    Start foreground BridgeService.
+
+mirror.fg(false)
+    Stop foreground service.
+
+mirror.alarm(ms, value)
+    Schedule alarm to reopen app with payload.
+
+mirror.lastIntent()
+    Last intent payload JSON.
+
+USB
+---
+
+mirror.usb()
+    Returns UsbManager handle.
+
+mirror.usbList()
+    Returns V + connected USB device summary.
+
+Dynamic extension
+-----------------
+
+mirror.dex(path, optDir, libDir)
+    Load external dex/jar/apk through DexClassLoader. Returns loader handle.
+
+mirror.lib(pathOrName, abs)
+    System.load or System.loadLibrary.
+
+Common patterns
+---------------
+
+1. App tester:
+    mirror.exec({observe:['screen'],act:[{click:'OK'},{input:['Name','Alice']}],returnEvents:true})
+
+2. Chrome player:
+    mirror.exec({act:[{openIn:{pkg:'com.android.chrome',uri:'https://google.com'}},{waitFor:{q:'Google',ms:5000}}]})
+
+3. Vision AI:
+    mirror.mp(1); mirror.mpStart(0,0,0);
+    const pipe = mirror.framePipeStart(JSON.stringify({l:0,t:0,r:720,b:1280,maxW:128,format:'gray',fps:30,slots:3}),0)
+
+4. Video chat:
+    HTML uses getUserMedia + RTCPeerConnection. mirror provides camera/mic permission and optional screen frame pipe.
+
+5. PC bridge:
+    HTML transport receives JSON command packets, runs mirror.exec(packet), returns result.
+
+6. Termux-like:
+    Use process sessions for persistent shell, sys('su') for root if available, sys('adb') for adb if available.
+
+7. Self-modification:
+    mirror.boot(newHtml,true), pageOpen/pageClose, framePipeStop, sensorStop, procKill.

+MIRROR /0 OVERALL SIMPLIFICATION MAP
+====================================
+
+This is the broader cleanup direction for the whole app, not just macros.
+
+Core rule
+---------
+
+mirror should expose a few orthogonal primitives. Everything else should be HTML/JS
+or thin wrappers.
+
+Wrong shape:
+
+    mirror.tap()
+    mirror.hold()
+    mirror.swipe()
+    mirror.macro()
+    mirror.fetchThing()
+    mirror.chromeThing()
+    mirror.testThing()
+    mirror.videoThing()
+    ... many specific product features ...
+
+Right shape:
+
+    mirror.exec(transactionJson)
+    mirror.events(queryJson)
+    mirror.caps()
+    mirror.gesture(strokesJson)
+    mirror.reflect/call low-level escape hatch
+    mirror.data pipes/files for large data
+
+Everything else should be implemented as JS SDK sugar or exec modules.
+
+Overall map from first principles
+---------------------------------
+
+A phone runtime needs five universal paths:
+
+1. Observe
+   - screen tree
+   - screen pixels/frame pipe
+   - notifications
+   - sensors
+   - speech results
+   - intents/share/deeplinks
+   - process output
+   - page events
+
+2. Act
+   - gestures/touch
+   - text input
+   - open/launch/share/intent
+   - speak/notify/schedule
+   - shell/process/root/adb/usb when available
+
+3. Remember
+   - profile
+   - logs
+   - model/cache files
+   - compressed snapshots
+   - boot document
+
+4. Extend
+   - Java/Kotlin reflection
+   - classloader/dex
+   - native libraries
+   - pages/workers
+   - process tools
+
+5. Transport/Data
+   - WebView bridge for small commands
+   - frame pipes/files for large/high-rate data
+   - process pipes
+   - native fetch
+   - HTML-controlled WebRTC/WebSocket/etc.
+
+All implementation should map into these, not become separate feature families.
+
+Desired public surface
+----------------------
+
+Minimal public surface:
+
+    mirror.exec(reqJson)
+    mirror.execAsync(reqJson, cbId)
+    mirror.events(queryJson)
+    mirror.caps()
+    mirror.gesture(strokesJson)
+    mirror.auth(password)
+
+Useful low-level escape hatches:
+
+    mirror.c / n / x / g / s / p / del   reflection handles
+    mirror.sh/sys/process                power/process path
+    mirror.pageOpen/pageEval/...         page path if exec wrappers are not enough
+
+But the canonical app-facing path should be exec.
+
+Exec module shape
+-----------------
+
+A clean exec request should look like:
+
+    {
+      "observe": ["screen", "events", "notifications", "capabilities"],
+      "act": [
+        {"gesture": [...]},
+        {"open": "https://example.com"},
+        {"say": "done"}
+      ],
+      "remember": {"file":"profile.json.gz", "value":"...", "compress":true},
+      "schedule": {"in":3600000, "payload":"drink water"},
+      "process": {"start":"sh"},
+      "page": {"id":"worker", "src":"...", "kind":"html", "visible":false},
+      "returnEvents": true
+    }
+
+Return should become structured JSON:
+
+    {
+      "ok": true,
+      "state": {...},
+      "results": [
+        {"ok": true, "value": ...},
+        {"ok": false, "error": {"code":"...", "message":"...", "fix":...}}
+      ],
+      "events": {...}
+    }
+
+Current code still mixes legacy V/P/E/OK returns. The next cleanup should normalize
+inside exec while leaving old direct helpers for compatibility with experiments.
+
+Subsystem consolidation targets
+-------------------------------
+
+1. Input / actions
+   Keep one primitive:
+       gesture(strokesJson)
+   Build tap/hold/swipe/macro in HTML or exec sugar.
+   Later add backend routing:
+       backend: auto | accessibility | root | adb
+
+2. Frames / media
+   Keep:
+       mp consent/start/stop
+       frame one-shot
+       framePipe data-plane
+   Avoid many competing frame APIs in user-facing docs.
+   Large data should use framePipe/files, not bridge JSON.
+
+3. Process / shell
+   Canonical:
+       process run/start/write/read/kill
+   sh/sys become thin convenience wrappers.
+
+4. Pages
+   Canonical:
+       page create/eval/show/close/list
+   Add roles later: main, worker, vision, remote, tool.
+
+5. Network
+   Canonical:
+       fetch request object
+   HTML decides headers/tracking/cookies.
+
+6. Reflection
+   Keep direct methods as syscall escape hatch.
+   Add exec.reflect later for structured reflection.
+
+7. Storage
+   Canonical:
+       file read/write/list/info/delete
+       profile save/load compressed
+   Current fw/fr/save/load are partial; binary primitives still needed.
+
+8. Runtime manager
+   Needed:
+       runtime stats
+       runtime stop {framePipes,sensors,processes,pages,projection}
+       runtime profile low_power/performance
+
+9. Event bus
+   Needed:
+       one event queue with ids/cursors/source/type/data
+   Current accessibility/notification/speech/sensor/process events are still too split.
+
+10. Permissions/power negotiation
+   Needed:
+       require [accessibility, mediaProjection, notifications, sensors, root?, adb?]
+   Return missing/fix info.
+
+What was simplified already
+---------------------------
+
+- Removed q one-letter mini-eval from /0.
+- Removed e/r aliases.
+- Removed B alias.
+- Removed macro wrapper logic.
+- Kept gesture as the minimal macro-capable primitive.
+- Kept run/flow as readable human script path for now.
+
+Next code refactor recommendation
+---------------------------------
+
+Do not add more wrappers. Next real code pass should:
+
+1. Introduce a small result-normalization helper:
+       ok(value), err(code,message,fix), legacyToJson(result)
+
+2. Make exec call module functions:
+       execObserve, execAct, execProcess, execPage, execRemember, execSchedule
+
+3. Move direct helpers to thin wrappers around module functions where possible.
+
+4. Add runtime manager and event bus before adding more capabilities.
+
+5. Keep /0 as latest working state; copy /0 to /4 only when user declares milestone.

+class Main : Activity() {
+    val H = ArrayList<Any?>()
+    val MC = HashMap<String, List<Method>>()
+    val FC = HashMap<String, List<Field>>()
+    val CC = HashMap<String, List<Constructor<*>>>()
+    data class Proc(val p: Process, val out: ByteArrayOutputStream = ByteArrayOutputStream(), val err: ByteArrayOutputStream = ByteArrayOutputStream())
+    data class FramePipe(val id: Int, val dir: java.io.File, @Volatile var run: Boolean = true, var th: Thread? = null)
+    val PR = HashMap<Int, Proc>()
+    val FP = HashMap<Int, FramePipe>()
+    var prid = 1
+    var fpid = 1
+    val L = Array(3) { HashSet<String>() } // 0=Blacklist, 1=Graylist, 2=Whitelist
+    val D = HashSet<String>() // Domain whitelist
+    lateinit var root: FrameLayout
+    lateinit var w: WebView
+    lateinit var b: Button
+    val pages = HashMap<String, WebView>()
+    var m = 0 // Mode: 0=Visible, 1=Focus, 2=Open, 3=Perma
+    var s = true // Sandbox
+    var A = false // Auth status
+    var NET = false // Net cut
+    var PW = "" // Password
+    var tts: TextToSpeech? = null
+    var sr: SpeechRecognizer? = null
+    val SL = HashMap<Int, android.hardware.SensorEventListener>()
+    var sid = 1
+    var mpCb = 0
+    var permCb = 0
+    var mpCode = 0
+    var mpData: Intent? = null
+    var mp: MediaProjection? = null
+    var ir: ImageReader? = null
+    var vd: VirtualDisplay? = null
+    @Volatile var mpStream = false
+    var mpStreamThread: Thread? = null
+    var lastIntent = "{}"
+    val P: SharedPreferences by lazy { getSharedPreferences("Z", Context.MODE_PRIVATE) }
+
+    fun S(k: String, v: Any) = with(P.edit()) {
+        if (v is Boolean) putBoolean(k, v) else if (v is Int) putInt(k, v) else putString(k, v.toString())
+        apply()
+    }
+
+    var editable: String? = null // set by local storage, reflection, or HTML
+    var html = editable ?: """<!DOCTYPE html><title>App Realizer</title><style>body{margin:0;padding:4px;background:#111;font-family:monospace}div{min-height:40vh;background:#222;color:#eee;border:solid #444;padding:8px;white-space:pre-wrap;margin-bottom:4px;overflow:auto}button{border:0;background:#058;color:#fff;width:100%;height:5vh;font-size:2vh}</style><div id=i contenteditable="plaintext-only" oninput="c.textContent=i.textContent.length"></div><button onclick="var code=i.textContent;document.open();document.write(code);document.close()">Realize (<b id=c>0</b> chars)</button>""" // open fallback literal stays right here
+    fun bootHtml() = html
+    fun loadBoot() = runOnUiThread { w.loadDataWithBaseURL(null, bootHtml(), "text/html", "utf-8", null) }
+
+    fun mkWeb(id: String): WebView = WebView(this).apply {
+        settings.javaScriptEnabled = true
+        settings.domStorageEnabled = true
+        settings.databaseEnabled = true
+        settings.allowFileAccess = true
+        settings.allowContentAccess = true
+        settings.javaScriptCanOpenWindowsAutomatically = true
+        settings.setSupportMultipleWindows(true)
+        settings.mediaPlaybackRequiresUserGesture = false
+        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 21) settings.mixedContentMode = WebSettings.MIXED_CONTENT_ALWAYS_ALLOW
+        WebView.setWebContentsDebuggingEnabled(true)
+        addJavascriptInterface(this@Main, "mirror")
+        webChromeClient = object : WebChromeClient() {
+            override fun onPermissionRequest(request: PermissionRequest?) { runOnUiThread { request?.grant(request.resources) } }
+            override fun onGeolocationPermissionsShowPrompt(origin: String?, callback: GeolocationPermissions.Callback?) { callback?.invoke(origin, true, false) }
+            override fun onCreateWindow(view: WebView?, isDialog: Boolean, isUserGesture: Boolean, resultMsg: android.os.Message?): Boolean {
+                val nid = "page" + System.currentTimeMillis()
+                val nw = pageMake(nid, true)
+                (resultMsg?.obj as? WebView.WebViewTransport)?.webView = nw
+                resultMsg?.sendToTarget()
+                return true
+            }
+        }
+        webViewClient = object : WebViewClient() {
+            override fun onPageStarted(view: WebView?, url: String?, favicon: Bitmap?) { A = false }
+            override fun shouldInterceptRequest(view: WebView?, request: WebResourceRequest?): WebResourceResponse? {
+                val url = request?.url; val h = url?.host?.lowercase()
+                val allowed = !NET || url?.scheme in listOf("file", "data", "about", "blob") || (h != null && D.any { it.isNotEmpty() && (h == it || h.endsWith(".$it")) })
+                return if (allowed) null else WebResourceResponse("text/plain", "utf-8", ByteArrayInputStream(ByteArray(0)))
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    fun pageMake(id: String, visible: Boolean): WebView {
+        pages[id]?.let { return it }
+        val v = mkWeb(id); pages[id] = v
+        root.addView(v, FrameLayout.LayoutParams(ViewGroup.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT, ViewGroup.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT))
+        v.visibility = if (visible) View.VISIBLE else View.GONE
+        if (visible) pageShow(id)
+        return v
+    }
+
+    override fun onCreate(savedInstanceState: Bundle?) {
+        super.onCreate(savedInstanceState)
+        H.add(this) // H[0] is always the Activity Context
+        (0..2).forEach { i ->
+            P.getString("$i", "")!!.split(",").map { it.trim().lowercase() }.filter { it.isNotEmpty() }.forEach { L[i].add(it) }
+        }
+        m = P.all["M"].toString().toIntOrNull() ?: 0
+        s = P.getBoolean("S", true)
+        NET = P.getBoolean("N", true)
+        PW = P.getString("W", "") ?: ""
+        P.getString("D", "")!!.split(",").map { it.trim().lowercase() }.filter { it.isNotEmpty() }.forEach { D.add(it) }
+        editable = P.getString("BOOT_HTML", null); html = editable ?: """<!DOCTYPE html><title>App Realizer</title><style>body{margin:0;padding:4px;background:#111;font-family:monospace}div{min-height:40vh;background:#222;color:#eee;border:solid #444;padding:8px;white-space:pre-wrap;margin-bottom:4px;overflow:auto}button{border:0;background:#058;color:#fff;width:100%;height:5vh;font-size:2vh}</style><div id=i contenteditable="plaintext-only" oninput="c.textContent=i.textContent.length"></div><button onclick="var code=i.textContent;document.open();document.write(code);document.close()">Realize (<b id=c>0</b> chars)</button>"""
+
+        val f = FrameLayout(this); root = f
+        b = Button(this).apply {
+            text = "⚙"
+            setTextColor(Color.WHITE)
+            setBackgroundColor(Color.parseColor("#99000000"))
+            layoutParams = FrameLayout.LayoutParams(120, 120, Gravity.TOP or Gravity.END)
+            setOnClickListener { E() }
+        }
+        w = mkWeb("main")
+        pages["main"] = w
+        f.addView(w, FrameLayout.LayoutParams(ViewGroup.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT, ViewGroup.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT))
+        f.addView(b)
+        f.setOnApplyWindowInsetsListener { _, i ->
+            val p = b.layoutParams as FrameLayout.LayoutParams
+            val d = maxOf(p.width, p.height)
+            val r = if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.R) {
+                windowManager.currentWindowMetrics.bounds
+            } else {
+                val dm = resources.displayMetrics
+                Rect(0, 0, dm.widthPixels, dm.heightPixels)
+            }
+            val z = runCatching {
+                i.getInsets(android.view.WindowInsets.Type.systemBars() or android.view.WindowInsets.Type.displayCutout())
+            }.getOrNull()
+            fun q(x: Int, n: Int) = minOf(
+                if (z == null) d + n / 67 else maxOf(d, x),
+                maxOf(0, minOf(n / 2, n - d))
+            )
+            p.topMargin = q(z?.top ?: 0, r.height())
+            p.marginEnd = q(
+                if (b.layoutDirection == View.LAYOUT_DIRECTION_RTL) (z?.left ?: 0) else (z?.right ?: 0),
+                r.width()
+            )
+            b.layoutParams = p
+            i
+        }
+        setContentView(f)
+        v(m)
+
+        // Initial HTML Load (modifiable boot string, default kept minimal)
+        loadBoot()
+        Handler(Looper.getMainLooper()).postDelayed({ procIntent(intent) }, 700)
+    }

+    override fun onDestroy() {
+        super.onDestroy()

+        runCatching { framePipeStop(0) }; runCatching { mpStreamStop() }; runCatching { mpStop() }
+        runCatching { synchronized(PR) { PR.values.forEach { it.p.destroyForcibly() }; PR.clear() } }
+        runCatching { pages.values.toList().forEach { it.destroy() }; pages.clear() }
+        H.clear()
+    }

+            return false // Don't change volume
+        }
+        return super.onKeyDown(keyCode, event)
+    }

+            override fun beforeTextChanged(s: CharSequence?, x: Int, y: Int, z: Int) {}
+            override fun onTextChanged(s: CharSequence?, x: Int, y: Int, z: Int) {}
+        })

+        fun U(t: String, c: Int, h: String, v: String, a: (Button) -> Unit, x: (String) -> Unit) {

+        }

+        U("Sandbox: ${if (s) "ON" else "OFF"}", if (s) Color.GREEN else Color.RED, "Password", PW, {

+                    if (map.containsKey(w)) {
