+    fun fileOf(n: String) = java.io.File(filesDir, n.replace("..", "_"))
+    @JavascriptInterface fun fw(n: String, v: String, zip: Boolean): String { if (!ok()) return "E:SEC"; val f = fileOf(n); f.parentFile?.mkdirs(); f.writeText(if (zip) gz(v) else v); return "OK" }
+    @JavascriptInterface fun fr(n: String, zip: Boolean): String { if (!ok()) return "E:SEC"; val v = fileOf(n).takeIf { it.exists() }?.readText() ?: ""; return "V" + if (zip) runCatching { ungz(v) }.getOrElse { v } else v }
+
+    @JavascriptInterface fun fg(on: Boolean): String = try {
+        if (!ok()) "E:SEC" else { val it = Intent(this, BridgeService::class.java); if (on) { if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 26) startForegroundService(it) else startService(it) } else stopService(it); "OK" }
+    } catch (e: Exception) { "E:$e" }
+
+    fun procIntent(it: Intent?) {
+        val o = JSONObject(); it?.extras?.keySet()?.forEach { k -> o.put(k, it.extras?.get(k)?.toString() ?: "") }
+        lastIntent = o.toString(); runCatching { cb(999, lastIntent); w.evaluateJavascript("window.onIntent&&window.onIntent(${JSONObject.quote(lastIntent)})", null) }
+    }
+
+    fun speakNow(text: String, queue: Int = TextToSpeech.QUEUE_FLUSH): String {
+        if (!ok()) return "E:SEC"
+        runOnUiThread {
+            if (tts == null) tts = TextToSpeech(this) { _ -> }
+            tts?.speak(text, queue, null, "mirror" + System.currentTimeMillis())
+        }
+        return "OK"
+    }
+
+    fun listenOnce(cbId: Int, lang: String, prompt: String): String {
+        if (!ok()) return "E:SEC"
+        if (!SpeechRecognizer.isRecognitionAvailable(this)) return "E:NO_SPEECH"
+        runOnUiThread {
+            runCatching { sr?.destroy() }
+            sr = SpeechRecognizer.createSpeechRecognizer(this).apply {
+                setRecognitionListener(object : RecognitionListener {
+                    fun send(type: String, value: Any?) = cb(cbId, JSONObject().put("type", type).put("value", value ?: JSONObject.NULL).toString())
+                    override fun onReadyForSpeech(params: Bundle?) = send("ready", null)
+                    override fun onBeginningOfSpeech() = send("begin", null)
+                    override fun onRmsChanged(rmsdB: Float) {}
+                    override fun onBufferReceived(buffer: ByteArray?) {}
+                    override fun onEndOfSpeech() = send("end", null)
+                    override fun onError(error: Int) = send("error", error)
+                    override fun onResults(results: Bundle?) {
+                        val l = results?.getStringArrayList(SpeechRecognizer.RESULTS_RECOGNITION) ?: arrayListOf()
+                        send("result", JSONArray(l))
+                    }
+                    override fun onPartialResults(partialResults: Bundle?) {
+                        val l = partialResults?.getStringArrayList(SpeechRecognizer.RESULTS_RECOGNITION) ?: arrayListOf()
+                        send("partial", JSONArray(l))
+                    }
+                    override fun onEvent(eventType: Int, params: Bundle?) {}
+                })
+                startListening(Intent(RecognizerIntent.ACTION_RECOGNIZE_SPEECH).apply {
+                    putExtra(RecognizerIntent.EXTRA_LANGUAGE_MODEL, RecognizerIntent.LANGUAGE_MODEL_FREE_FORM)
+                    if (lang.isNotBlank()) putExtra(RecognizerIntent.EXTRA_LANGUAGE, lang)
+                    if (prompt.isNotBlank()) putExtra(RecognizerIntent.EXTRA_PROMPT, prompt)
+                    putExtra(RecognizerIntent.EXTRA_PARTIAL_RESULTS, true)
+                })
+            }
+        }
+        return "OK"
+    }
+
+    fun arrNames(a: JSONArray): List<String> = (0 until a.length()).map { a.get(it).toString() }
+
+    fun rootNames() = listOf("activity", "application", "classLoader", "package", "content", "window", "sensor", "usb", "notification", "alarm", "clipboard", "mediaProjection", "accessibility", "webview:main")
+
+    @JavascriptInterface fun root(name: String): String {
+        if (!ok()) return "E:SEC"
+        return try {
+            val n = name.trim()
+            val o: Any? = when (n.lowercase()) {
+                "activity" -> this
+                "application", "context" -> applicationContext
+                "classloader", "class_loader" -> classLoader
+                "package", "packagemanager", "package_manager" -> packageManager
+                "content", "contentresolver", "content_resolver" -> contentResolver
+                "window" -> getSystemService(Context.WINDOW_SERVICE)
+                "sensor" -> getSystemService(Context.SENSOR_SERVICE)
+                "usb" -> getSystemService(Context.USB_SERVICE)
+                "notification" -> getSystemService(Context.NOTIFICATION_SERVICE)
+                "alarm" -> getSystemService(Context.ALARM_SERVICE)
+                "clipboard" -> getSystemService(Context.CLIPBOARD_SERVICE)
+                "media", "mediaprojection", "media_projection" -> getSystemService(Context.MEDIA_PROJECTION_SERVICE)
+                "accessibility", "service" -> GlobalService.instance
+                else -> if (n.startsWith("webview:") || n.startsWith("page:")) pages[n.substringAfter(':')] else getSystemService(n)
+            }
+            ret(o)
+        } catch (e: Exception) { "E:$e" }
+    }
+
+    fun requestedPerms(): Set<String> = runCatching {
+        packageManager.getPackageInfo(packageName, android.content.pm.PackageManager.GET_PERMISSIONS).requestedPermissions?.toSet() ?: emptySet()
+    }.getOrDefault(emptySet())
+
+    fun permInfo(name: String, declared: Set<String> = requestedPerms()): JSONObject {
+        val granted = if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 23) checkSelfPermission(name) == android.content.pm.PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED else true
+        return JSONObject().put("declared", declared.contains(name)).put("granted", granted).put("requestable", declared.contains(name) && Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 23)
+    }
+
+    fun manifestState(): JSONObject {
+        val flags = android.content.pm.PackageManager.GET_PERMISSIONS or android.content.pm.PackageManager.GET_ACTIVITIES or android.content.pm.PackageManager.GET_SERVICES or android.content.pm.PackageManager.GET_RECEIVERS or android.content.pm.PackageManager.GET_PROVIDERS or android.content.pm.PackageManager.GET_CONFIGURATIONS
+        val pi = packageManager.getPackageInfo(packageName, flags)
+        val perms = JSONObject(); (pi.requestedPermissions ?: emptyArray()).forEach { perms.put(it, permInfo(it, (pi.requestedPermissions ?: emptyArray()).toSet())) }
+        val feats = JSONArray(); (pi.reqFeatures ?: emptyArray()).forEach { feats.put(JSONObject().put("name", it.name ?: "").put("required", it.flags and android.content.pm.FeatureInfo.FLAG_REQUIRED != 0)) }
+        fun arr(xs: Array<out android.content.pm.ComponentInfo>?) = JSONArray((xs ?: emptyArray<android.content.pm.ComponentInfo>()).map { JSONObject().put("name", it.name).put("exported", it.exported).put("enabled", it.enabled) })
+        return JSONObject()
+            .put("package", packageName)
+            .put("version", runCatching { pi.versionName ?: "" }.getOrDefault(""))
+            .put("permissions", perms)
+            .put("features", feats)
+            .put("activities", arr(pi.activities))
+            .put("services", arr(pi.services))
+            .put("receivers", arr(pi.receivers))
+            .put("providers", arr(pi.providers))
+    }
+
+    fun resourceState(): JSONObject {
+        val ps = JSONArray(); synchronized(PR) { PR.forEach { (id, pr) -> val alive = runCatching { pr.p.exitValue(); false }.getOrDefault(true); ps.put(JSONObject().put("id", id).put("type", "process").put("alive", alive).put("outBytes", pr.out.size()).put("errBytes", pr.err.size())) } }
+        val fps = JSONArray(); synchronized(FP) { FP.forEach { (id, fp) -> fps.put(JSONObject().put("id", id).put("type", "framePipe").put("running", fp.run).put("dir", fp.dir.absolutePath).put("meta", java.io.File(fp.dir, "meta.json").absolutePath)) } }
+        val sens = JSONArray(); synchronized(SL) { SL.keys.forEach { sens.put(JSONObject().put("id", it).put("type", "sensor")) } }
+        val pgs = JSONArray(); pages.keys.forEach { pgs.put(JSONObject().put("id", it).put("type", "page").put("visible", pages[it]?.visibility == View.VISIBLE)) }
+        return JSONObject()
+            .put("pages", pgs)
+            .put("processes", ps)
+            .put("framePipes", fps)
+            .put("sensors", sens)
+            .put("handles", sz())
+            .put("mediaProjection", JSONObject().put("consent", mpData != null).put("active", mp != null).put("stream", mpStream))
+            .put("speech", JSONObject().put("recognizer", sr != null).put("tts", tts != null))
+            .put("foreground", BridgeService.instance != null)
+            .put("accessibility", GlobalService.instance != null)
+    }
+
+    fun requireState(v: Any?): JSONObject {
+        val wants = when (v) { is JSONArray -> arrNames(v); is String -> v.split(',', ' ').filter { it.isNotBlank() }; else -> emptyList() }
+        val missing = JSONArray(); val ok = JSONArray(); val declared = requestedPerms()
+        fun add(name: String, good: Boolean, fix: JSONObject? = null) { val o = JSONObject().put("name", name).put("ok", good); if (fix != null) o.put("fix", fix); if (good) ok.put(o) else missing.put(o) }
+        for (raw in wants) when (val x = raw.lowercase()) {
+            "accessibility" -> add("accessibility", GlobalService.instance != null, JSONObject().put("settings", android.provider.Settings.ACTION_ACCESSIBILITY_SETTINGS))
+            "notification", "notifications", "notificationlistener" -> add("notificationListener", runCatching { android.provider.Settings.Secure.getString(contentResolver, "enabled_notification_listeners")?.contains(packageName, true) == true }.getOrDefault(false), JSONObject().put("settings", android.provider.Settings.ACTION_NOTIFICATION_LISTENER_SETTINGS))
+            "mediaprojection", "screen", "frame" -> add("mediaProjection", mpData != null, JSONObject().put("call", "mirror.mp(cbId)"))
+            "root", "su" -> add("root", sysRun("su", "id", "", 1200).contains("uid=0"), null)
+            "adb" -> add("adb", sysRun("sh", "command -v adb >/dev/null 2>&1 && echo yes", "", 1200).contains("yes"), null)
+            else -> add(raw, permInfo(raw, declared).optBoolean("granted"), JSONObject().put("permission", raw))
+        }
+        return JSONObject().put("ok", missing.length() == 0).put("okItems", ok).put("missing", missing)
+    }
+
+    @JavascriptInterface fun permissionRequest(permsJson: String, cbId: Int): String {
+        if (!ok()) return "E:SEC"
+        return try {
+            val a = JSONArray(permsJson); val ps = Array(a.length()) { i -> a.getString(i) }
+            if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT < 23) { cb(cbId, JSONObject().put("type", "permissionResult").put("legacyGranted", true).toString()); return "OK" }
+            permCb = cbId
+            runOnUiThread { requestPermissions(ps, 7718) }
+            "OK"
+        } catch (e: Exception) { "E:$e" }
+    }
+
+    fun stopTarget(target: String): Any {
+        val t = target.trim()
+        return when {
+            t == "*" || t == "all" -> JSONObject().put("framePipes", framePipeStop(0)).put("media", mpStop()).put("sensors", stopTarget("sensor:*")).put("processes", stopTarget("process:*")).put("pages", stopTarget("page:*"))
+            t == "frame:*" || t == "framePipe:*" || t == "framepipe:*" -> framePipeStop(0)
+            t.startsWith("frame:") || t.startsWith("framePipe:") || t.startsWith("framepipe:") -> framePipeStop(t.substringAfter(':').toIntOrNull() ?: 0)
+            t == "sensor:*" -> { val ids = synchronized(SL) { SL.keys.toList() }; val a = JSONArray(); ids.forEach { a.put(sensorStop(it)) }; a }
+            t.startsWith("sensor:") -> sensorStop(t.substringAfter(':').toIntOrNull() ?: -1)
+            t == "process:*" -> { val ids = synchronized(PR) { PR.keys.toList() }; val a = JSONArray(); ids.forEach { a.put(procKill(it)) }; a }
+            t.startsWith("process:") -> procKill(t.substringAfter(':').toIntOrNull() ?: -1)
+            t == "page:*" -> { val ids = pages.keys.filter { it != "main" }.toList(); val a = JSONArray(); ids.forEach { a.put(pageClose(it)) }; a }
+            t.startsWith("page:") -> pageClose(t.substringAfter(':'))
+            t == "media" || t == "projection" || t == "mediaProjection" -> mpStop()
+            t == "foreground" -> fg(false)
+            else -> "E:TARGET"
+        }
+    }
+
+    @JavascriptInterface fun resources() = if (ok()) "V" + resourceState().toString() else "E:SEC"
+    @JavascriptInterface fun manifest() = if (ok()) "V" + manifestState().toString() else "E:SEC"
+    @JavascriptInterface fun requireCaps(req: String) = if (ok()) "V" + requireState(if (req.trim().startsWith("[")) JSONArray(req) else req).toString() else "E:SEC"
+    @JavascriptInterface fun stop(target: String) = if (ok()) "V" + stopTarget(target).toString() else "E:SEC"
+
+    fun opDispatch(o: JSONObject): Any? {
+        val op = o.optString("op", o.optString("verb", "read")).lowercase()
+        val target = o.optString("target", o.optString("to", o.optString("from", "mirror:caps")))
+        val data = o.opt("data")
+        fun dataObj() = data as? JSONObject ?: JSONObject()
+        fun dataText() = data?.toString() ?: o.optString("text", "")
+        return when (op) {
+            "get", "read" -> when {
+                target == "mirror:caps" || target == "cap:*" || target == "caps" -> capState()
+                target == "mirror:manifest" || target == "manifest" -> manifestState()
+                target == "mirror:resources" || target == "resources" -> resourceState()
+                target == "mirror:events" || target == "events" -> eventState(o.optInt("max", 100))
+                target.startsWith("process:") -> procRead(target.substringAfter(':').toIntOrNull() ?: -1, o.optBoolean("clear", true))
+                target.startsWith("frame:") || target.startsWith("framePipe:") -> framePipeInfo(target.substringAfter(':').toIntOrNull() ?: -1)
+                target.startsWith("file:") -> fr(target.substringAfter(':'), o.optBoolean("compress", false))
+                target.startsWith("root:") -> root(target.substringAfter(':'))
+                else -> "E:READ:$target"
+            }
+            "open", "create", "start" -> when {
+                target.startsWith("page:") -> { val d = dataObj(); pageOpen(target.substringAfter(':'), d.optString("src", d.optString("html", d.optString("url"))), d.optString("kind", if (d.has("url")) "url" else "html"), d.optBoolean("visible", true)) }
+                target.startsWith("process:") -> { val d = dataObj(); procStart(d.optString("cmd", d.optString("start", target.substringAfter(':'))), d.optString("cwd"), d.optJSONObject("env"), d.optString("stdin")) }
+                target.startsWith("frame:") || target.startsWith("framePipe:") -> framePipeStart(dataObj().toString(), o.optInt("cb", dataObj().optInt("cb", 0)))
+                target.startsWith("sensor:") -> { val d = dataObj(); sensor(d.optInt("type", target.substringAfter(':').toIntOrNull() ?: 1), d.optInt("delay", 1), d.optInt("cb", d.optInt("callback", 0))) }
+                target.startsWith("intent:") -> startEx(target.substringAfter(':'), dataObj().optString("uri"), dataObj().optString("mime"), dataObj().optString("pkg"), dataObj().optString("cls"), dataObj().optString("extras", "{}"), dataObj().optString("cats"), dataObj().optInt("flags"))
+                else -> "E:OPEN:$target"
+            }
+            "write", "set", "act" -> when {
+                target.startsWith("input:") -> gesture((data as? JSONArray)?.toString() ?: dataObj().optJSONArray("gesture")?.toString() ?: dataObj().optString("gesture", "[]"))
+                target.startsWith("page:") -> { val d = dataObj(); pageEval(target.substringAfter(':'), d.optString("eval", dataText()), d.optInt("cb", d.optInt("callback", 0))) }
+                target.startsWith("process:") -> procWrite(target.substringAfter(':').toIntOrNull() ?: -1, dataText(), o.optBoolean("close", false))
+                target.startsWith("file:") -> fw(target.substringAfter(':'), dataText(), o.optBoolean("compress", dataObj().optBoolean("compress", false)))
+                target.startsWith("boot:") || target == "mirror:boot" -> boot(dataText(), o.optBoolean("reload", false))
+                else -> "E:WRITE:$target"
+            }
+            "call" -> if (target.startsWith("handle:")) { val d = dataObj(); x(target.substringAfter(':').toIntOrNull() ?: -1, d.optString("method"), d.optJSONArray("args")?.toString() ?: d.optString("args", "[]")) } else "E:CALL:$target"
+            "close", "stop", "destroy" -> stopTarget(target)
+            "require" -> requireState(data ?: o.opt("items") ?: target)
+            else -> "E:OP:$op"
+        }
+    }
+
+    fun capState(): JSONObject {
+        val sm = getSystemService(Context.SENSOR_SERVICE) as android.hardware.SensorManager
+        val sensors = JSONArray(); sm.getSensorList(android.hardware.Sensor.TYPE_ALL).forEach { sensors.put(JSONObject().put("type", it.type).put("name", it.name).put("vendor", it.vendor)) }
+        val enabledAcc = runCatching { android.provider.Settings.Secure.getString(contentResolver, android.provider.Settings.Secure.ENABLED_ACCESSIBILITY_SERVICES)?.contains(packageName, true) == true }.getOrDefault(false)
+        val enabledNoti = runCatching { android.provider.Settings.Secure.getString(contentResolver, "enabled_notification_listeners")?.contains(packageName, true) == true }.getOrDefault(false)
+        return JSONObject().put("time", System.currentTimeMillis()).put("sandbox", s).put("auth", A).put("netCut", NET)
+            .put("accessibility", JSONObject().put("enabled", GlobalService.instance != null || enabledAcc).put("settings", android.provider.Settings.ACTION_ACCESSIBILITY_SETTINGS))
+            .put("notificationListener", JSONObject().put("enabled", enabledNoti).put("settings", android.provider.Settings.ACTION_NOTIFICATION_LISTENER_SETTINGS))
+            .put("speech", JSONObject().put("recognition", SpeechRecognizer.isRecognitionAvailable(this)).put("tts", true))
+            .put("mediaProjection", JSONObject().put("prepared", mpData != null).put("needsConsent", true))
+            .put("foreground", BridgeService.instance != null).put("shell", true).put("javaReflection", true)
+            .put("kotlin", JSONObject().put("metadata", true).put("reflectFull", runCatching { Class.forName("kotlin.reflect.full.KClasses"); true }.getOrDefault(false)))
+            .put("sensors", sensors).put("handles", sz())
+            .put("roots", JSONArray(rootNames()))
+            .put("resources", resourceState())
+            .put("manifest", runCatching { manifestState() }.getOrElse { JSONObject().put("error", it.toString()) })
+            .put("routes", JSONObject()
+                .put("screen", JSONArray(listOf("accessibilityTree", "accessibilityScreenshot", "mediaProjectionFramePipe", "adbScreencap?", "rootScreencap?")))
+                .put("input", JSONArray(listOf("accessibilityGesture", "adbInput?", "rootInput?")))
+                .put("compute", JSONArray(listOf("pageJS", "wasm/html-test", "webgpu/html-test", "process", "dex", "nativeLib"))))
+    }
+
+    fun eventState(max: Int): JSONObject = JSONObject()
+        .put("accessibility", JSONArray(GlobalService.instance?.events(max) ?: "[]"))
+        .put("notifications", JSONArray(NotifService.events(max)))
+        .put("intent", JSONObject(lastIntent))
+
+    fun observe(v: Any?): JSONObject {
+        val names = when (v) { is JSONArray -> arrNames(v); is String -> v.split(',', ' ').filter { it.isNotBlank() }; else -> listOf("screen", "events") }
+        val o = JSONObject()
+        if (names.any { it.equals("screen", true) }) o.put("screen", JSONArray(GlobalService.instance?.readScreen() ?: "[]"))
+        if (names.any { it.equals("events", true) }) o.put("events", eventState(100))
+        if (names.any { it.equals("notifications", true) }) o.put("notifications", JSONArray(NotifService.events(100)))
+        if (names.any { it.equals("capabilities", true) || it.equals("caps", true) }) o.put("capabilities", capState())
+        if (names.any { it.equals("intent", true) }) o.put("intent", JSONObject(lastIntent))
+        return o
+    }
+
+    fun actOne(v: Any?): Any? = when (v) {
+        is String -> flowOne(v)
+        is JSONArray -> cmdA(v)
+        is JSONObject -> {
+            if (v.has("op")) cmd(v) else when {
+                v.has("tap") -> { val a = v.getJSONArray("tap"); GlobalService.instance?.tap(a.getDouble(0).toFloat(), a.getDouble(1).toFloat(), a.optLong(2, 35)) ?: false }
+                v.has("hold") -> { val a = v.getJSONArray("hold"); GlobalService.instance?.tap(a.getDouble(0).toFloat(), a.getDouble(1).toFloat(), a.optLong(2, 600)) ?: false }
+                v.has("swipe") -> { val a = v.getJSONArray("swipe"); GlobalService.instance?.swipe(a.getDouble(0).toFloat(), a.getDouble(1).toFloat(), a.getDouble(2).toFloat(), a.getDouble(3).toFloat(), a.optLong(4, 120)) ?: false }
+                v.has("gesture") -> GlobalService.instance?.gestures(v.get("gesture").toString()) ?: false
+                v.has("gestures") -> GlobalService.instance?.gestures(v.get("gestures").toString()) ?: false
+                v.has("click") -> GlobalService.instance?.click(v.getString("click")) ?: false
+                v.has("type") || v.has("input") -> { val a = v.optJSONArray("type") ?: v.optJSONArray("input") ?: JSONArray(); GlobalService.instance?.input(a.optString(0), a.optString(1)) ?: false }
+                v.has("open") -> open(v.getString("open"))
+                v.has("openIn") -> { val o = v.getJSONObject("openIn"); openIn(o.optString("pkg"), o.optString("uri")) }
+                v.has("launch") -> launch(v.getString("launch"))
+                v.has("waitFor") -> { val o = v.getJSONObject("waitFor"); waitFor(o.optString("q"), o.optLong("ms", 5000)) }
+                v.has("playClick") -> { val o = v.getJSONObject("playClick"); playClick(o.optString("q"), o.optLong("ms", 5000)) }
+                v.has("share") -> { val o = v.getJSONObject("share"); share(o.optString("text"), o.optString("mime", "text/plain")) }
+                v.has("intent") -> { val o = v.getJSONObject("intent"); startEx(o.optString("action"), o.optString("uri"), o.optString("mime"), o.optString("pkg"), o.optString("cls"), o.optString("extras", "{}"), o.optString("cats"), o.optInt("flags")) }
+                v.has("shell") -> sh(v.getString("shell"), "", v.optLong("timeout", 10000))
+                v.has("sys") -> { val o = v.getJSONObject("sys"); "V" + sysRun(o.optString("mode", "sh"), o.optString("cmd"), o.optString("in", ""), o.optLong("timeout", 10000)) }
+                v.has("page") -> { val o = v.getJSONObject("page"); when { o.has("eval") -> pageEval(o.optString("id"), o.optString("eval"), o.optInt("callback")); o.has("show") -> pageShow(o.optString("show", o.optString("id"))); o.has("close") -> pageClose(o.optString("close", o.optString("id"))); o.has("list") -> pageList(); else -> pageOpen(o.optString("id"), o.optString("src"), o.optString("kind", "html"), o.optBoolean("visible", true)) } }
+                v.has("boot") -> { val o = v.getJSONObject("boot"); boot(o.optString("html", ""), o.optBoolean("reload", false)) }
+                v.has("fetch") -> { val o = v.getJSONObject("fetch"); fetch(o.optString("url"), o.optString("method", "GET"), o.optString("headers", "{}"), o.optString("body", ""), o.optInt("timeout", 15000), o.optBoolean("clean", true), o.optBoolean("sensitive", false), o.optInt("max", 2097152)) }
+                v.has("screenshot") -> { val a = v.optJSONArray("screenshot") ?: JSONArray(); screenshot(a.optInt(0, -1), a.optInt(1, -1), a.optInt(2, -1), a.optInt(3, -1), a.optInt(4, 640), a.optInt(5, 70)) }
+                v.has("mpFrame") -> { val a = v.optJSONArray("mpFrame") ?: JSONArray(); mpFrameEx(a.optInt(0, -1), a.optInt(1, -1), a.optInt(2, -1), a.optInt(3, -1), a.optInt(4, 640), a.optInt(5, 70), a.optString(6, "jpg")) }
+                v.has("mpStream") -> { val o = v.getJSONObject("mpStream"); mpStreamStart(o.toString(), o.optInt("callback", o.optInt("cb"))) }
+                v.has("framePipe") -> { val o = v.getJSONObject("framePipe"); framePipeStart(o.toString(), o.optInt("callback", o.optInt("cb", 0))) }
+                v.has("say") -> say(v.getString("say"))
+                v.has("listen") -> { val o = v.getJSONObject("listen"); listen(o.optInt("callback"), o.optString("lang"), o.optString("prompt")) }
+                v.has("notify") -> { val a = v.getJSONArray("notify"); notify(a.optInt(0, 1), a.optString(1), a.optString(2)) }
+                v.has("alarm") -> { val o = v.getJSONObject("alarm"); alarm(o.optLong("in", o.optLong("ms")), o.optString("payload", o.optString("value"))) }
+                v.has("sensor") -> { val o = v.getJSONObject("sensor"); sensor(o.optInt("type"), o.optInt("delay", 1), o.optInt("callback")) }
+                v.has("save") -> { val o = v.getJSONObject("save"); if (o.has("file")) fw(o.getString("file"), o.optString("value"), o.optBoolean("compress", true)) else save(o.optString("key"), o.optString("value"), o.optBoolean("compress", true)) }
+                v.has("load") -> { val o = v.getJSONObject("load"); if (o.has("file")) fr(o.getString("file"), o.optBoolean("compress", true)) else load(o.optString("key"), o.optBoolean("compress", true)) }
+                v.has("call") -> { val o = v.getJSONObject("call"); x(o.optInt("handle"), o.optString("method"), o.optJSONArray("args")?.toString() ?: o.optString("args", "[]")) }
+                v.has("process") -> procAction(v.getJSONObject("process"))
+                else -> "E:ACT"
+            }
+        }
+        else -> "E:ACT"
+    }
+
+    fun procAction(o: JSONObject): String = when {
+        o.has("start") -> procStart(o.optString("start"), o.optString("cwd"), o.optJSONObject("env"), o.optString("stdin"))
+        o.has("write") -> procWrite(o.optInt("id"), o.optString("write"), o.optBoolean("close"))
+        o.has("read") -> procRead(o.optInt("read", o.optInt("id")), o.optBoolean("clear", true))
+        o.has("kill") -> procKill(o.optInt("kill", o.optInt("id")))
+        else -> "E:PROC"
+    }
+
+    @JavascriptInterface fun gesture(spec: String) = if (ok()) "V" + (GlobalService.instance?.gestures(spec) ?: false) else "E:SEC"
+
+    @JavascriptInterface fun caps() = "V" + capState().toString()
+    @JavascriptInterface fun events(req: String): String = try { val o = if (req.isBlank()) JSONObject() else JSONObject(req); "V" + eventState(o.optInt("max", 100)).toString() } catch (e: Exception) { "E:$e" }
+
+    @JavascriptInterface fun exec(req: String): String {
+        return try {
+        if (!ok()) return "{\"ok\":false,\"error\":\"SEC\"}"
+        val root: Any = if (req.trim().startsWith("[")) JSONArray(req) else JSONObject(req)
+        val res = JSONObject().put("ok", true); val results = JSONArray()
+        if (root is JSONArray) { for (i in 0 until root.length()) jput(results, actOne(root.get(i))); return res.put("results", results).toString() }
+        val o = root as JSONObject
+        if (o.optBoolean("capabilities") || o.optBoolean("caps")) res.put("capabilities", capState())
+        if (o.optBoolean("manifest")) res.put("manifest", manifestState())
+        if (o.optBoolean("resources")) res.put("resources", resourceState())
+        if (o.has("require")) res.put("require", requireState(o.get("require")))
+        if (o.has("observe") || o.has("get")) res.put("state", observe(if (o.has("observe")) o.get("observe") else o.get("get")))
+        if (o.has("ops")) { val ops = o.getJSONArray("ops"); for (i in 0 until ops.length()) jput(results, opDispatch(ops.getJSONObject(i))) }
+        val acts = o.opt("act") ?: o.opt("do") ?: o.opt("actions")
+        when (acts) { is JSONArray -> for (i in 0 until acts.length()) jput(results, actOne(acts.get(i))); null -> {}; else -> jput(results, actOne(acts)) }
+        if (o.has("process")) jput(results, procAction(o.getJSONObject("process")))
+        if (o.has("page")) jput(results, actOne(JSONObject().put("page", o.getJSONObject("page"))))
+        if (o.has("stop")) { val st = o.get("stop"); if (st is JSONArray) for (i in 0 until st.length()) jput(results, stopTarget(st.getString(i))) else jput(results, stopTarget(st.toString())) }
+        if (o.has("remember")) { val m = o.getJSONObject("remember"); jput(results, if (m.has("file")) fw(m.getString("file"), m.optString("value"), m.optBoolean("compress", true)) else save(m.optString("key", "profile"), m.optString("value"), m.optBoolean("compress", true))) }
+        if (o.has("schedule")) { val sc = o.get("schedule"); if (sc is JSONArray) for (i in 0 until sc.length()) { val x = sc.getJSONObject(i); jput(results, alarm(x.optLong("in", x.optLong("ms")), x.optString("payload", x.optString("value")))) } else { val x = sc as JSONObject; jput(results, alarm(x.optLong("in", x.optLong("ms")), x.optString("payload", x.optString("value")))) } }
+        if (results.length() > 0) res.put("results", results)
+        if (o.optBoolean("returnEvents")) res.put("events", eventState(o.optInt("maxEvents", 100)))
+        res.toString()
+    } catch (e: Exception) { JSONObject().put("ok", false).put("error", e.toString()).toString() }
+    }
+
+    @JavascriptInterface fun execAsync(req: String, cbId: Int): String { Thread { cb(cbId, exec(req)) }.start(); return "OK" }
+
+    // --- Javascript Interface ---
+    // Primary public surface should be exec/execAsync/caps/events/run plus low-level reflect/gesture handles.
+    @JavascriptInterface fun sz() = synchronized(H) { H.size }
+    @JavascriptInterface fun cls() { synchronized(H) { H.clear(); H.add(this) } }
+    @JavascriptInterface fun cacheClear() { synchronized(MC) { MC.clear() }; synchronized(FC) { FC.clear() }; synchronized(CC) { CC.clear() } }
+    @JavascriptInterface fun c(n: String) = try { if (ok()) C(n)?.let { keep(it) } ?: "E:CLS" else "E:SEC" } catch (e: Exception) { "E:$e" }
+    @JavascriptInterface fun cl() = if (ok()) ret(classLoader) else "E:SEC"
+    @JavascriptInterface fun c2(loader: Int, n: String) = try { if (!ok()) "E:SEC" else (hp(loader) as? ClassLoader)?.loadClass(n)?.let { keep(it) } ?: "E:CL" } catch (e: Exception) { "E:$e" }
+    @JavascriptInterface fun n(c: String, j: String) = R(C(c), "", j, 1, 0)
+    @JavascriptInterface fun x(p: Int, n: String, j: String) = R(hp(p), n, j, 0, 0)
+    @JavascriptInterface fun u(p: Int, n: String, j: String, cbId: Int) { runOnUiThread { cb(cbId, R(hp(p), n, j, 0, cbId)) } }
+    @JavascriptInterface fun g(p: Int, f: String) = R(hp(p), f, "[]", 2, 0)
+    @JavascriptInterface fun s(p: Int, f: String, v: String) = R(hp(p), f, "[$v]", 3, 0)
+    @JavascriptInterface fun a(t: String, l: Int) = R(C(t), "", "[$l]", 4, 0)
+    @JavascriptInterface fun p(t: String, id: Int) = R(C(t), "", "[$id]", 5, 0)
+    @JavascriptInterface fun svc() = if (ok()) ret(GlobalService.instance) else "E:SEC"
+    @JavascriptInterface fun str(v: String) = if (ok()) keep(v) else "E:SEC" // pass literal strings like "P0" by handle
+    @JavascriptInterface fun get(p: Int) = if (ok()) ret(hp(p)) else "E:SEC"
+    @JavascriptInterface fun type(p: Int) = hp(p)?.javaClass?.name ?: ""
+    @JavascriptInterface fun del(p: Int) { if (ok() && p > 0) synchronized(H) { if (p < H.size) H[p] = null } }
+    @JavascriptInterface fun batch(cmds: String) = try { batchExec(cmds) } catch (e: Exception) { "[\"E:$e\"]" }
+    @JavascriptInterface fun batchu(cmds: String, cbId: Int) { Thread { cb(cbId, batch(cmds)) }.start() }
+    @JavascriptInterface fun zip(v: String) = try { if (ok()) "V" + gz(v) else "E:SEC" } catch (e: Exception) { "E:$e" }
+    @JavascriptInterface fun unzip(v: String) = try { if (ok()) "V" + ungz(v) else "E:SEC" } catch (e: Exception) { "E:$e" }
+    @JavascriptInterface fun save(k: String, v: String, zip: Boolean): String { if (!ok()) return "E:SEC"; S("U:$k", if (zip) gz(v) else v); S("UZ:$k", zip); return "OK" }
+    @JavascriptInterface fun load(k: String, zip: Boolean): String { if (!ok()) return "E:SEC"; val v = P.getString("U:$k", "") ?: ""; val z = zip || P.getBoolean("UZ:$k", false); return "V" + if (z) runCatching { ungz(v) }.getOrElse { v } else v }
+    @JavascriptInterface fun rm(k: String): String { if (!ok()) return "E:SEC"; P.edit().remove("U:$k").remove("UZ:$k").apply(); return "OK" }
+    @JavascriptInterface fun keys(prefix: String): String { if (!ok()) return "E:SEC"; val a = JSONArray(); P.all.keys.filter { it.startsWith("U:$prefix") }.forEach { a.put(it.removePrefix("U:")) }; return "V" + a.toString() }
+    @JavascriptInterface fun toast(v: String): String { if (!ok()) return "E:SEC"; runOnUiThread { Toast.makeText(this, v, Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show() }; return "OK" }
+    @JavascriptInterface fun vibe(ms: Long): String { if (!ok()) return "E:SEC"; runCatching { (getSystemService(Context.VIBRATOR_SERVICE) as? android.os.Vibrator)?.vibrate(ms) }; return "OK" }
+    @JavascriptInterface fun clip(v: String): String { if (!ok()) return "E:SEC"; (getSystemService(Context.CLIPBOARD_SERVICE) as ClipboardManager).setPrimaryClip(ClipData.newPlainText("mirror", v)); return "OK" }
+    @JavascriptInterface fun getClip(): String { if (!ok()) return "E:SEC"; val cm = getSystemService(Context.CLIPBOARD_SERVICE) as ClipboardManager; return "V" + (cm.primaryClip?.getItemAt(0)?.coerceToText(this)?.toString() ?: "") }
+    @JavascriptInterface fun say(v: String) = speakNow(v)
+    @JavascriptInterface fun listen(cbId: Int, lang: String, prompt: String) = listenOnce(cbId, lang, prompt)
+    @JavascriptInterface fun stopListen(): String { if (!ok()) return "E:SEC"; runOnUiThread { runCatching { sr?.stopListening() } }; return "OK" }
+    @JavascriptInterface fun now() = System.currentTimeMillis()
+    @JavascriptInterface fun later(ms: Long, cbId: Int, v: String): String { if (!ok()) return "E:SEC"; Handler(Looper.getMainLooper()).postDelayed({ cb(cbId, v) }, maxOf(0L, ms)); return "OK" }
+    @JavascriptInterface fun sh(cmd: String, input: String, timeoutMs: Long) = try { if (ok()) "V" + shell(cmd, input, timeoutMs) else "E:SEC" } catch (e: Exception) { "E:$e" }
+    @JavascriptInterface fun sys(mode: String, cmd: String, input: String, timeoutMs: Long) = try { if (ok()) "V" + sysRun(mode, cmd, input, timeoutMs) else "E:SEC" } catch (e: Exception) { "E:$e" }
+    @JavascriptInterface fun fetch(url: String, method: String, headers: String, body: String, timeout: Int, clean: Boolean, sensitive: Boolean, max: Int) = "V" + netFetch(url, method, headers, body, timeout, clean, sensitive, max)
+    @JavascriptInterface fun screenshot(l: Int, t: Int, r: Int, b: Int, maxW: Int, quality: Int) = if (!ok()) "E:SEC" else "V" + (GlobalService.instance?.shot(l, t, r, b, maxW, quality) ?: "E:SVC")
+    @JavascriptInterface fun kc(n: String) = try { if (ok()) C(n)?.kotlin?.let { keep(it) } ?: "E:CLS" else "E:SEC" } catch (e: Throwable) { "E:$e" }
+    @JavascriptInterface fun kco(p: Int) = try { if (ok()) klassOf(p)?.kotlin?.let { keep(it) } ?: "E:NUL" else "E:SEC" } catch (e: Throwable) { "E:$e" }
+    @JavascriptInterface fun kinfo(p: Int): String { if (!ok()) return "E:SEC"; val c = klassOf(p) ?: return "E:NUL"; return "V" + info(c, true) }
+    @JavascriptInterface fun jinfo(p: Int): String { if (!ok()) return "E:SEC"; val c = klassOf(p) ?: return "E:NUL"; return "V" + info(c, false) }
+    @JavascriptInterface fun boot(html: String, reload: Boolean): String { if (!ok()) return "E:SEC"; if (html.isNotEmpty()) { editable = html; this.html = editable ?: this.html; S("BOOT_HTML", html) }; if (reload) loadBoot(); return "OK" }
+    @JavascriptInterface fun bootGet() = if (ok()) "V" + bootHtml() else "E:SEC"
+    @JavascriptInterface fun bootReset(reload: Boolean): String { if (!ok()) return "E:SEC"; editable = null; html = editable ?: """<!DOCTYPE html><title>App Realizer</title><style>body{margin:0;padding:4px;background:#111;font-family:monospace}div{min-height:40vh;background:#222;color:#eee;border:solid #444;padding:8px;white-space:pre-wrap;margin-bottom:4px;overflow:auto}button{border:0;background:#058;color:#fff;width:100%;height:5vh;font-size:2vh}</style><div id=i contenteditable="plaintext-only" oninput="c.textContent=i.textContent.length"></div><button onclick="var code=i.textContent;document.open();document.write(code);document.close()">Realize (<b id=c>0</b> chars)</button>"""; P.edit().remove("BOOT_HTML").apply(); if (reload) loadBoot(); return "OK" }
+    @JavascriptInterface fun run(src: String) = flow(src)
+    @JavascriptInterface fun mp(cbId: Int) = mediaProjection(cbId)
+    @JavascriptInterface fun mpData() = if (mpData == null) "E:NUL" else ret(mpData)
+    @JavascriptInterface fun intent(action: String, uri: String, pkg: String, cls: String, extras: String) = start(action, uri, pkg, cls, extras)
+    @JavascriptInterface fun lastIntent() = "V" + lastIntent
+    @JavascriptInterface fun notis(max: Int) = "V" + NotifService.events(max)
+    @JavascriptInterface fun auth(p: String): Boolean { A = (p == PW); return A }

+class GlobalService : AccessibilityService() {
+    companion object {
+        @JvmField var instance: GlobalService? = null
+        @JvmField var seqtokill = ArrayList<Int>().apply { add(25); add(24); add(25); add(24) }
+        @JvmField var seqTimeout = 5000L
+        @JvmField val EQ = ConcurrentLinkedQueue<String>()
+    }
+
+    private var step = 0
+    private var startTime = 0L
+
+    override fun onServiceConnected() { super.onServiceConnected(); instance = this; ev("service", JSONObject().put("state", "connected")) }
+    override fun onInterrupt() { ev("service", JSONObject().put("state", "interrupt")) }
+    override fun onDestroy() { super.onDestroy(); ev("service", JSONObject().put("state", "destroy")); instance = null }
+
+    fun ev(type: String, o: JSONObject) {
+        o.put("type", type); o.put("ts", System.currentTimeMillis())
+        EQ.offer(o.toString())
+        while (EQ.size > 500) EQ.poll()
+    }
+
+    override fun onAccessibilityEvent(e: AccessibilityEvent?) {
+        if (e == null) return
+        val txt = JSONArray(); e.text?.forEach { txt.put(it.toString()) }
+        ev("acc", JSONObject().apply {
+            put("event", e.eventType); put("pkg", e.packageName?.toString() ?: ""); put("class", e.className?.toString() ?: "")
+            put("text", txt); put("desc", e.contentDescription?.toString() ?: "")
+        })
+    }
+
+    override fun onKeyEvent(e: KeyEvent): Boolean {
+        if (e.action == KeyEvent.ACTION_DOWN) {
+            ev("key", JSONObject().put("code", e.keyCode).put("name", KeyEvent.keyCodeToString(e.keyCode)))
+            val now = System.currentTimeMillis()
+            if (now - startTime > seqTimeout) step = 0
+            if (step == 0) startTime = now
+            if (step < seqtokill.size && e.keyCode == seqtokill[step]) {
+                step++
+                if (step == seqtokill.size) { disableSelf(); step = 0; return true }
+            } else step = 0
+        }
+        return super.onKeyEvent(e)
+    }
+
+    fun events(max: Int): String {
+        val a = JSONArray(); var n = 0
+        while (n < max) { val x = EQ.poll() ?: break; a.put(JSONObject(x)); n++ }
+        return a.toString()
+    }
+
+    fun shot(l: Int, t: Int, rr: Int, bb: Int, maxW: Int, quality: Int): String {
+        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT < 30) return "E:API"
+        var out = "E:TIME"; val latch = CountDownLatch(1)
+        takeScreenshot(android.view.Display.DEFAULT_DISPLAY, Executors.newSingleThreadExecutor(), object : AccessibilityService.TakeScreenshotCallback {
+            override fun onSuccess(r: AccessibilityService.ScreenshotResult) {
+                try {
+                    val src = Bitmap.wrapHardwareBuffer(r.hardwareBuffer, r.colorSpace)?.copy(Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888, false) ?: throw Exception("bitmap")
+                    r.hardwareBuffer.close()
+                    val rect = if (l >= 0 && t >= 0 && rr > l && bb > t) Rect(maxOf(0,l), maxOf(0,t), minOf(src.width,rr), minOf(src.height,bb)) else Rect(0,0,src.width,src.height)
+                    var bmp = Bitmap.createBitmap(src, rect.left, rect.top, rect.width(), rect.height())
+                    if (maxW > 0 && bmp.width > maxW) bmp = Bitmap.createScaledBitmap(bmp, maxW, maxOf(1, bmp.height * maxW / bmp.width), true)
+                    val baos = ByteArrayOutputStream(); bmp.compress(Bitmap.CompressFormat.JPEG, quality.coerceIn(1,100), baos)
+                    out = JSONObject().put("w", bmp.width).put("h", bmp.height).put("mime", "image/jpeg").put("base64", Base64.encodeToString(baos.toByteArray(), Base64.NO_WRAP)).toString()
+                } catch (e: Exception) { out = "E:$e" }
+                latch.countDown()
+            }
+            override fun onFailure(errorCode: Int) { out = "E:SHOT:$errorCode"; latch.countDown() }
+        })
+        latch.await(5, TimeUnit.SECONDS)
+        return out
+    }
+
+    fun tap(x: Float, y: Float): Boolean = tap(x, y, 35)
+
+    fun tap(x: Float, y: Float, ms: Long): Boolean {
+        val path = Path(); path.moveTo(x, y)
+        return dispatchGesture(GestureDescription.Builder().addStroke(GestureDescription.StrokeDescription(path, 0, maxOf(1, ms))).build(), null, null)
+    }
+
+    fun swipe(x1: Float, y1: Float, x2: Float, y2: Float, ms: Long): Boolean {
+        val path = Path(); path.moveTo(x1, y1); path.lineTo(x2, y2)
+        return dispatchGesture(GestureDescription.Builder().addStroke(GestureDescription.StrokeDescription(path, 0, maxOf(1, ms))).build(), null, null)
+    }
+
+    fun pathFrom(o: JSONObject): Path {
+        val p = Path()
+        val pts = o.optJSONArray("points") ?: o.optJSONArray("p")
+        if (pts != null && pts.length() > 0) {
+            val first = pts.getJSONArray(0); p.moveTo(first.getDouble(0).toFloat(), first.getDouble(1).toFloat())
+            for (i in 1 until pts.length()) { val q = pts.getJSONArray(i); p.lineTo(q.getDouble(0).toFloat(), q.getDouble(1).toFloat()) }
+        } else if (o.has("x")) p.moveTo(o.getDouble("x").toFloat(), o.getDouble("y").toFloat())
+        else { p.moveTo(o.getDouble("x1").toFloat(), o.getDouble("y1").toFloat()); p.lineTo(o.getDouble("x2").toFloat(), o.getDouble("y2").toFloat()) }
+        return p
+    }
+
+    // Multi-touch/accessibility macro gesture. Strokes with same d/start run simultaneously.
+    // Supports tap/hold: {x,y,ms,d}; swipe: {x1,y1,x2,y2,ms,d}; path: {points:[[x,y],...],ms,d}.
+    fun gestures(spec: String): Boolean {
+        val a = JSONArray(spec); val b = GestureDescription.Builder()
+        for (i in 0 until minOf(a.length(), 128)) {
+            val o = a.getJSONObject(i)
+            b.addStroke(GestureDescription.StrokeDescription(pathFrom(o), maxOf(0, o.optLong("d", o.optLong("start", 0))), maxOf(1, o.optLong("ms", o.optLong("duration", 35)))))
+        }
+        return dispatchGesture(b.build(), null, null)
+    }
+
+    fun global(action: Int) = performGlobalAction(action)
+    fun back() = performGlobalAction(GLOBAL_ACTION_BACK)
+    fun home() = performGlobalAction(GLOBAL_ACTION_HOME)
+    fun recents() = performGlobalAction(GLOBAL_ACTION_RECENTS)
+    fun notifications() = performGlobalAction(GLOBAL_ACTION_NOTIFICATIONS)
+    fun quickSettings() = if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 17) performGlobalAction(GLOBAL_ACTION_QUICK_SETTINGS) else false
+
+    fun currentPackage(): String = rootInActiveWindow?.packageName?.toString() ?: ""
+    fun waitFor(q: String, ms: Long): Boolean {
+        val end = System.currentTimeMillis() + maxOf(0L, ms)
+        do { if (findNode(rootInActiveWindow, q.lowercase()) != null) return true; Thread.sleep(80) } while (System.currentTimeMillis() < end)
+        return false
+    }
+
+    fun readScreen(): String {
+        val root = rootInActiveWindow ?: return "[]"
+        val out = JSONArray(); traverse(root, out, "", 400)
+        return out.toString()
+    }
+
+    fun nodes(q: String, limit: Int): String {
+        val root = rootInActiveWindow ?: return "[]"
+        val out = JSONArray(); findAll(root, q.lowercase(), out, minOf(limit, 300))
+        return out.toString()
+    }
+
+    fun click(q: String): Boolean = act(q, AccessibilityNodeInfo.ACTION_CLICK)
+    fun longClick(q: String): Boolean = act(q, AccessibilityNodeInfo.ACTION_LONG_CLICK)
+    fun focus(q: String): Boolean = act(q, AccessibilityNodeInfo.ACTION_FOCUS)
+
+    fun input(q: String, text: String): Boolean {
+        val n = findNode(rootInActiveWindow, q.lowercase()) ?: return false
+        val args = Bundle(); args.putCharSequence(AccessibilityNodeInfo.ACTION_ARGUMENT_SET_TEXT_CHARSEQUENCE, text)
+        return n.performAction(AccessibilityNodeInfo.ACTION_SET_TEXT, args)
+    }
+
+    fun act(q: String, action: Int): Boolean {
+        var n = findNode(rootInActiveWindow, q.lowercase()) ?: return false
+        var cur: AccessibilityNodeInfo? = n
+        while (cur != null) {
+            if (cur.performAction(action)) return true
+            cur = cur.parent
+        }
+        return false
+    }
+
+    fun findNode(n: AccessibilityNodeInfo?, q: String): AccessibilityNodeInfo? {
+        if (n == null) return null
+        if (matches(n, q)) return n
+        for (i in 0 until n.childCount) findNode(n.getChild(i), q)?.let { return it }
+        return null
+    }
+
+    fun findAll(n: AccessibilityNodeInfo?, q: String, out: JSONArray, limit: Int) {
+        if (n == null || out.length() >= limit) return
+        if (matches(n, q)) out.put(nodeJson(n))
+        for (i in 0 until n.childCount) findAll(n.getChild(i), q, out, limit)
+    }
+
+    fun matches(n: AccessibilityNodeInfo, q: String): Boolean {
+        if (q.isBlank() || q == "*") return true
+        val hay = ((n.text ?: "").toString() + "\n" + (n.contentDescription ?: "").toString() + "\n" + (n.viewIdResourceName ?: "") + "\n" + (n.className ?: "").toString()).lowercase()
+        return hay.contains(q)
+    }
+
+    fun nodeJson(n: AccessibilityNodeInfo): JSONObject {
+        val r = Rect(); n.getBoundsInScreen(r)
+        return JSONObject().apply {
+            put("text", n.text?.toString() ?: ""); put("desc", n.contentDescription?.toString() ?: ""); put("id", n.viewIdResourceName ?: "")
+            put("class", n.className?.toString() ?: ""); put("pkg", n.packageName?.toString() ?: "")
+            put("x", r.centerX()); put("y", r.centerY()); put("l", r.left); put("t", r.top); put("r", r.right); put("b", r.bottom)
+            put("click", n.isClickable); put("long", n.isLongClickable); put("edit", n.isEditable); put("enabled", n.isEnabled); put("checked", n.isChecked); put("focus", n.isFocused)
+        }
+    }
+
+    fun traverse(n: AccessibilityNodeInfo?, out: JSONArray, path: String, limit: Int) {
+        if (n == null || out.length() >= limit) return
+        val j = nodeJson(n); j.put("path", path)
+        val hasData = j.optString("text").isNotEmpty() || j.optString("desc").isNotEmpty() || j.optString("id").isNotEmpty() || j.optBoolean("click") || j.optBoolean("edit")
+        if (hasData) out.put(j)
+        for (i in 0 until n.childCount) traverse(n.getChild(i), out, if (path.isEmpty()) "$i" else "$path.$i", limit)
+    }
+}
+
+
+class BridgeService : Service() {
+    companion object { @JvmField var instance: BridgeService? = null }
+    override fun onBind(intent: Intent?): IBinder? = null
+    override fun onCreate() { super.onCreate(); instance = this; foreground("HTML Realizer bridge active") }
+    override fun onDestroy() { instance = null; super.onDestroy() }
+    override fun onStartCommand(intent: Intent?, flags: Int, startId: Int): Int { foreground(intent?.getStringExtra("text") ?: "HTML Realizer bridge active"); return START_STICKY }
+    fun foreground(text: String) {
+        val ch = "K_fg"; val nm = getSystemService(Context.NOTIFICATION_SERVICE) as android.app.NotificationManager
+        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 26) nm.createNotificationChannel(android.app.NotificationChannel(ch, "HTML Realizer", android.app.NotificationManager.IMPORTANCE_LOW))
+        val pi = android.app.PendingIntent.getActivity(this, 1, Intent(this, Main::class.java), android.app.PendingIntent.FLAG_UPDATE_CURRENT or if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 23) android.app.PendingIntent.FLAG_IMMUTABLE else 0)
+        val icon = if (applicationInfo.icon != 0) applicationInfo.icon else android.R.drawable.ic_dialog_info
+        val nb = if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 26) android.app.Notification.Builder(this, ch) else android.app.Notification.Builder(this)
+        startForeground(7, nb.setSmallIcon(icon).setContentTitle("HTML Realizer").setContentText(text).setContentIntent(pi).setOngoing(true).build())
+    }
+}

+class NotifService : NotificationListenerService() {
+    companion object {
+        @JvmField val EQ = ConcurrentLinkedQueue<String>()
+        fun push(o: JSONObject) { o.put("ts", System.currentTimeMillis()); EQ.offer(o.toString()); while (EQ.size > 500) EQ.poll() }
+        fun events(max: Int): String { val a = JSONArray(); var n = 0; while (n < max) { val x = EQ.poll() ?: break; a.put(JSONObject(x)); n++ }; return a.toString() }
+    }
+    override fun onNotificationPosted(sbn: StatusBarNotification?) { if (sbn != null) push(JSONObject().put("op", "post").put("pkg", sbn.packageName).put("id", sbn.id).put("tag", sbn.tag ?: "").put("title", sbn.notification.extras?.getCharSequence("android.title")?.toString() ?: "").put("text", sbn.notification.extras?.getCharSequence("android.text")?.toString() ?: "")) }
+    override fun onNotificationRemoved(sbn: StatusBarNotification?) { if (sbn != null) push(JSONObject().put("op", "rm").put("pkg", sbn.packageName).put("id", sbn.id).put("tag", sbn.tag ?: "")) }
+}
+
+class MainReceiver : BroadcastReceiver() {
+    override fun onReceive(context: Context, intent: Intent?) {
+        val i = Intent(context, Main::class.java).addFlags(Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK)
+        intent?.extras?.keySet()?.forEach { k -> i.putExtra(k, intent.extras?.get(k)?.toString() ?: "") }
+        context.startActivity(i)
+    }
+}

+App Realizer snapshot numbering
+===============================
+
+/1  Original/live snapshot. Keep unchanged.
+/2  Preserved proposal state from when /2 was introduced for code suggestions. Keep frozen unless explicitly requested.
+/3  Milestone saved from /0 when testable mirror overlay HTML existed; includes test_html/ with that HTML.
+/4  Manifest-first/resource-introspection milestone copied from /0 and enhanced with generic resources/caps/roots/permission/stop paths.
+/0  Always-latest continuous working state. Future iterative edits should happen here first unless user asks for a milestone.
+/5+ Future milestones only when explicitly declared by the user (copy /0 or requested state to that number at that time).
+
+Current rule:
+- Do not mutate /1.
+- Do not mutate /2.
+- Do not mutate /3.
+- Treat /4 as a milestone for manifest-first/fractal kernel work.
+- Use /0 for ongoing latest work unless the user asks to work in a milestone.

+function buildDebugReport(full=false){

+async function bitmapRegion(bitmap,left,top,width,height){

+  finally{safeNativeCall(crop,'recycle',[]);safeNativeCall(output,'close',[]);dropToken(bytes);dropToken(output);dropToken(crop)}

+function showPreview(region,marks=[]){

+function locateRing(region,circle,expectedX,expectedY){
+  log(60,1);const {width,height,data}=region.imageData,mask=new Uint8Array(width*height),queue=new Int32Array(width*height),redPixel=index=>data[index]>=190&&data[index+1]<=135&&data[index+2]<=150&&data[index]-data[index+1]>=70&&data[index]-data[index+2]>=55&&data[index+3]>=120;let redCount=0;
+  for(let pixel=0,index=0;pixel<mask.length;pixel++,index+=4)if(redPixel(index)){mask[pixel]=1;redCount++}const expectedLocalX=expectedX-region.x,expectedLocalY=expectedY-region.y,diameter=circle.radius*2,candidates=[];log(60,2);
+  for(let seed=0;seed<mask.length;seed++){if(mask[seed]!==1)continue;let head=0,tail=0,count=0,minX=width,maxX=-1,minY=height,maxY=-1;queue[tail++]=seed;mask[seed]=2;
+    while(head<tail){const pixel=queue[head++],x=pixel%width,y=(pixel/width)|0;count++;if(x<minX)minX=x;if(x>maxX)maxX=x;if(y<minY)minY=y;if(y>maxY)maxY=y;const neighbours=[pixel-1,pixel+1,pixel-width,pixel+width];for(let n=0;n<4;n++){const next=neighbours[n];if(next<0||next>=mask.length||mask[next]!==1)continue;if((n===0&&x===0)||(n===1&&x===width-1))continue;mask[next]=2;queue[tail++]=next}}
+    const boxWidth=maxX-minX+1,boxHeight=maxY-minY+1;if(count<Math.max(24,circle.radius*2)||boxWidth<circle.radius||boxHeight<circle.radius)continue;const centerX=(minX+maxX)/2,centerY=(minY+maxY)/2,sizeError=(Math.abs(boxWidth-diameter)+Math.abs(boxHeight-diameter))/diameter,roundError=Math.abs(boxWidth-boxHeight)/diameter,distance=Math.hypot(centerX-expectedLocalX,centerY-expectedLocalY)/Math.max(circle.radius,1),density=count/(boxWidth*boxHeight),densityError=Math.abs(density-0.16)/0.16,score=1-(sizeError*0.34+roundError*0.18+Math.min(distance,4)*0.18+Math.min(densityError,3)*0.12);candidates.push({centerX,centerY,boxWidth,boxHeight,count,score})
+  }
+  candidates.sort((a,b)=>b.score-a.score);const best=candidates[0];if(!best||best.score<0.30){log(60,3);return null}const located={x:region.x+best.centerX,y:region.y+best.centerY,radius:circle.radius,visualRadius:(best.boxWidth+best.boxHeight)/4,confidence:Math.max(0,Math.min(1,best.score)),redPixels:best.count,redCount};log(60,4);return located;
+}
+
+function cropImageData(source,left,top,width,height){
+  log(96,1);left=Math.max(0,Math.min(source.width-1,Math.floor(left)));top=Math.max(0,Math.min(source.height-1,Math.floor(top)));width=Math.max(1,Math.min(source.width-left,Math.ceil(width)));height=Math.max(1,Math.min(source.height-top,Math.ceil(height)));const data=new Uint8ClampedArray(width*height*4);for(let row=0;row<height;row++){const from=((top+row)*source.width+left)*4;data.set(source.data.subarray(from,from+width*4),row*width*4)}log(96,2);return {x:left,y:top,width,height,imageData:new ImageData(data,width,height)};
+}

+function locateLiveCircles(thumbnail){
+  log(97,1);const detections=[],scaleRadius=(thumbnail.scaleX+thumbnail.scaleY)/2;for(const circle of state.circles){const expected=centerFor(circle,false),expectedX=expected.x*thumbnail.scaleX,expectedY=expected.y*thumbnail.scaleY,scaledRadius=Math.max(4,circle.radius*scaleRadius),half=Math.ceil(scaledRadius*2.45+10),region=cropImageData(thumbnail.imageData,expectedX-half,expectedY-half,half*2,half*2),located=locateRing(region,{radius:scaledRadius},expectedX,expectedY);
+    if(located){const actual={x:located.x/thumbnail.scaleX,y:located.y/thumbnail.scaleY,radius:circle.radius,visualRadius:located.visualRadius/scaleRadius,confidence:located.confidence,redPixels:located.redPixels,revision:circle.revision,at:new Date().toISOString()};circle.calibrated=actual;detections.push({circle,expected,actual,preview:{x:located.x,y:located.y,radius:located.visualRadius}});trace('LIVE RING FOUND',{id:circle.id,x:actual.x,y:actual.y,confidence:actual.confidence})}
+    else detections.push({circle,expected,actual:null,preview:{x:expectedX,y:expectedY,radius:scaledRadius}});
+  }state.live.found=detections.filter(item=>item.actual).length;if(state.live.found){state.live.lastFeedbackAt=new Date().toISOString();ui.feedbackTime.textContent=state.live.lastFeedbackAt.slice(11,23)}renderPositions();log(97,2);return detections;
+}

+function drawLiveFrame(thumbnail,detections=[]){
+  log(98,1);if(!liveContext)return;ui.livePreview.width=thumbnail.width;ui.livePreview.height=thumbnail.height;liveContext.putImageData(thumbnail.imageData,0,0);let red=0,green=0,blue=0;const pixels=thumbnail.imageData.data;for(let index=0;index<pixels.length;index+=4){red+=pixels[index];green+=pixels[index+1];blue+=pixels[index+2]}
+  liveContext.lineWidth=2;liveContext.font='bold 12px sans-serif';for(const detection of detections){const mark=detection.preview;if(detection.actual){liveContext.strokeStyle='#28e9ff';liveContext.fillStyle='#28e9ff';liveContext.beginPath();liveContext.arc(mark.x,mark.y,mark.radius,0,Math.PI*2);liveContext.stroke();const expectedX=detection.expected.x*thumbnail.scaleX,expectedY=detection.expected.y*thumbnail.scaleY;liveContext.beginPath();liveContext.moveTo(expectedX,expectedY);liveContext.lineTo(mark.x,mark.y);liveContext.stroke();liveContext.fillText('#'+detection.circle.id+' '+Math.round(detection.actual.confidence*100)+'%',mark.x+5,mark.y-5)}else{liveContext.strokeStyle='#ffd35a';liveContext.setLineDash([5,4]);liveContext.beginPath();liveContext.arc(mark.x,mark.y,mark.radius,0,Math.PI*2);liveContext.stroke();liveContext.setLineDash([]);liveContext.fillStyle='#ffd35a';liveContext.fillText('#'+detection.circle.id+' not found',mark.x+5,mark.y-5)}}
+  const now=performance.now(),delta=state.live.lastFrameAt?now-state.live.lastFrameAt:0;state.live.lastFrameAt=now;state.live.frames++;ui.liveDimensions.textContent=thumbnail.fullWidth+' × '+thumbnail.fullHeight+' / '+thumbnail.width+' × '+thumbnail.height;ui.liveRate.textContent=(delta?(1000/delta).toFixed(2):'—')+' fps';ui.liveRgb.textContent=[red,green,blue].map(formatInteger).join(' / ');renderState();log(98,2);
+}
+
+function liveIntervalValue(){log(99,1);return Math.max(1100,Math.min(5000,Number(ui.liveInterval.value)||1200))}
+function livePreviewWidth(){log(100,1);return Math.max(240,Math.min(900,Number(ui.previewWidth.value)||500))}

+function scheduleLive(delayMs=0){
+  log(101,1);clearTimeout(state.live.timer);state.live.timer=0;if(state.live.enabled)state.live.timer=setTimeout(()=>captureLiveFrame().catch(fail),Math.max(0,delayMs));

+async function captureLiveFrame(){
+  log(102,1);if(!state.live.enabled)return;if(state.live.busy||state.busy||state.captureOwner){scheduleLive(120);log(102,2);return}state.live.busy=true;const started=performance.now();try{await prepareNative();if(state.ringLayer)await syncOverlayState();await withCapture('LIVE',async()=>{let bitmap;try{bitmap=await captureBitmap('CURRENT');const thumbnail=await bitmapThumbnail(bitmap,livePreviewWidth()),detections=state.live.feedback&&state.overlayMode!=='HIDDEN'?locateLiveCircles(thumbnail):[];if(state.live.feedback&&state.overlayMode==='HIDDEN')state.live.found=0;drawLiveFrame(thumbnail,detections)}finally{safeNativeCall(bitmap,'recycle',[]);dropToken(bitmap)}});trace('LIVE FRAME',{frame:state.live.frames,found:state.live.found,elapsed:performance.now()-started})}
+  catch(error){state.lastError=error.message||String(error);trace('LIVE FRAME FAILED',error.stack||String(error));setStatus('Live view failed: '+state.lastError,true)}
+  finally{state.live.busy=false;renderState();if(state.live.enabled)scheduleLive(Math.max(0,liveIntervalValue()-(performance.now()-started)));log(102,3)}
+}

+async function setLiveView(enabled){
+  log(103,1);enabled=!!enabled;clearTimeout(state.live.timer);state.live.timer=0;if(!enabled){state.live.enabled=false;state.live.feedback=false;setStatus('LIVE VIEW OFF. No automatic screenshots are scheduled.');renderState();updateControllerText();log(103,2);return}
+  await prepareNative();state.live.enabled=true;state.live.lastStartedAt=Date.now();setStatus('LIVE VIEW ON. Capturing downscaled screen frames; exact circle reads remain full resolution.');renderState();updateControllerText();scheduleLive(0);log(103,3);

+async function setSelfFeedback(enabled){

Live view state is unchanged.');renderState();updateControllerText();if(enabled)scheduleLive(0);log(104,2);

+async function calibrateTargets(targets){
+  log(61,1);if(!targets.length)throw new Error('No circles to calibrate');await prepareNative();if(state.ringLayer)await syncOverlayState();return withCapture('CALIBRATE',async()=>{const bitmap=await captureBitmap('LOCATOR');let success=0;try{
+    for(const circle of targets){const expected=centerFor(circle,false),half=Math.ceil(circle.radius*2.25+dp(18)),region=await bitmapRegion(bitmap,expected.x-half,expected.y-half,half*2,half*2),located=locateRing(region,circle,expected.x,expected.y);if(located){circle.calibrated={...located,revision:circle.revision,at:new Date().toISOString()};success++;trace('CIRCLE LOCATED',{id:circle.id,...located})}else{circle.calibrated=null;trace('CIRCLE NOT LOCATED',{id:circle.id,expected})}showPreview(region,located?[{x:located.x,y:located.y,radius:located.radius,label:'#'+circle.id+' '+(located.confidence*100).toFixed(0)+'%'}]:[{x:expected.x,y:expected.y,radius:circle.radius,label:'#'+circle.id+' expected',color:'#ffd35a'}])}
+  }finally{safeNativeCall(bitmap,'recycle',[]);dropToken(bitmap)}renderState();renderPositions();setStatus('Visual calibration found '+success+' of '+targets.length+' ring'+(targets.length===1?'':'s')+'.');log(61,2);return success});

+async function calibrateAll(){
+  log(62,1);if(state.busy){log(62,2);return}if(!state.circles.length){setStatus('Spawn at least one circle first.',true);return}state.busy=true;renderState();try{await calibrateTargets([...state.circles])}finally{state.busy=false;renderState()}log(62,3);

+function centerFor(circle,allowCalibration=true){
+  log(63,1);const calibrated=allowCalibration&&circle.calibrated&&circle.calibrated.revision===circle.revision?circle.calibrated:null;return calibrated?{x:calibrated.x,y:calibrated.y,radius:calibrated.radius,source:'visual'}:{x:circle.x+circle.radius,y:circle.y+circle.radius,radius:circle.radius,source:'layout estimate'};
+}
+
+function accumulator(){log(64,1);return {pixels:0,red:0,green:0,blue:0,min:[255,255,255],max:[0,0,0],hist:[new Uint32Array(16),new Uint32Array(16),new Uint32Array(16)]}}
+
+function addPixel(target,red,green,blue){
+  log(65,1);target.pixels++;target.red+=red;target.green+=green;target.blue+=blue;const values=[red,green,blue];for(let channel=0;channel<3;channel++){if(values[channel]<target.min[channel])target.min[channel]=values[channel];if(values[channel]>target.max[channel])target.max[channel]=values[channel];target.hist[channel][values[channel]>>4]++}
+}
+
+function finishAccumulator(target){
+  log(66,1);const total=target.red+target.green+target.blue,pixels=target.pixels||1;return {pixels:target.pixels,sum:[target.red,target.green,target.blue],average:[target.red/pixels,target.green/pixels,target.blue/pixels],share:total?[target.red/total,target.green/total,target.blue/total]:[0,0,0],min:target.min,max:target.max,hist:target.hist.map(channel=>Array.from(channel))};
+}
+
+function analyzeCircle(imageData,centerX,centerY,radius){
+  log(67,1);const core=accumulator(),outer=accumulator(),annulus=accumulator(),r2=radius*radius,r4=4*r2,{width,height,data}=imageData;for(let y=0;y<height;y++){const dy=y+0.5-centerY;for(let x=0;x<width;x++){const dx=x+0.5-centerX,distance=dx*dx+dy*dy;if(distance>r4)continue;const index=(y*width+x)*4,red=data[index],green=data[index+1],blue=data[index+2];addPixel(outer,red,green,blue);if(distance<=r2)addPixel(core,red,green,blue);else addPixel(annulus,red,green,blue)}}log(67,2);return {core:finishAccumulator(core),outer:finishAccumulator(outer),annulus:finishAccumulator(annulus)};
+}
+
+async function readCircle(circle,clickAfter=false){
+  log(68,1);return withCapture(clickAfter?'READ_CLICK':'READ',async()=>{const center=centerFor(circle),bitmap=await captureBitmap('CLEAN',clickAfter?()=>bridgeAsync(state.native.service,'tap',[center.x,center.y]):null);try{const margin=dp(3),diameter=center.radius*4,region=await bitmapRegion(bitmap,center.x-center.radius*2-margin,center.y-center.radius*2-margin,diameter+margin*2,diameter+margin*2),analysis=analyzeCircle(region.imageData,center.x-region.x,center.y-region.y,center.radius),result={at:new Date().toISOString(),center:{x:center.x,y:center.y,radius:center.radius,source:center.source},...analysis,clicked:clickAfter};circle.result=result;state.lastResult={circleId:circle.id,...result};showPreview(region,[{x:center.x,y:center.y,radius:center.radius,label:'#'+circle.id+' r'},{x:center.x,y:center.y,radius:center.radius*2,label:'2r',color:'#ffd35a'}]);renderResults();trace('RGB READ',{circle:circle.id,center:result.center,core:result.core,outer:result.outer,clicked:clickAfter});log(68,2);return result}finally{safeNativeCall(bitmap,'recycle',[]);dropToken(bitmap)}});
+}
+
+async function clickCircle(circle){
+  log(69,1);return withCapture('CLICK',async()=>{const center=centerFor(circle);await temporaryPresentation('CLEAN');await delay(90);try{await bridgeAsync(state.native.service,'tap',[center.x,center.y]);trace('TAP',{circle:circle.id,x:center.x,y:center.y,source:center.source});await delay(90)}finally{await restorePresentation()}log(69,2);return center});
+}

+async function runAction(kind){
+  log(70,1);if(state.busy){log(70,2);return}if(state.ringLayer)await syncOverlayState();const circle=selectedCircle();if(!circle){setStatus('Select or spawn a circle first.',true);return}state.busy=true;ui.action.textContent=kind;renderState();try{await prepareNative();const hasFreshCalibration=circle.calibrated?.revision===circle.revision;if(state.overlayMode!=='HIDDEN'&&!hasFreshCalibration){setStatus(`Visually locating circle ${circle.id} before ${kind}…`);await calibrateTargets([circle])}
+    if(kind==='READ'){setStatus(`Reading RGB inside circle ${circle.id} at r and 2r…`);await readCircle(circle,false);setStatus(`Circle ${circle.id} RGB read complete.`)}
+    else if(kind==='CLICK'){setStatus(`Explicitly clicking circle ${circle.id}…`);const center=await clickCircle(circle);setStatus(`Clicked circle ${circle.id} at ${Math.round(center.x)}, ${Math.round(center.y)} using ${center.source}.`)}
+    else{setStatus(`Reading circle ${circle.id}, then clicking the same captured location…`);await readCircle(circle,true);setStatus(`Circle ${circle.id} was read and then clicked.`)}
+  }finally{state.busy=false;ui.action.textContent='idle';renderState()}log(70,3);
+}

+function formatInteger(value){log(71,1);return Math.round(Number(value)||0).toLocaleString('en-US').replace(/,/g,' ')}
+function histogramText(stats){log(73,1);const labels=Array.from({length:16},(_,index)=>`${index*16}-${index*16+15}`);return ['R','G','B'].map((channel,index)=>`${channel}: ${labels.map((label,bin)=>`${label}:${stats.hist[index][bin]}`).join(' | ')}`).join('\n')}
+
+function statsHtml(title,stats){
+  log(74,1);return `<div><span>${title} pixels</span><b>${formatInteger(stats.pixels)}</b></div><div><span>Average R / G / B</span><b><i class="r">${stats.average[0].toFixed(1)}</i> / <i class="g">${stats.average[1].toFixed(1)}</i> / <i class="b">${stats.average[2].toFixed(1)}</i></b></div><div><span>Channel share R / G / B</span><b>${stats.share.map(value=>(value*100).toFixed(2)+'%').join(' / ')}</b></div><div><span>Sum R / G / B</span><b>${stats.sum.map(formatInteger).join(' / ')}</b></div><div><span>Min R / G / B</span><b>${stats.min.join(' / ')}</b></div><div><span>Max R / G / B</span><b>${stats.max.join(' / ')}</b></div>`;
+}

+function renderResults(){

+    "function bounds(radius){return {maxX:Math.max(0,innerWidth-radius*2),maxY:Math.max(0,innerHeight-radius*2)}}",
+    "function clamp(circle){var limit=bounds(circle.radius);circle.left=Math.max(0,Math.min(limit.maxX,Number(circle.left)||0));circle.top=Math.max(0,Math.min(limit.maxY,Number(circle.top)||0))}",
+    "function paint(circle){clamp(circle);var node=document.getElementById('ring-'+circle.id);if(!node){node=document.createElement('div');node.id='ring-'+circle.id;node.className='ring';node.dataset.id=circle.id;node.innerHTML='<b>'+circle.id+'</b>';host.appendChild(node)}node.style.left=circle.left+'px';node.style.top=circle.top+'px';node.style.width=circle.radius*2+'px';node.style.height=circle.radius*2+'px';node.classList.toggle('selected',circle.id===selectedId)}",
+    "function select(id){selectedId=Number(id);circles.forEach(paint)}",
+    "function setState(value){var incoming=new Set;value.circles.forEach(function(raw){var circle={id:Number(raw.id),left:Number(raw.left),top:Number(raw.top),radius:Number(raw.radius)};incoming.add(circle.id);circles.set(circle.id,circle)});Array.from(circles.keys()).forEach(function(id){if(!incoming.has(id)){circles.delete(id);var node=document.getElementById('ring-'+id);if(node)node.remove()}});selectedId=value.selectedId==null?selectedId:Number(value.selectedId);if(!circles.has(selectedId))selectedId=circles.size?Array.from(circles.keys()).pop():null;document.body.dataset.mode=value.mode||document.body.dataset.mode||'HIDDEN';circles.forEach(paint);return true}",
+    "function exportState(){return JSON.stringify({dpr:devicePixelRatio||1,width:innerWidth,height:innerHeight,selectedId:selectedId,circles:Array.from(circles.values()).map(function(circle){return {id:circle.id,left:circle.left,top:circle.top,radius:circle.radius}})})}",
+    "function setMode(mode){document.body.dataset.mode=mode;return mode}",
+    "addEventListener('pointerdown',function(event){var node=event.target.closest&&event.target.closest('.ring');if(!node||document.body.dataset.mode!=='PLACE')return;event.preventDefault();var circle=circles.get(Number(node.dataset.id));select(circle.id);drag={id:circle.id,pointer:event.pointerId,dx:event.clientX-circle.left,dy:event.clientY-circle.top};node.setPointerCapture&&node.setPointerCapture(event.pointerId)},{passive:false});",
+    "addEventListener('pointermove',function(event){if(!drag||event.pointerId!==drag.pointer||document.body.dataset.mode!=='PLACE')return;event.preventDefault();var circle=circles.get(drag.id);circle.left=event.clientX-drag.dx;circle.top=event.clientY-drag.dy;paint(circle)},{passive:false});",
+    "function end(event){if(!drag||event.pointerId!==drag.pointer)return;event.preventDefault();var circle=circles.get(drag.id);if(circle)paint(circle);drag=null}",
+    "addEventListener('pointerup',end,{passive:false});addEventListener('pointercancel',end,{passive:false});addEventListener('resize',function(){circles.forEach(paint)});",
+    "window.overlayApi={setState:setState,exportState:exportState,setMode:setMode};document.body.dataset.mode='HIDDEN';",
+    '})();<\/script>'
+  ].join('');

+async function evaluateOverlay(code,timeoutMs=12000){

+function decodeImage(base64,mime='image/png'){
+  log(57,1);return new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{const image=new Image();image.onload=()=>{log(57,2);try{const canvas=document.createElement('canvas');canvas.width=image.naturalWidth;canvas.height=image.naturalHeight;const context=canvas.getContext('2d',{willReadFrequently:true,colorSpace:'srgb'});context.drawImage(image,0,0);resolve({imageData:context.getImageData(0,0,canvas.width,canvas.height),canvas})}catch(error){log(57,3);reject(error)}};image.onerror=()=>{log(57,4);reject(new Error('Native '+mime+' image could not be decoded'))};image.src='data:'+mime+';base64,'+base64});
+}
+
+function decodePng(base64){log(94,1);return decodeImage(base64,'image/png')}
+
+async function bitmapRegion(bitmap,left,top,width,height){
+  log(58,1);const bitmapWidth=Number(nativeValue(nativeCall(bitmap,'getWidth',[]))),bitmapHeight=Number(nativeValue(nativeCall(bitmap,'getHeight',[]))),x=Math.max(0,Math.min(bitmapWidth-1,Math.floor(left))),y=Math.max(0,Math.min(bitmapHeight-1,Math.floor(top))),w=Math.max(1,Math.min(bitmapWidth-x,Math.ceil(width))),h=Math.max(1,Math.min(bitmapHeight-y,Math.ceil(height)));let crop,output,bytes;
+  try{crop=nativeCall(state.native.bitmapClass,'createBitmap',[bitmap,x,y,w,h]);output=nativeNew('java.io.ByteArrayOutputStream',[]);const compressed=nativeCall(crop,'compress',[state.native.png,100,output]);if(compressed!=='Vtrue')throw new Error('Crop PNG compression failed');bytes=nativeCall(output,'toByteArray',[]);const encoded=nativeCall(state.native.base64Class,'encodeToString',[bytes,2]),decoded=await decodePng(nativeValue(encoded));log(58,2);return {...decoded,x,y,width:w,height:h}}
+  finally{safeNativeCall(crop,'recycle',[]);safeNativeCall(output,'close',[]);dropToken(bytes);dropToken(output);dropToken(crop)}
+}
+
+async function bitmapThumbnail(bitmap,maxWidth){
+  log(95,1);const fullWidth=Number(nativeValue(nativeCall(bitmap,'getWidth',[]))),fullHeight=Number(nativeValue(nativeCall(bitmap,'getHeight',[]))),scale=Math.min(1,Math.max(0.1,Number(maxWidth)||500)/fullWidth),width=Math.max(1,Math.round(fullWidth*scale)),height=Math.max(1,Math.round(fullHeight*scale));let scaled,output,bytes;
+  try{const source=scale<.999?(scaled=nativeCall(state.native.bitmapClass,'createScaledBitmap',[bitmap,width,height,true])):bitmap;output=nativeNew('java.io.ByteArrayOutputStream',[]);const compressed=nativeCall(source,'compress',[state.native.jpeg,72,output]);if(compressed!=='Vtrue')throw new Error('Live JPEG compression failed');bytes=nativeCall(output,'toByteArray',[]);const encoded=nativeCall(state.native.base64Class,'encodeToString',[bytes,2]),decoded=await decodeImage(nativeValue(encoded),'image/jpeg');log(95,2);return {...decoded,fullWidth,fullHeight,width,height,scaleX:width/fullWidth,scaleY:height/fullHeight}}
+  finally{safeNativeCall(scaled,'recycle',[]);safeNativeCall(output,'close',[]);dropToken(bytes);dropToken(output);dropToken(scaled)}
+}
+
+function showPreview(region,marks=[]){
+  log(59,1);if(!previewContext)return;ui.preview.width=region.width;ui.preview.height=region.height;previewContext.putImageData(region.imageData,0,0);previewContext.lineWidth=Math.max(2,Math.round(state.density*2));previewContext.font=`${Math.max(12,Math.round(state.density*11))}px sans-serif`;for(const mark of marks){previewContext.strokeStyle=mark.color||'#38e7ff';previewContext.fillStyle=mark.color||'#38e7ff';previewContext.beginPath();previewContext.arc(mark.x-region.x,mark.y-region.y,mark.radius,0,Math.PI*2);previewContext.stroke();previewContext.fillText(mark.label||'',mark.x-region.x+5,mark.y-region.y-5)}log(59,2);
+}
+
+function locateRing(region,circle,expectedX,expectedY){
+  log(60,1);const {width,height,data}=region.imageData,mask=new Uint8Array(width*height),queue=new Int32Array(width*height),redPixel=index=>data[index]>=190&&data[index+1]<=135&&data[index+2]<=150&&data[index]-data[index+1]>=70&&data[index]-data[index+2]>=55&&data[index+3]>=120;let redCount=0;
+  for(let pixel=0,index=0;pixel<mask.length;pixel++,index+=4)if(redPixel(index)){mask[pixel]=1;redCount++}const expectedLocalX=expectedX-region.x,expectedLocalY=expectedY-region.y,diameter=circle.radius*2,candidates=[];log(60,2);
+  for(let seed=0;seed<mask.length;seed++){if(mask[seed]!==1)continue;let head=0,tail=0,count=0,minX=width,maxX=-1,minY=height,maxY=-1;queue[tail++]=seed;mask[seed]=2;
+    while(head<tail){const pixel=queue[head++],x=pixel%width,y=(pixel/width)|0;count++;if(x<minX)minX=x;if(x>maxX)maxX=x;if(y<minY)minY=y;if(y>maxY)maxY=y;const neighbours=[pixel-1,pixel+1,pixel-width,pixel+width];for(let n=0;n<4;n++){const next=neighbours[n];if(next<0||next>=mask.length||mask[next]!==1)continue;if((n===0&&x===0)||(n===1&&x===width-1))continue;mask[next]=2;queue[tail++]=next}}
+    const boxWidth=maxX-minX+1,boxHeight=maxY-minY+1;if(count<Math.max(24,circle.radius*2)||boxWidth<circle.radius||boxHeight<circle.radius)continue;const centerX=(minX+maxX)/2,centerY=(minY+maxY)/2,sizeError=(Math.abs(boxWidth-diameter)+Math.abs(boxHeight-diameter))/diameter,roundError=Math.abs(boxWidth-boxHeight)/diameter,distance=Math.hypot(centerX-expectedLocalX,centerY-expectedLocalY)/Math.max(circle.radius,1),density=count/(boxWidth*boxHeight),densityError=Math.abs(density-0.16)/0.16,score=1-(sizeError*0.34+roundError*0.18+Math.min(distance,4)*0.18+Math.min(densityError,3)*0.12);candidates.push({centerX,centerY,boxWidth,boxHeight,count,score})
+  }
+  candidates.sort((a,b)=>b.score-a.score);const best=candidates[0];if(!best||best.score<0.30){log(60,3);return null}const located={x:region.x+best.centerX,y:region.y+best.centerY,radius:circle.radius,visualRadius:(best.boxWidth+best.boxHeight)/4,confidence:Math.max(0,Math.min(1,best.score)),redPixels:best.count,redCount};log(60,4);return located;
+}
+
+function cropImageData(source,left,top,width,height){
+  log(96,1);left=Math.max(0,Math.min(source.width-1,Math.floor(left)));top=Math.max(0,Math.min(source.height-1,Math.floor(top)));width=Math.max(1,Math.min(source.width-left,Math.ceil(width)));height=Math.max(1,Math.min(source.height-top,Math.ceil(height)));const data=new Uint8ClampedArray(width*height*4);for(let row=0;row<height;row++){const from=((top+row)*source.width+left)*4;data.set(source.data.subarray(from,from+width*4),row*width*4)}log(96,2);return {x:left,y:top,width,height,imageData:new ImageData(data,width,height)};
+}

+function locateLiveCircles(thumbnail){
+  log(97,1);const detections=[],scaleRadius=(thumbnail.scaleX+thumbnail.scaleY)/2;for(const circle of state.circles){const expected=centerFor(circle,false),expectedX=expected.x*thumbnail.scaleX,expectedY=expected.y*thumbnail.scaleY,scaledRadius=Math.max(4,circle.radius*scaleRadius),half=Math.ceil(scaledRadius*2.45+10),region=cropImageData(thumbnail.imageData,expectedX-half,expectedY-half,half*2,half*2),located=locateRing(region,{radius:scaledRadius},expectedX,expectedY);
+    if(located){const actual={x:located.x/thumbnail.scaleX,y:located.y/thumbnail.scaleY,radius:circle.radius,visualRadius:located.visualRadius/scaleRadius,confidence:located.confidence,redPixels:located.redPixels,revision:circle.revision,at:new Date().toISOString()};circle.calibrated=actual;detections.push({circle,expected,actual,preview:{x:located.x,y:located.y,radius:located.visualRadius}});trace('LIVE RING FOUND',{id:circle.id,x:actual.x,y:actual.y,confidence:actual.confidence})}
+    else detections.push({circle,expected,actual:null,preview:{x:expectedX,y:expectedY,radius:scaledRadius}});
+  }state.live.found=detections.filter(item=>item.actual).length;if(state.live.found){state.live.lastFeedbackAt=new Date().toISOString();ui.feedbackTime.textContent=state.live.lastFeedbackAt.slice(11,23)}renderPositions();log(97,2);return detections;
+}
