+function drawLiveFrame(thumbnail,detections=[]){
+  log(98,1);if(!liveContext)return;ui.livePreview.width=thumbnail.width;ui.livePreview.height=thumbnail.height;liveContext.putImageData(thumbnail.imageData,0,0);let red=0,green=0,blue=0;const pixels=thumbnail.imageData.data;for(let index=0;index<pixels.length;index+=4){red+=pixels[index];green+=pixels[index+1];blue+=pixels[index+2]}
+  liveContext.lineWidth=2;liveContext.font='bold 12px sans-serif';for(const detection of detections){const mark=detection.preview;if(detection.actual){liveContext.strokeStyle='#28e9ff';liveContext.fillStyle='#28e9ff';liveContext.beginPath();liveContext.arc(mark.x,mark.y,mark.radius,0,Math.PI*2);liveContext.stroke();const expectedX=detection.expected.x*thumbnail.scaleX,expectedY=detection.expected.y*thumbnail.scaleY;liveContext.beginPath();liveContext.moveTo(expectedX,expectedY);liveContext.lineTo(mark.x,mark.y);liveContext.stroke();liveContext.fillText('#'+detection.circle.id+' '+Math.round(detection.actual.confidence*100)+'%',mark.x+5,mark.y-5)}else{liveContext.strokeStyle='#ffd35a';liveContext.setLineDash([5,4]);liveContext.beginPath();liveContext.arc(mark.x,mark.y,mark.radius,0,Math.PI*2);liveContext.stroke();liveContext.setLineDash([]);liveContext.fillStyle='#ffd35a';liveContext.fillText('#'+detection.circle.id+' not found',mark.x+5,mark.y-5)}}
+  const now=performance.now(),delta=state.live.lastFrameAt?now-state.live.lastFrameAt:0;state.live.lastFrameAt=now;state.live.frames++;ui.liveDimensions.textContent=thumbnail.fullWidth+' × '+thumbnail.fullHeight+' / '+thumbnail.width+' × '+thumbnail.height;ui.liveRate.textContent=(delta?(1000/delta).toFixed(2):'—')+' fps';ui.liveRgb.textContent=[red,green,blue].map(formatInteger).join(' / ');renderState();log(98,2);
+}

+function liveIntervalValue(){log(99,1);return Math.max(1100,Math.min(5000,Number(ui.liveInterval.value)||1200))}
+function livePreviewWidth(){log(100,1);return Math.max(240,Math.min(900,Number(ui.previewWidth.value)||500))}

+function scheduleLive(delayMs=0){
+  log(101,1);clearTimeout(state.live.timer);state.live.timer=0;if(state.live.enabled)state.live.timer=setTimeout(()=>captureLiveFrame().catch(fail),Math.max(0,delayMs));

+async function captureLiveFrame(){
+  log(102,1);if(!state.live.enabled)return;if(state.live.busy||state.busy||state.captureOwner){scheduleLive(120);log(102,2);return}state.live.busy=true;const started=performance.now();try{await prepareNative();if(state.ringLayer)await syncOverlayState();await withCapture('LIVE',async()=>{let bitmap;try{bitmap=await captureBitmap('CURRENT');const thumbnail=await bitmapThumbnail(bitmap,livePreviewWidth()),detections=state.live.feedback&&state.overlayMode!=='HIDDEN'?locateLiveCircles(thumbnail):[];if(state.live.feedback&&state.overlayMode==='HIDDEN')state.live.found=0;drawLiveFrame(thumbnail,detections)}finally{safeNativeCall(bitmap,'recycle',[]);dropToken(bitmap)}});trace('LIVE FRAME',{frame:state.live.frames,found:state.live.found,elapsed:performance.now()-started})}
+  catch(error){state.lastError=error.message||String(error);trace('LIVE FRAME FAILED',error.stack||String(error));setStatus('Live view failed: '+state.lastError,true)}
+  finally{state.live.busy=false;renderState();if(state.live.enabled)scheduleLive(Math.max(0,liveIntervalValue()-(performance.now()-started)));log(102,3)}
+}

+  await prepareNative();state.live.enabled=true;state.live.lastStartedAt=Date.now();setStatus('LIVE VIEW ON. Capturing downscaled screen frames; exact circle reads remain full resolution.');renderState();updateControllerText();scheduleLive(0);log(103,3);
+}

+async function setSelfFeedback(enabled){

Live view state is unchanged.');renderState();updateControllerText();if(enabled)scheduleLive(0);log(104,2);

+async function calibrateTargets(targets){
+  log(61,1);if(!targets.length)throw new Error('No circles to calibrate');await prepareNative();if(state.ringLayer)await syncOverlayState();return withCapture('CALIBRATE',async()=>{const bitmap=await captureBitmap('LOCATOR');let success=0;try{
+    for(const circle of targets){const expected=centerFor(circle,false),half=Math.ceil(circle.radius*2.25+dp(18)),region=await bitmapRegion(bitmap,expected.x-half,expected.y-half,half*2,half*2),located=locateRing(region,circle,expected.x,expected.y);if(located){circle.calibrated={...located,revision:circle.revision,at:new Date().toISOString()};success++;trace('CIRCLE LOCATED',{id:circle.id,...located})}else{circle.calibrated=null;trace('CIRCLE NOT LOCATED',{id:circle.id,expected})}showPreview(region,located?[{x:located.x,y:located.y,radius:located.radius,label:'#'+circle.id+' '+(located.confidence*100).toFixed(0)+'%'}]:[{x:expected.x,y:expected.y,radius:circle.radius,label:'#'+circle.id+' expected',color:'#ffd35a'}])}
+  }finally{safeNativeCall(bitmap,'recycle',[]);dropToken(bitmap)}renderState();renderPositions();setStatus('Visual calibration found '+success+' of '+targets.length+' ring'+(targets.length===1?'':'s')+'.');log(61,2);return success});
+}

+async function calibrateAll(){
+  log(62,1);if(state.busy){log(62,2);return}if(!state.circles.length){setStatus('Spawn at least one circle first.',true);return}state.busy=true;renderState();try{await calibrateTargets([...state.circles])}finally{state.busy=false;renderState()}log(62,3);

+function centerFor(circle,allowCalibration=true){
+  log(63,1);const calibrated=allowCalibration&&circle.calibrated&&circle.calibrated.revision===circle.revision?circle.calibrated:null;return calibrated?{x:calibrated.x,y:calibrated.y,radius:calibrated.radius,source:'visual'}:{x:circle.x+circle.radius,y:circle.y+circle.radius,radius:circle.radius,source:'layout estimate'};
+}
+
+function accumulator(){log(64,1);return {pixels:0,red:0,green:0,blue:0,min:[255,255,255],max:[0,0,0],hist:[new Uint32Array(16),new Uint32Array(16),new Uint32Array(16)]}}
+
+function addPixel(target,red,green,blue){
+  log(65,1);target.pixels++;target.red+=red;target.green+=green;target.blue+=blue;const values=[red,green,blue];for(let channel=0;channel<3;channel++){if(values[channel]<target.min[channel])target.min[channel]=values[channel];if(values[channel]>target.max[channel])target.max[channel]=values[channel];target.hist[channel][values[channel]>>4]++}
+}
+
+function finishAccumulator(target){
+  log(66,1);const total=target.red+target.green+target.blue,pixels=target.pixels||1;return {pixels:target.pixels,sum:[target.red,target.green,target.blue],average:[target.red/pixels,target.green/pixels,target.blue/pixels],share:total?[target.red/total,target.green/total,target.blue/total]:[0,0,0],min:target.min,max:target.max,hist:target.hist.map(channel=>Array.from(channel))};
+}
+
+function analyzeCircle(imageData,centerX,centerY,radius){
+  log(67,1);const core=accumulator(),outer=accumulator(),annulus=accumulator(),r2=radius*radius,r4=4*r2,{width,height,data}=imageData;for(let y=0;y<height;y++){const dy=y+0.5-centerY;for(let x=0;x<width;x++){const dx=x+0.5-centerX,distance=dx*dx+dy*dy;if(distance>r4)continue;const index=(y*width+x)*4,red=data[index],green=data[index+1],blue=data[index+2];addPixel(outer,red,green,blue);if(distance<=r2)addPixel(core,red,green,blue);else addPixel(annulus,red,green,blue)}}log(67,2);return {core:finishAccumulator(core),outer:finishAccumulator(outer),annulus:finishAccumulator(annulus)};
+}
+
+async function readCircle(circle,clickAfter=false){
+  log(68,1);return withCapture(clickAfter?'READ_CLICK':'READ',async()=>{const center=centerFor(circle),bitmap=await captureBitmap('CLEAN',clickAfter?()=>bridgeAsync(state.native.service,'tap',[center.x,center.y]):null);try{const margin=dp(3),diameter=center.radius*4,region=await bitmapRegion(bitmap,center.x-center.radius*2-margin,center.y-center.radius*2-margin,diameter+margin*2,diameter+margin*2),analysis=analyzeCircle(region.imageData,center.x-region.x,center.y-region.y,center.radius),result={at:new Date().toISOString(),center:{x:center.x,y:center.y,radius:center.radius,source:center.source},...analysis,clicked:clickAfter};circle.result=result;state.lastResult={circleId:circle.id,...result};showPreview(region,[{x:center.x,y:center.y,radius:center.radius,label:'#'+circle.id+' r'},{x:center.x,y:center.y,radius:center.radius*2,label:'2r',color:'#ffd35a'}]);renderResults();trace('RGB READ',{circle:circle.id,center:result.center,core:result.core,outer:result.outer,clicked:clickAfter});log(68,2);return result}finally{safeNativeCall(bitmap,'recycle',[]);dropToken(bitmap)}});
+}
+
+async function clickCircle(circle){
+  log(69,1);return withCapture('CLICK',async()=>{const center=centerFor(circle);await temporaryPresentation('CLEAN');await delay(90);try{await bridgeAsync(state.native.service,'tap',[center.x,center.y]);trace('TAP',{circle:circle.id,x:center.x,y:center.y,source:center.source});await delay(90)}finally{await restorePresentation()}log(69,2);return center});
+}

+async function runAction(kind){
+  log(70,1);if(state.busy){log(70,2);return}if(state.ringLayer)await syncOverlayState();const circle=selectedCircle();if(!circle){setStatus('Select or spawn a circle first.',true);return}state.busy=true;ui.action.textContent=kind;renderState();try{await prepareNative();const hasFreshCalibration=circle.calibrated?.revision===circle.revision;if(state.overlayMode!=='HIDDEN'&&!hasFreshCalibration){setStatus(`Visually locating circle ${circle.id} before ${kind}…`);await calibrateTargets([circle])}
+    if(kind==='READ'){setStatus(`Reading RGB inside circle ${circle.id} at r and 2r…`);await readCircle(circle,false);setStatus(`Circle ${circle.id} RGB read complete.`)}
+    else if(kind==='CLICK'){setStatus(`Explicitly clicking circle ${circle.id}…`);const center=await clickCircle(circle);setStatus(`Clicked circle ${circle.id} at ${Math.round(center.x)}, ${Math.round(center.y)} using ${center.source}.`)}
+    else{setStatus(`Reading circle ${circle.id}, then clicking the same captured location…`);await readCircle(circle,true);setStatus(`Circle ${circle.id} was read and then clicked.`)}
+  }finally{state.busy=false;ui.action.textContent='idle';renderState()}log(70,3);
+}

+function formatInteger(value){log(71,1);return Math.round(Number(value)||0).toLocaleString('en-US').replace(/,/g,' ')}
+function histogramText(stats){log(73,1);const labels=Array.from({length:16},(_,index)=>`${index*16}-${index*16+15}`);return ['R','G','B'].map((channel,index)=>`${channel}: ${labels.map((label,bin)=>`${label}:${stats.hist[index][bin]}`).join(' | ')}`).join('\n')}
+
+function statsHtml(title,stats){
+  log(74,1);return `<div><span>${title} pixels</span><b>${formatInteger(stats.pixels)}</b></div><div><span>Average R / G / B</span><b><i class="r">${stats.average[0].toFixed(1)}</i> / <i class="g">${stats.average[1].toFixed(1)}</i> / <i class="b">${stats.average[2].toFixed(1)}</i></b></div><div><span>Channel share R / G / B</span><b>${stats.share.map(value=>(value*100).toFixed(2)+'%').join(' / ')}</b></div><div><span>Sum R / G / B</span><b>${stats.sum.map(formatInteger).join(' / ')}</b></div><div><span>Min R / G / B</span><b>${stats.min.join(' / ')}</b></div><div><span>Max R / G / B</span><b>${stats.max.join(' / ')}</b></div>`;
+}

+function renderResults(){
+  log(75,1);const withResults=state.circles.filter(circle=>circle.result);if(!withResults.length){ui.results.innerHTML='<p>No RGB reading yet.</p>';log(75,2);return}ui.results.innerHTML=withResults.map(circle=>{const result=circle.result,center=result.center;return `<article class="result"><h3>Circle ${circle.id}${result.clicked?' · read then clicked':''}</h3><p>Center ${center.x.toFixed(1)}, ${center.y.toFixed(1)} · radius ${center.radius.toFixed(1)} px · ${center.source} · ${result.at}</p><div class="stats">${statsHtml('r core',result.core)}</div><details><summary>2r full circle</summary><div class="stats">${statsHtml('2r',result.outer)}</div></details><details><summary>r → 2r annulus</summary><div class="stats">${statsHtml('annulus',result.annulus)}</div></details><details><summary>16-bin RGB histograms</summary><pre>CORE\n${histogramText(result.core)}\n\n2R\n${histogramText(result.outer)}\n\nANNULUS\n${histogramText(result.annulus)}</pre></details></article>`}).join('');log(75,3);
+}

+function renderPositions(){
+  log(105,1);if(!state.circles.length){ui.positions.innerHTML='<p>No circles yet.</p>';return}ui.positions.innerHTML=state.circles.map(circle=>{const layout=centerFor(circle,false),visual=circle.calibrated&&circle.calibrated.revision===circle.revision?circle.calibrated:null,delta=visual?' · Δ '+(visual.x-layout.x).toFixed(1)+', '+(visual.y-layout.y).toFixed(1)+' px · '+(visual.confidence*100).toFixed(0)+'%':' · not visually found';return '<div class="position"><strong>#'+circle.id+'</strong><span>Layout center / radius</span><b>'+layout.x.toFixed(1)+', '+layout.y.toFixed(1)+' / '+layout.radius.toFixed(1)+' px</b><span>Screenshot feedback</span><b>'+(visual?visual.x.toFixed(1)+', '+visual.y.toFixed(1):'—')+delta+'</b></div>'}).join('');log(105,2);

+function updateControllerButton(key,handle,text,color){

+function updateControllerText(){
+  log(76,1);if(!state.controller)return;const selected=state.selectedId==null?'—':'#'+state.selectedId,text='MODE '+state.overlayMode+' · '+state.circles.length+' · '+selected+' · L'+(state.live.enabled?'ON':'OFF')+' · S'+(state.live.feedback?'ON':'OFF');if(state.controller.lastText!==text){state.controller.lastText=text;bridgeAsync(state.controller.status,'setText',[text]).catch(error=>{log(76,2);trace('CONTROLLER TEXT FAILED',error.message)})}updateControllerButton('lastLive',state.controller.liveButton,state.live.enabled?'LIVE ON':'LIVE OFF',state.live.enabled?0xff138759:0xff394251);updateControllerButton('lastFeedback',state.controller.feedbackButton,state.live.feedback?'SELF ON':'SELF OFF',state.live.feedback?0xff8a5b16:0xff394251);
+}
+
+function renderState(){
+  log(77,1);ui.mode.textContent=state.overlayMode;ui.count.textContent=String(state.circles.length);ui.selected.textContent=state.selectedId==null?'none':'#'+state.selectedId;ui.action.textContent=state.busy?(ui.action.textContent==='idle'?'busy':ui.action.textContent):(state.captureOwner||'idle');ui.liveState.textContent=state.live.enabled?'ON':'OFF';ui.feedbackState.textContent=state.live.feedback?(state.overlayMode==='HIDDEN'?'ON · paused':'ON'):'OFF';ui.frameState.textContent=String(state.live.frames);ui.foundState.textContent=state.live.found+' / '+state.circles.length;const disabled=state.busy;[ui.start,ui.add,ui.remove,ui.accessibility,ui.calibrate,ui.place,ui.visual,ui.read,ui.click,ui.both].forEach(button=>button.disabled=disabled);ui.destroy.disabled=false;ui.hidden.disabled=false;ui.remove.disabled=disabled||!state.circles.length;ui.calibrate.disabled=disabled||!state.circles.length;ui.read.disabled=disabled||!state.circles.length;ui.click.disabled=disabled||!state.circles.length;ui.both.disabled=disabled||!state.circles.length;ui.liveOn.disabled=disabled||state.live.enabled;ui.liveOff.disabled=!state.live.enabled;ui.feedbackOn.disabled=disabled||state.live.feedback;ui.feedbackOff.disabled=!state.live.feedback;updateControllerText();scheduleDebug();log(77,2);
+}
+
+async function openAccessibilitySettings(){
+  log(78,1);await allowNative();const intent=nativeNew('android.content.Intent',['android.settings.ACCESSIBILITY_SETTINGS']);try{await bridgeAsync(0,'startActivity',[intent]);setStatus('Accessibility settings opened. Enable HTML Realizer Service, then return.')}finally{dropToken(intent)}log(78,2);
+}
+
+function fail(error){
+  log(79,1);const message=error?.message||String(error);state.lastError=message;trace('FAIL',error?.stack||message);setStatus(message,true);state.busy=false;ui.action.textContent='idle';renderState();console.error(error);log(79,2);
+}
+
+ui.start.addEventListener('click',()=>startOverlay().catch(fail));
+ui.add.addEventListener('click',()=>spawnCircle().catch(fail));
+ui.remove.addEventListener('click',()=>removeSelectedCircle().catch(fail));
+ui.destroy.addEventListener('click',()=>{try{destroyOverlaysNow()}catch(error){trace('DESTROY NOW FAILED',error.message)}destroyOverlays().catch(fail)});
+ui.place.addEventListener('click',()=>setOverlayMode('PLACE').catch(fail));
+ui.visual.addEventListener('click',()=>setOverlayMode('VISUAL').catch(fail));
+ui.hidden.addEventListener('click',()=>setOverlayMode('HIDDEN').catch(fail));
+ui.liveOn.addEventListener('click',()=>setLiveView(true).catch(fail));
+ui.liveOff.addEventListener('click',()=>setLiveView(false).catch(fail));
+ui.feedbackOn.addEventListener('click',()=>setSelfFeedback(true).catch(fail));
+ui.feedbackOff.addEventListener('click',()=>setSelfFeedback(false).catch(fail));
+ui.calibrate.addEventListener('click',()=>calibrateAll().catch(fail));
+ui.read.addEventListener('click',()=>runAction('READ').catch(fail));
+ui.click.addEventListener('click',()=>runAction('CLICK').catch(fail));
+ui.both.addEventListener('click',()=>runAction('READ_CLICK').catch(fail));
+ui.accessibility.addEventListener('click',()=>openAccessibilitySettings().catch(fail));
+ui.copy.addEventListener('click',()=>copyDebug().catch(fail));
+ui.save.addEventListener('click',saveDebug);
+addEventListener('pagehide',()=>{log(80,1);state.live.enabled=false;clearTimeout(state.live.timer);destroyOverlaysNow()});
+addEventListener('beforeunload',()=>{log(80,2);state.live.enabled=false;clearTimeout(state.live.timer);destroyOverlaysNow()});
+addEventListener('error',event=>fail(event.error||new Error(`${event.message} at ${event.filename}:${event.lineno}:${event.colno}`)));
+addEventListener('unhandledrejection',event=>fail(event.reason||new Error('Unhandled promise rejection')));
